# CLAUDE.md

> Repo renamed from zimage.swift to comfybox on 2026-05-08. Package and executable are now "ComfyBox"; the ZImage Swift module name is unchanged.

This file provides guidance to Claude Code (claude.ai/code) when working with code in this repository.

## Project Overview

ComfyBox (formerly Z-Image.swift) is a Swift port of [Z-Image-Turbo](https://huggingface.co/Tongyi-MAI/Z-Image-Turbo) using mlx-swift for Apple Silicon. It provides a CLI tool and library for text-to-image generation with support for LoRA and ControlNet.

## Build Commands

```bash
# Build release CLI binary
xcodebuild -scheme ComfyBox -configuration Release -destination 'platform=macOS' -derivedDataPath .build/xcode

# Run all tests (use -enableCodeCoverage NO to avoid creating default.profraw)
xcodebuild test -scheme comfybox-Package -destination 'platform=macOS' -enableCodeCoverage NO

# Run specific test target
xcodebuild test -scheme comfybox-Package -destination 'platform=macOS' -enableCodeCoverage NO -only-testing:ZImageTests

# Run a single test class
xcodebuild test -scheme comfybox-Package -destination 'platform=macOS' -enableCodeCoverage NO -only-testing:ZImageTests/FlowMatchSchedulerTests

# Run a single test method
xcodebuild test -scheme comfybox-Package -destination 'platform=macOS' -enableCodeCoverage NO -only-testing:ZImageTests/FlowMatchSchedulerTests/testTimestepsDecreasing
```

## Architecture

### Core Components

**Application Layer** (`Sources/ComfyBox`):
- `ComfyBox`: Entry point for generation, controlnet, and quantization commands. Handles argument parsing and global GPU settings.

**Pipeline Layer** (`Sources/ZImage/Pipeline`):
- `ZImagePipeline`: Orchestrates Text-to-Image generation. Manages dynamic model loading/unloading (phase-scoped lifetimes), LoRA application, and the denoising loop.
- `ZImageControlPipeline`: Extends generation with ControlNet conditioning (image/mask inputs) and Inpainting.
- `FlowMatchScheduler`: Implements Flow Matching Euler scheduler with "Dynamic Shifting" for resolution-dependent noise schedules.

**Model Layer** (`Sources/ZImage/Model`):
- **TextEncoder**: Qwen-based transformer. Acts as both an Encoder (for conditioning) and a Generator (for Prompt Enhancement via LLM).
- **Transformer**: The Diffusion Transformer (DiT).
  - `ZImageTransformer2DModel`: Hybrid architecture with separate "refiner" streams (Noise & Context) that merge into joint attention blocks.
  - `ZImageControlTransformer2DModel`: Adds ControlNet blocks to the base DiT architecture.
- **VAE**: `AutoencoderKL` for encoding/decoding images to/from latents. Supports `AutoencoderDecoderOnly` for inference optimization.

**Infrastructure** (`Sources/ZImage/Weights`, `/Quantization`, `/LoRA`):
- **Weights**: Handles downloading from Hugging Face (`HubSnapshot`), parsing `.safetensors`, and detecting AIO checkpoints.
- **Quantization**: `ZImageQuantizer` supports 4-bit and 8-bit group-wise quantization (Affine/MXFP4) for reduced memory footprint.
- **LoRA**: `LoRAApplicator` supports both baked-in and dynamic (runtime) adapters, including LoKr (Kronecker product) and quantization compatibility.

### Key Data Flow

1. **Enhancement (Optional)**: Text prompt -> QwenTextEncoder (LLM Mode) -> Enhanced Prompt.
2. **Encoding**: Enhanced Prompt -> QwenTokenizer -> QwenTextEncoder -> Text Embeddings.
3. **Initialization**: Random Gaussian Latents generated.
4. **Denoising Loop**:
   - Latents + Timestep + Text Embeddings -> `ZImageTransformer2D` (Refiners -> Joint Blocks) -> Noise Prediction.
   - `FlowMatchEulerScheduler` updates latents based on prediction.
5. **Decoding**: Refined Latents -> `AutoencoderKL` (Decoder) -> RGB Image.

### Test Structure

- `Tests/ZImageTests/` - Unit tests (scheduler, config parsing, image I/O).
- `Tests/ZImageIntegrationTests/` - Integration tests requiring model weights (pipeline, ControlNet, LoRA).
- `Tests/ZImageE2ETests/` - End-to-end CLI tests (builds and runs the actual binary).

## Requirements

- macOS 14.0+ / iOS 16+
- Apple Silicon
- Swift 5.9+

  1640	  /// Auto-stage: rewrite bare LoRA filenames that only exist on nearline
  1641	  /// storage to their freshly staged local paths, so a preset (or any swap
  1642	  /// request) can reference archived LoRAs and they appear on demand.
  1643	  private func stageNearlineLoras(in payload: LoRASwapPayload) -> LoRASwapPayload {
  1644	    let entries = payload.loras.map { entry -> LoRAEntry in
  1645	      // Only bare safetensors filenames are candidates — absolute/relative
  1646	      // paths and HF ids resolve through the normal machinery.
  1647	      guard !entry.path.hasPrefix("/"), !entry.path.hasPrefix("~"), !entry.path.hasPrefix("."),
  1648	            entry.path.hasSuffix(".safetensors"),
  1649	            !FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: (entry.path as NSString).expandingTildeInPath),
  1650	            nearlineLibrary.item(named: entry.path) != nil
  1651	      else { return entry }
  1652	      guard let staged = try? nearlineLibrary.stage(name: entry.path) else { return entry }
  1653	      logger.info("Nearline: auto-staged \(entry.path) for LoRA swap")
  1654	      return LoRAEntry(path: staged, scale: entry.scale)
  1655	    }
  1656	    return LoRASwapPayload(loras: entries)
  1657	  }

  1661	  /// Body for the local LTX-2 video route (snake_case over the wire).
  1662	  /// Decode a base64 image (image_base64) to a temp PNG and return its path, so
  1663	  /// remote clients can send an init image without a pre-existing server file.
  1664	  /// Returns nil when the string is absent/undecodable.
  1665	  private static func writeTempImage(base64: String?) -> String? {
  1666	    guard let base64, let data = Data(base64Encoded: base64) else { return nil }
  1667	    let path = NSTemporaryDirectory() + "zimage-vidinit-\(UUID().uuidString).png"
  1668	    return (try? data.write(to: URL(fileURLWithPath: path))) != nil ? path : nil
  1669	  }

  1671	  private struct LocalVideoRequest: Decodable {
  1672	    let prompt: String
  1673	    let negativePrompt: String?
  1674	    let imagePath: String?
  1675	    /// I2V init image sent as base64 (image_base64) for remote clients.
  1676	    let imageBase64: String?
  1677	    let width: Int?
  1678	    let height: Int?
  1679	    let frames: Int?
  1680	    let steps: Int?
  1681	    let seed: UInt64?
  1682	    let strength: Float?
  1683	    /// Conditioning compression (libx264 CRF) override for THIS render — the
  1684	    /// daemon sends a higher value (more motion) for partnered-action prompts
  1685	    /// and a low value (fidelity) for solo/portrait. nil = env default.
  1686	    let imgCompression: Int?
  1687	    /// CFG guidance override (>1 amplifies action; motion recipe sends 2.0 for
  1688	    /// partnered-action, omits for solo=fidelity). nil = env/config default.
  1689	    let guidance: Float?
  1690	    let extendToSeconds: Float?
  1691	    /// Target duration in seconds — the daemon/MCP vocabulary. For local
  1692	    /// renders this maps onto `extendToSeconds` (chunked continuation, each
  1693	    /// chunk re-anchored on the previous chunk\u{27}s last frame) when it
  1694	    /// exceeds one chunk. `extend_to_seconds` still wins when both are set.
  1695	    let duration: Float?
  1696	    /// Identity re-anchor strength for continuation chunks (0 disables).
  1697	    /// Default 0.5 for extended renders \u{2014} counters per-chunk subject drift.
  1698	    let identityAnchorStrength: Float?
  1699	    let fps: Int?
  1700	    let loraPath: String?
  1701	    let loraStrength: Float?
  1702	    /// Multiple LoRAs, applied in order — same {path, scale} shape as image
  1703	    /// LoRA requests. `loraPath`/`loraStrength` still work for a single LoRA.
  1704	    let loras: [LoRAEntry]?
  1705	    let outputPath: String?
  1706	    /// Which client/app submitted this job (desktop, bree, api…) — surfaced in
  1707	    /// the async job status and /health, same as image `GeneratePayload.source`.
  1708	    let source: String?
  1709	    /// Tier A tuning overrides (snake_case JSON via decoder strategy).
  1710	    let tuning: LTX2VideoTuning?
  1711	    /// Server-minted id from /v1/enhance binding this render to its
  1712	    /// optimization lineage (task #19, finding #6).
  1713	    let optimizationAttemptId: String?
  1714	    /// Optional preset id resolved from the shared PresetStore (mediaKind
  1715	    /// "video"): LoRAs, prompt prefix/suffix, negative prompt, dims budget,
  1716	    /// steps, seed. Explicit request fields always override preset values.
  1717	    let preset: String?
  1718	    /// Character whose canonical description is prepended to the prompt so the
  1719	    /// subject renders on-model. For T2V (no init image) this is the ONLY
  1720	    /// identity source; defaults to "kira" when unset. For I2V the init image
  1721	    /// already carries identity, so it's injected only when explicitly named.
  1722	    let character: String?
  1723	    /// Content mode (neutral/apple/banana/avocado) gating the character's
  1724	    /// mode-specific description addendum. Defaults to the server's current mode.
  1725	    let contentMode: String?
  1726	    /// Auto-enhance the prompt through the configured prompt-optimization
  1727	    /// provider (Dan's-PE via LM Studio) before encoding. Default on when a
  1728	    /// provider is configured; set false to send the raw prompt.
  1729	    let enhance: Bool?
  1730	    /// Named resolution budget: "480p" | "720p" | "1080p". Maps to a
  1731	    /// width x height pixel budget when explicit width/height are absent
  1732	    /// (previously this key was silently DROPPED on the local path).
  1733	    let resolution: String?
  1734	    /// Aspect ratio for the resolution budget: "16:9" (default) or "9:16".
  1735	    /// For I2V the source image's aspect still wins (budget only).
  1736	    let aspectRatio: String?
  1737	    /// Generate synchronized audio (task #21). T2V single-chunk only in v1;
  1738	    /// first audio render reloads the transformer with the audio branch.
  1739	    let audio: Bool?
  1740	  }
  1741	
  1742	  /// Map a named resolution + aspect to a width x height budget. Dims are
  1743	  /// budgets, not finals — the existing /64 snapping and I2V source-aspect
  1744	  /// derivation still apply downstream.
  1745	  private static func videoDims(resolution: String?, aspectRatio: String?) -> (width: Int, height: Int)? {
  1746	    guard let res = resolution?.lowercased() else { return nil }
  1747	    let landscape: (Int, Int)
  1748	    switch res {
  1749	    case "480p": landscape = (832, 480)
  1750	    case "720p": landscape = (1280, 720)
  1751	    case "1080p": landscape = (1920, 1080)
  1752	    default: return nil
  1753	    }
  1754	    let portrait = (aspectRatio ?? "16:9") == "9:16"
  1755	    return portrait ? (landscape.1, landscape.0) : landscape
  1756	  }

  1758	  private struct LocalVideoResponse: Encodable {
  1759	    let success: Bool
  1760	    let outputPath: String
  1910	          let areaErr = abs(area - budget) / budget
  1911	          if let b = best {
  1912	            let better = aspectErr < b.aspectErr - 1e-9
  1913	              || (abs(aspectErr - b.aspectErr) <= 1e-9 && areaErr < b.areaErr)
  1914	            if better { best = (w, h, aspectErr, areaErr) }
  1915	          } else {
  1916	            best = (w, h, aspectErr, areaErr)
  1917	          }
  1918	        }
  1919	      }
  1920	      return best
  1921	    }
  1922	
  1923	    // Prefer staying near the budget; but at small budgets the 256 floor pins one
  1924	    // axis and the tight cap can force a badly stretched pair (a halved two-stage
  1925	    // budget hit 19% that way), so allow a larger clip rather than distort.
  1926	    var pick = search(areaCap: 1.25)
  1927	    if pick == nil || pick!.aspectErr > 0.03, let relaxed = search(areaCap: 1.6),
  1928	       relaxed.aspectErr < (pick?.aspectErr ?? .infinity) - 1e-9 {
  1929	      pick = relaxed
  1930	    }
  1931	    guard let chosen = pick else {
  1932	      return (snapDim64(Int(idealW.rounded())), snapDim64(Int(idealH.rounded())))
  1933	    }
  1934	    return (chosen.w, chosen.h)
  1935	  }
  1936	
  1937	  /// Pixel dimensions of an image file without decoding the bitmap.
  1938	  static func imagePixelSize(atPath path: String) -> (width: Int, height: Int)? {
  1939	    let url = URL(fileURLWithPath: (path as NSString).expandingTildeInPath)
  1940	    guard let source = CGImageSourceCreateWithURL(url as CFURL, nil),
  1941	          let props = CGImageSourceCopyPropertiesAtIndex(source, 0, nil) as? [CFString: Any],
  1942	          let width = props[kCGImagePropertyPixelWidth] as? Int,
  1943	          let height = props[kCGImagePropertyPixelHeight] as? Int
  1944	    else { return nil }
  1945	    return (width, height)
  1946	  }
  1947	
  1948	  /// Map an LTX-2 (chunk, step) progress tick to an overall 0-100 percent across
  1949	  /// all chunks. Pure so it can be unit-tested and reused by both local paths.
  1950	  static func localVideoProgressPercent(chunk: Int, totalChunks: Int, step: Int, totalSteps: Int) -> Int {
  1951	    let chunks = max(1, totalChunks)
  1952	    let steps = max(1, totalSteps)
  1953	    let done = max(0, chunk) * steps + max(0, step)
  1954	    let total = chunks * steps
  1955	    return min(100, max(0, Int((Double(done) / Double(total)) * 100.0)))
  1956	  }

  1958	  /// Resolve LTX-2 weights, build + validate the render request. Returns nil when
  1959	  /// local LTX-2 isn't configured (caller falls through to Replicate); throws for
  1960	  /// a malformed request or invalid output path.
  1961	  private func prepareLocalVideo(body: Data) async throws -> PreparedLocalVideo? {
  1962	    guard let weights = configuration.ltx2WeightsPath, let gemma = configuration.ltx2GemmaPath else {
  1963	      return nil
  1964	    }
  1965	    let req = try decode(LocalVideoRequest.self, from: body)
  1966	
  1967	    // Video presets — same PresetStore as images (mediaKind "video"). A
  1968	    // preset is a named bundle: LoRAs (bare filenames resolve through the
  1969	    // LoRA library search roots), prompt shaping, negative prompt, dims
  1970	    // budget, steps, seed. Explicit request fields win over the preset.
  1971	    var videoPreset: ImagePreset? = nil
  1972	    if let presetId = req.preset, !presetId.isEmpty {
  1973	      guard let found = presetStore.get(presetId) else {
  1974	        throw WarmServerError.invalidRequest(message: "Unknown preset \u{27}\(presetId)\u{27} — see /v1/presets")
  1975	      }
  1976	      videoPreset = found
  1977	      logger.info("LTX-2: applying video preset \u{27}\(presetId)\u{27} (\(found.loras.count) LoRA(s))")
  1978	    }
  1979	
  1980	    // Contain the output within the allowed directory. A relative (or absent)
  1981	    // output path is resolved against the ALLOWED directory, not the process
  1982	    // CWD — under launchd the CWD is the repo checkout, so the old bare-name
  1983	    // default failed its own containment check on every submit (#219).
  1984	    let requestedOutputRaw = req.outputPath ?? "ltx2-\(UUID().uuidString).mp4"
  1985	    let requestedOutput: String
  1986	    if requestedOutputRaw.hasPrefix("/") || requestedOutputRaw.hasPrefix("~") {
  1987	      requestedOutput = requestedOutputRaw
  1988	    } else {
  1989	      requestedOutput = (configuration.allowedOutputDirectory as NSString)
  1990	        .appendingPathComponent(requestedOutputRaw)
  1991	    }
  1992	    let resolvedOutput = try WarmServerOutputPathValidator.resolveOutputPath(
  1993	      requestedOutput, allowedOutputDirectory: configuration.allowedOutputDirectory).path
  1994	
  1995	    let generator: LTX2VideoGenerator
  1996	    if let existing = videoHolder.get() {
  1997	      generator = existing
  1998	    } else {
  1999	      logger.info("LTX-2: resolving weights/text-encoder (downloads on first use if not cached)…")
  2000	      let weightsURL = try await ModelResolution.resolve(
  2001	        modelSpec: weights,
  2002	        filePatterns: ["transformer-distilled.safetensors", "connector.safetensors",
  2003	                        "vae_decoder.safetensors", "vae_encoder.safetensors", "config.json"]
  2004	      )
  2005	      let gemmaURL = try await ModelResolution.resolve(
  2006	        modelSpec: gemma,
  2007	        filePatterns: ["*.safetensors", "*.json", "tokenizer/*", "*.model"]
  2008	      )
  2009	      generator = LTX2VideoGenerator(
  2010	        config: .init(weightsDir: weightsURL.path, gemmaPath: gemmaURL.path), logger: logger)
  2011	    }
  2012	    // Publish into the shared holder so the coordinator can evict it before an
  2013	    // image load, and so the render queue evicts image models before this one
  2014	    // actually loads its ~65GB of weights inside generate() (#218).
  2015	    videoHolder.set(generator)
  2016	
  2017	    // Accept an init image as bytes (image_base64) when no server path is given.
  2018	    let effectiveInitImage = req.imagePath ?? Self.writeTempImage(base64: req.imageBase64)
  2019	
  2020	    var loraEntries: [LoRAEntry] = req.loras ?? []
  2021	    if loraEntries.isEmpty, req.loraPath == nil, let preset = videoPreset, !preset.loras.isEmpty {
  2022	      loraEntries = preset.loras.map { LoRAEntry(path: $0.filename, scale: Float($0.scale)) }
  2023	    }
  2024	    if loraEntries.isEmpty, req.loraPath == nil,
  2025	       let defaultLoRA = configuration.ltx2DefaultLoRA, !defaultLoRA.isEmpty {
  2026	      // "path" or "path@scale"
  2027	      let parts = defaultLoRA.split(separator: "@", maxSplits: 1).map(String.init)
  2028	      let scale = parts.count == 2 ? Float(parts[1]) ?? 1.0 : 1.0
  2029	      loraEntries = [LoRAEntry(path: parts[0], scale: scale)]
  2030	      logger.info("LTX-2: applying default video LoRA \(parts[0]) @ \(scale) (--ltx2-lora)")
  2031	    }
  2032	    let resolvedLoRAs: [LTX2LoRAReference] = try loraEntries.map { entry in
  2033	      let config = try entry.makeConfiguration()
  2034	      guard case .local(let url) = config.source else {
  2035	        throw WarmServerError.invalidRequest(
  2036	          message: "LTX-2 video LoRAs must be local files (got a HuggingFace reference for '\(entry.path)')")
  2037	      }
  2038	      return LTX2LoRAReference(path: url.path, scale: config.scale)
  2039	    }
  2040	
  2041	    // LTX-2 render dims must be divisible by 64: 32-multiples that are not
  2042	    // 64-multiples (e.g. 480) produce progressive haze/ghosting (#219). I2V
  2043	    // output must additionally match the SOURCE image aspect ratio — a fixed
  2044	    // preset like 704x448 applied to a portrait source distorts the
  2045	    // conditioning frame and the render drifts off the image. The requested
  2046	    // width x height is kept only as a pixel-area budget for I2V.
  2047	    // Priority: explicit width/height > named resolution ("720p" etc., FIXED:
  2048	    // previously silently dropped) > preset dims > 704x448 default.
  2049	    let namedDims = Self.videoDims(resolution: req.resolution, aspectRatio: req.aspectRatio)
  2050	    var renderWidth = req.width ?? namedDims?.width ?? videoPreset?.width ?? 704
  2051	    var renderHeight = req.height ?? namedDims?.height ?? videoPreset?.height ?? 448
  2052	    if req.width == nil, let nd = namedDims {
  2053	      logger.info("LTX-2: resolution '\(req.resolution ?? "")' -> \(nd.width)x\(nd.height) budget")
  2054	    }
  2055	    if let initPath = effectiveInitImage,
  2056	       let sourceSize = Self.imagePixelSize(atPath: initPath) {
  2057	      let derived = Self.deriveVideoDims(
  2058	        sourceWidth: sourceSize.width, sourceHeight: sourceSize.height,
  2059	        budgetWidth: renderWidth, budgetHeight: renderHeight)
  2060	      if derived.width != renderWidth || derived.height != renderHeight {
  2061	        logger.info(
  2062	          "LTX-2 I2V: adjusted \(renderWidth)x\(renderHeight) -> \(derived.width)x\(derived.height) (source \(sourceSize.width)x\(sourceSize.height), aspect-matched, /64)")
  2063	        renderWidth = derived.width
  2064	        renderHeight = derived.height
  2065	      }
  2066	    } else {
  2067	      let snappedW = Self.snapDim64(renderWidth)
  2068	      let snappedH = Self.snapDim64(renderHeight)
  2069	      if snappedW != renderWidth || snappedH != renderHeight {
  2070	        logger.info("LTX-2: snapped \(renderWidth)x\(renderHeight) -> \(snappedW)x\(snappedH) (dims must be /64, #219)")
  2071	        renderWidth = snappedW
  2072	        renderHeight = snappedH
  2073	      }
  2074	    }
  2075	    // Two-stage dims convention (2026-08-02): with LTX2_TWO_STAGE=1 the request
  2076	    // dims are the FINAL output size (matching ComfyUI and every caller's
  2077	    // intuition). ComfyBox's pipeline doubles stage-1 dims through the refine,
  2078	    // so hand it the /64-snapped HALF. Without this, enabling two-stage doubled
  2079	    // every clip's output size and the refine-volume gate silently skipped the
  2080	    // refine — the worst of both worlds. Stage-1 /64 keeps the final /128-ish
  2081	    // and matches all validated two-stage renders (stage 1 at 448x256 etc.).
  2082	    // Typed resolution honors request/preset tuning overrides (finding #18):
  2083	    // a request can enable two-stage without the plist knowing.
  2084	    if LTX2ConfigResolver.resolveTyped(request: req.tuning, preset: videoPreset?.videoTuning).twoStage {
  2085	      let s1 = Self.stageOneDims(finalWidth: renderWidth, finalHeight: renderHeight)
  2086	      if s1.halved {
  2087	        logger.info(
  2088	          "LTX-2 two-stage: request dims \(renderWidth)x\(renderHeight) = FINAL; stage 1 paints \(s1.width)x\(s1.height), refine doubles to \(s1.width * 2)x\(s1.height * 2)")
  2089	        renderWidth = s1.width
  2090	        renderHeight = s1.height
  2091	      } else {
  2092	        logger.warning("""
  2093	          LTX-2 two-stage: request \(renderWidth)x\(renderHeight) would paint stage 1 at \
  2094	          \(Self.snapDim64(renderWidth / 2))x\(Self.snapDim64(renderHeight / 2)) — below the \
  2095	          stage-1 floor, which renders SOFT (the refine sharpens, it cannot invent detail). \
  2096	          Treating the request as stage-1 dims instead; output will be \
  2097	          \(renderWidth * 2)x\(renderHeight * 2). Send ~2x larger dims for the intended size.
  2098	          """)
  2099	      }
  2100	    }
  2101	    if let requestedSteps = req.steps, requestedSteps != 8 {
  2102	      logger.warning(
  2103	        "LTX-2: steps=\(requestedSteps) requested, but the distilled pipeline uses a fixed 8-step sigma schedule — the value is currently ignored (#219)")
  2104	    }
  2105	
  2106	    var effectivePrompt = req.prompt
  2107	
  2108	    // Character identity + optional prompt enhancement. For T2V (no init image)
  2109	    // there is no other identity source, so default to "kira" when the caller
  2110	    // names no character. For I2V the init image already carries identity.
  2111	    let isT2V = (effectiveInitImage == nil)
  2112	    let characterName = req.character ?? (isT2V ? "kira" : nil)
  2113	    let charMode = req.contentMode.flatMap { ContentModeManager.Mode(rawValue: $0) } ?? .neutral
  2114	    var characterDesc: String? = nil
  2115	    if let name = characterName,
  2116	       let entry = await characterStore.get(CharacterEntry.slug(name)) {
  2117	      characterDesc = entry.resolvedDescription(for: charMode)
  2118	    }
  2119	
  2120	    // Auto-enhance the video prompt through the configured prompt-optimization
  2121	    // provider (Dan's-PE via LM Studio). The optimizer weaves in the character
  2122	    // description AND enriches the scene, so it replaces the manual character
  2123	    // prepend. Opt out per request with enhance:false; falls back to the manual
  2124	    // prepend when no provider is configured or enhancement fails.
  2125	    var enhancedApplied = false
  2126	    let aiProviderConfig = ComfyBoxServerConfig.loadOrMigrate()
  2127	    if req.enhance != false, let endpoint = aiProviderConfig.providers.promptOptimization {
  2128	      var base = endpoint.baseUrl
  2129	      while base.hasSuffix("/") { base.removeLast() }
  2130	      if base.hasSuffix("/v1") { base = String(base.dropLast(3)) }
  2131	      while base.hasSuffix("/") { base.removeLast() }
  2132	      let optimizer = PromptOptimizer(
  2133	        configuration: PromptOptimizer.Configuration(
  2134	          ollamaBaseURL: base, lmStudioBaseURL: nil, model: endpoint.model,
  2135	          timeoutSeconds: 90, enabled: true),
  2136	        logger: logger)
  2137	      // i2v: motion-only enhancement (the init image fixes subject/scene); t2v: full scene.
  2138	      let result = await optimizer.optimize(
  2139	        prompt: req.prompt, character: characterName,
  2140	        characterDescription: characterDesc, contentMode: charMode.rawValue,
  2141	        mediaKind: isT2V ? "video" : "video-i2v")
  2142	      if result.enhanced {
  2143	        effectivePrompt = result.prompt
  2144	        enhancedApplied = true
  2145	        logger.info("Video: enhanced prompt via \(endpoint.model)\(characterName.map { " (character \($0))" } ?? "").")
  2146	      }
  2147	    }
  2148	
  2149	    // Fallback: manual character prepend when enhancement didn't run/apply.
  2150	    if !enhancedApplied, let name = characterName, let desc = characterDesc, !desc.isEmpty {
  2151	      // Idempotency: skip if the caller already wrote the description in.
  2152	      let alreadyPresent = desc.split(separator: " ").prefix(4).allSatisfy {
  2153	        effectivePrompt.localizedCaseInsensitiveContains($0)
  2154	      }
  2155	      if !alreadyPresent {
  2156	        effectivePrompt = desc + " " + effectivePrompt
  2157	        logger.info("Video: prepended character '\(name)' (mode \(charMode.rawValue)) to prompt.")
  2158	      }
  2159	    }
  2160	
  2161	    if let preset = videoPreset {
  2162	      if let prefix = preset.promptPrefix, !prefix.isEmpty { effectivePrompt = prefix + ", " + effectivePrompt }
  2163	      if let suffix = preset.promptSuffix, !suffix.isEmpty { effectivePrompt = effectivePrompt + ", " + suffix }
  2164	    }
  2165	
  2166	    // Single-pass fold (2026-08-02): continuation chunks DEGENERATE — chunk 2
  2167	    // collapses into fragments (long-known for i2v, which is why the daemon
  2168	    // sends explicit single-pass frames there; observed for T2V today the
  2169	    // moment two-stage went live: chunk 1 clean, chunk 2 psychedelic). The
  2170	    // daemon's rule only covers i2v, so fold ANY duration that fits the
  2171	    // trained window (289f = 12s) into ONE chunk here, t2v included.
  2172	    var foldedFramesPerChunk = req.frames ?? 97
  2173	    var foldedExtendSeconds = req.extendToSeconds
  2174	      ?? Self.extendSecondsFromDuration(req.duration, framesPerChunk: foldedFramesPerChunk, fps: req.fps ?? 24)
  2175	    if foldedExtendSeconds > 0 {
  2176	      let fps = req.fps ?? 24
  2177	      let targetFrames = Int((foldedExtendSeconds * Float(fps)).rounded())
  2178	      if targetFrames <= 289 {
  2179	        let singleFrames = min(289, ((max(targetFrames, 9) - 2) / 8) * 8 + 9)  // 1+8k covering target
  2180	        foldedFramesPerChunk = max(foldedFramesPerChunk, singleFrames)
  2181	        logger.info(
  2182	          "LTX-2: folded \(foldedExtendSeconds)s request into a single \(foldedFramesPerChunk)f chunk (continuation chunks degenerate; ≤289f renders single-pass)")
  2183	        foldedExtendSeconds = 0  // 0 = no continuation chunks
  2184	      }
  2185	    }
  2186	
  2187	    let videoRequest = LTX2VideoRequest(
  2188	      prompt: effectivePrompt,
  2189	      negativePrompt: req.negativePrompt ?? videoPreset?.negativePrompt,
  2190	      initImagePath: effectiveInitImage,
  2191	      width: renderWidth,
  2192	      height: renderHeight,
  2193	      framesPerChunk: foldedFramesPerChunk,
  2194	      steps: req.steps ?? videoPreset?.steps ?? 8,
  2195	      seed: req.seed ?? videoPreset?.seed.map(UInt64.init) ?? 42,
  2196	      strength: req.strength ?? 1.0,
  2197	      imgCompression: req.imgCompression,
  2198	      guidance: req.guidance,
  2199	      // Re-enabled by default for EXTENDED renders (#231, 2026-07-16): the
  2200	      // 2026-07-13 MLX mutex crash on this path was memory pressure — with
  2201	      // the int8 stack (#230) a 12s/3-chunk anchored render completed clean
  2202	      // (289f, no crash). Single-chunk renders don't anchor (nothing to
  2203	      // drift); callers can still pass 0 to disable.
  2204	      // Mid-pass identity re-anchor is OPT-IN and default OFF — it was superseded
  2205	      // by the face-region anchor (LTX2_FACE_ANCHOR_STRENGTH), which holds partner
  2206	      // faces without the multi-keyframe gap-collapse. Enable explicitly via
  2207	      // LTX2_REANCHOR_INTERVAL>0 (+ _STRENGTH); a standard 97f/4s render NEVER takes
  2208	      // it unless the interval is set below the frame count. Only the pre-existing
  2209	      // extended/chunked anchor stays on by default (unchanged behavior).
  2210	      identityAnchorStrength: req.identityAnchorStrength
  2211	        ?? (Self.isExtendedRender(
  2212	              extendToSeconds: req.extendToSeconds, duration: req.duration,
  2213	              framesPerChunk: req.frames ?? 97, fps: req.fps ?? 24)
  2214	            ? 0.5
  2215	            : ((effectiveInitImage != nil
  2216	                && (Int(ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_REANCHOR_INTERVAL"] ?? "") ?? 0) > 0
  2217	                && (req.frames ?? 97) > (Int(ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_REANCHOR_INTERVAL"] ?? "") ?? 0))
  2218	               ? (Float(ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_REANCHOR_STRENGTH"] ?? "") ?? 0.4) : 0)),
  2219	      identityReAnchorInterval: (Int(ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_REANCHOR_INTERVAL"] ?? "") ?? 0),
  2220	      extendToSeconds: foldedExtendSeconds,
  2221	      fps: req.fps ?? 24,
  2222	      loraPath: req.loraPath,
  2223	      loraStrength: req.loraStrength ?? 1.0,
  2224	      loras: resolvedLoRAs,
  2225	      outputPath: resolvedOutput
  2225	      outputPath: resolvedOutput
  2226	,
  2227	      tuning: req.tuning,
  2228	      presetTuning: videoPreset?.videoTuning,
  2229	      audio: req.audio ?? false
  2230	    )
  2231	    // Validate before enqueuing so bad frames/dims fail fast.
  2232	    try generator.validate(videoRequest)
  2233	
  2234	    return PreparedLocalVideo(
  2235	      generator: generator,
  2236	      request: videoRequest,
  2237	      mode: (effectiveInitImage?.isEmpty == false) ? .i2v : .t2v,
  2238	      source: req.source ?? "api",
  2239	      optimizationAttemptId: req.optimizationAttemptId)
  2240	  }

  2242	  /// If LTX-2 is configured, ASYNC-submit the local render and return 202 + a
  2243	  /// job status immediately; otherwise nil so the caller falls through to the
  2244	  /// Replicate proxy. Poll GET /v1/video/status/{id} for completion. This is the
  2245	  /// path a long (multi-minute / multi-chunk) render must take — it never holds
  2246	  /// the HTTP connection open for the whole denoise.
  2247	  private func localVideoAsyncResponseIfConfigured(body: Data) async -> RoutedResponse? {
  2248	    do {
  2249	      guard let prep = try await prepareLocalVideo(body: body) else { return nil }
  2250	      logger.info("LTX-2: local video job submitted (\(prep.request.width)x\(prep.request.height), \(prep.request.framesPerChunk)f)")
  2251	      var tracePayload: [String: String] = ["prompt": prep.request.prompt]
  2252	      if prep.request.audio {
  2253	        tracePayload["has_audio"] = "true"
  2254	        tracePayload["audio_seconds"] = String(format: "%.2f",
  2255	          Float(prep.request.framesPerChunk) / Float(prep.request.fps))
  2256	      }

  2258	        tracePayload["optimization_attempt_id"] = attemptId
  2259	      }
  2260	      let status = videoJobTracker.submit(
  2261	        source: prep.source, mode: prep.mode, coordinator: coordinator,
  2262	        // Snapshot at SUBMIT time (finding #15): the authoritative resolution
  2263	        // this render will use, durable on the job status.
  2264	        resolvedConfig: LTX2ConfigResolver.resolveTyped(
  2265	          request: prep.request.tuning, preset: prep.request.presetTuning).params,
  2266	        tracePayload: tracePayload,
  2267	        wantsAudio: prep.request.audio
  2268	      ) { report in
  2269	        try prep.generator.generate(prep.request) { chunk, totalChunks, step, totalSteps in
  2270	          report(Self.localVideoProgressPercent(
  2271	            chunk: chunk, totalChunks: totalChunks, step: step, totalSteps: totalSteps))
  2272	        }
  2273	      }
  2274	      let encoder = JSONEncoder()
  2275	      encoder.keyEncodingStrategy = .convertToSnakeCase
  2276	      let data = try encoder.encode(status)
  2277	      return .json(.rawJSON(status: 202, data: data))
  2278	    } catch let error as LTX2VideoError {
  2279	      return .error(.error(status: 400, message: error.localizedDescription))
  2280	    } catch {
  2281	      return .error(response(for: error))
  2282	    }
  2283	  }
  2284	
  2285	  /// If LTX-2 is configured, generate the video locally and return the result
  2286	  /// SYNCHRONOUSLY (blocks the HTTP connection for the whole render); otherwise
  2287	  /// nil so the caller falls through to the Replicate proxy. Kept for backward

  2289	  private func localVideoResponseIfConfigured(body: Data) async -> RoutedResponse? {

codex
The first concrete risk is already visible: enhancement replaces the entire caller prompt before tokenization, while manual character injection prepends to it. Those are two different failure modes—semantic loss in the optimizer and deterministic tail truncation—and the proposed P0 changes the very tail whose survival needs to be tested. I’m tracing both separately now.

     1	// PromptOptimizer.swift — Local LLM prompt optimization for image generation.
     2	//
     3	// Calls Ollama (primary) or LM Studio (fallback) via OpenAI-compatible
     4	// /v1/chat/completions endpoint. Falls back to raw prompt on any failure.
     5	//
     6	// Uses the same YOUR CONTEXT / YOUR PHOTO format as the server's prompt-optimizer.ts.
     7	// Zero external dependencies — URLSession only.
     8	
     9	import Foundation
    10	import Logging
    11	
    12	// MARK: - Types
    13	
    14	public struct OptimizeResult: Sendable {
    15	  public let prompt: String
    16	  public let enhanced: Bool
    17	  public let note: String?
    18	  /// Task #19 lineage (Codex findings #4/#7): which template produced this,
    19	  /// and what actually happened — so traces and training exports are
    20	  /// attributable. `outcome` ∈ skipped/succeeded/refused/timeout/error/fallback.
    21	  public let templateId: String?
    22	  public let templateHash: String?
    23	  public let templateSource: String?
    24	  public let outcome: String
    25	
    26	  public init(
    27	    prompt: String, enhanced: Bool, note: String?,
    28	    templateId: String? = nil, templateHash: String? = nil,
    29	    templateSource: String? = nil, outcome: String = "succeeded"
    30	  ) {
    31	    self.prompt = prompt
    32	    self.enhanced = enhanced
    33	    self.note = note
    34	    self.templateId = templateId
    35	    self.templateHash = templateHash
    36	    self.templateSource = templateSource
    37	    self.outcome = outcome
    38	  }
    39	}
