    41	// MARK: - PromptOptimizer
    42	
    43	public final class PromptOptimizer: @unchecked Sendable {
    44	  public struct Configuration: Sendable {
    45	    public let ollamaBaseURL: String
    46	    public let lmStudioBaseURL: String?
    47	    public let model: String
    48	    public let timeoutSeconds: Int
    49	    public let enabled: Bool
    50	
    51	    public init(
    52	      ollamaBaseURL: String = "http://localhost:11434",
    53	      lmStudioBaseURL: String? = "http://localhost:1234",
    54	      model: String = "qwen3:8b",
    55	      timeoutSeconds: Int = 15,
    56	      enabled: Bool = true
    57	    ) {
    58	      self.ollamaBaseURL = ollamaBaseURL
    59	      self.lmStudioBaseURL = lmStudioBaseURL
    60	      self.model = model
    61	      self.timeoutSeconds = timeoutSeconds
    62	      self.enabled = enabled
    63	    }
    64	  }
    65	
    66	  private let config: Configuration
    67	  private let logger: Logger
    68	
    69	  public init(configuration: Configuration, logger: Logger = Logger(label: "comfybox.optimizer")) {
    70	    self.config = configuration
    71	    self.logger = logger
    72	  }
    73	
    74	  /// Optimize a prompt for Z-Image Turbo.
    75	  /// Injects character description if provided.
    76	  /// Falls back to raw prompt if LLM is unavailable.
    77	  public func optimize(
    78	    prompt: String,
    79	    character: String?,
    80	    characterDescription: String?,
    81	    contentMode: String,
    82	    mediaKind: String = "image"
    83	  ) async -> OptimizeResult {
    84	    // Video (LTX) uses a different prompt format than image (Z-Image); its
    85	    // fallbacks must NOT emit the image "YOUR CONTEXT/YOUR PHOTO" wrapper — that
    86	    // pollutes LTX conditioning. On failure, video returns enhanced:false so the
    87	    // caller applies its own plain-prompt/character handling.
    88	    let isVideo = mediaKind.lowercased().hasPrefix("video")
    89	    guard config.enabled else {
    90	      if isVideo { return OptimizeResult(prompt: prompt, enhanced: false, note: "optimizer disabled", outcome: "skipped") }
    91	      let wrapped = Self.wrapInQwen3Format(prompt: prompt, contentMode: contentMode)
    92	      return OptimizeResult(prompt: wrapped, enhanced: true, note: "Rule-based format (optimizer disabled)", outcome: "skipped")
    93	    }
    94	
    95	    // Task #15: templates resolve through the store (file override > shipped
    96	    // builtin), and the id+hash lands in the log so any render's prompt text
    97	    // is attributable to a template version.
    98	    let templateId = PromptTemplateStore.templateId(contentMode: contentMode, mediaKind: mediaKind)
    99	    let resolvedTemplate = PromptTemplateStore.shared.template(templateId)
   100	    let systemPrompt = resolvedTemplate.text
   101	    let exemplars = ExemplarStore.shared.matching(mediaKind: mediaKind, contentMode: contentMode)
   102	    logger.info("prompt template: \(templateId)@\(resolvedTemplate.hash) (\(resolvedTemplate.source.rawValue)) exemplars=\(exemplars.count)@\(ExemplarStore.setDigest(exemplars))")
   103	    let userMessage = Self.buildUserMessage(
   104	      prompt: prompt,
   105	      character: character,
   106	      characterDescription: characterDescription,
   107	      contentMode: contentMode
   108	    )
   109	
   110	    // Try Ollama first
   111	    if let result = await callLLM(baseURL: config.ollamaBaseURL, systemPrompt: systemPrompt, userMessage: userMessage, contentMode: contentMode, exemplars: exemplars) {
   112	      let cleaned = Self.cleanLLMOutput(result)
   113	      if Self.looksLikeRefusal(cleaned) {
   114	        logger.warning("Optimizer output looks like a REFUSAL — discarding (a refusal string rendered as the prompt is the author-workflow trap; raw prompt is safer).")
   115	      } else if cleaned.count > 20 {
   116	        logger.info("Prompt optimized via Ollama (\(cleaned.count) chars)")
   117	        return OptimizeResult(
   118	          prompt: cleaned, enhanced: true, note: nil,
   119	          templateId: templateId, templateHash: resolvedTemplate.hash,
   120	          templateSource: resolvedTemplate.source.rawValue, outcome: "succeeded")
   121	      }
   122	    }
   123	
   124	    // Try LM Studio fallback
   125	    if let lmStudioURL = config.lmStudioBaseURL {
   126	      if let result = await callLLM(baseURL: lmStudioURL, systemPrompt: systemPrompt, userMessage: userMessage, contentMode: contentMode, exemplars: exemplars) {
   127	        let cleaned = Self.cleanLLMOutput(result)
   128	        if Self.looksLikeRefusal(cleaned) {
   129	          logger.warning("Optimizer (LM Studio) output looks like a REFUSAL — discarding.")
   130	        } else if cleaned.count > 20 {
   131	          logger.info("Prompt optimized via LM Studio (\(cleaned.count) chars)")
   132	          return OptimizeResult(
   133	            prompt: cleaned, enhanced: true, note: "LM Studio fallback",
   134	            templateId: templateId, templateHash: resolvedTemplate.hash,
   135	            templateSource: resolvedTemplate.source.rawValue, outcome: "succeeded")
   136	        }
   137	      }
   138	    }
   139	
   140	    // Both LLMs failed — video returns enhanced:false (caller handles plain
   141	    // prompt + character); image uses rule-based YOUR CONTEXT/YOUR PHOTO wrap.
   142	    logger.warning("Optimizer unavailable — \(isVideo ? "video: returning raw prompt" : "using rule-based format wrapping")")
   143	    if isVideo {
   144	      return OptimizeResult(
   145	        prompt: prompt, enhanced: false, note: "optimizer unavailable",
   146	        templateId: templateId, templateHash: resolvedTemplate.hash,
   147	        templateSource: resolvedTemplate.source.rawValue, outcome: "error")
   148	    }
   149	    let wrapped = Self.wrapInQwen3Format(prompt: prompt, contentMode: contentMode)
   150	    return OptimizeResult(
   151	      prompt: wrapped, enhanced: true, note: "Rule-based format (LLM unavailable)",
   152	      templateId: templateId, templateHash: resolvedTemplate.hash,
   153	      templateSource: resolvedTemplate.source.rawValue, outcome: "fallback")
   154	  }
   155	
   156	  // MARK: - LLM HTTP Call
   157	
   158	  private func callLLM(
   159	    baseURL: String, systemPrompt: String, userMessage: String, contentMode: String,
   160	    exemplars: [PromptExemplar] = []
   161	  ) async -> String? {
   162	    // Higher temperature for avocado — push past the model's "safe" defaults
   163	    let temperature: Double = contentMode == "avocado" ? 0.9 : 0.4
   164	
   165	    // Finding #5: exemplars ride as SEPARATE user/assistant few-shot pairs
   166	    // after the system message — never concatenated into the system prompt.
   167	    var messages: [[String: String]] = [["role": "system", "content": systemPrompt]]
   168	    for ex in exemplars {
   169	      messages.append(["role": "user", "content": ex.intent])
   170	      messages.append(["role": "assistant", "content": ex.final])
   171	    }
   172	    messages.append(["role": "user", "content": userMessage])
   173	
   174	    let payload: [String: Any] = [
   175	      "model": config.model,
   176	      "messages": messages,
   177	      "temperature": temperature,
   178	      "max_tokens": 1024,
   179	      "stream": false
   180	    ]
   181	
   182	    guard let payloadData = try? JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: payload),
   183	          let url = URL(string: "\(baseURL)/v1/chat/completions") else {
   184	      return nil
   185	    }
   186	
   187	    var request = URLRequest(url: url)
   188	    request.httpMethod = "POST"
   189	    request.setValue("application/json", forHTTPHeaderField: "Content-Type")
   190	    request.httpBody = payloadData
   191	    request.timeoutInterval = TimeInterval(config.timeoutSeconds)
   192	
   193	    do {
   194	      let (data, response) = try await URLSession.shared.data(for: request)
   195	      guard let httpResponse = response as? HTTPURLResponse, httpResponse.statusCode == 200 else {
   196	        return nil
   197	      }
   198	      guard let json = try? JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data) as? [String: Any],
   199	            let choices = json["choices"] as? [[String: Any]],
   200	            let first = choices.first,
   201	            let message = first["message"] as? [String: Any],
   202	            let content = message["content"] as? String else {
   203	        return nil
   204	      }
   205	      return content
   206	    } catch {
   207	      logger.debug("LLM call to \(baseURL) failed: \(error.localizedDescription)")
   208	      return nil
   209	    }
   210	  }
   211	
   212	  // MARK: - System Prompts
   213	
   214	  /// Z-Image Turbo rendering rules (shared across all modes).
   215	  private static let zImageRules = """
   216	  ## Z-IMAGE TURBO — Qwen3-4B text encoder, CFG-distilled
   217	
   218	  Z-Image's text encoder is Qwen3-4B (an LLM, not CLIP). It parses structured prose, not keyword tags. Negative prompts have zero effect (CFG=1.0 distilled). Keyword stacks ("masterpiece, 8k") are ignored.
   219	
   220	  ## OUTPUT FORMAT
   221	
   222	  Your output MUST use this two-block structure:
   223	
   224	  YOUR CONTEXT:
   225	  [Style/technical persona — described look and depth-of-field, lighting philosophy, aesthetic, skin texture approach]
   226	  YOUR PHOTO:
   227	  [Scene description — subject with physical traits woven in, action, composition, environment, atmosphere]
   228	
   229	  This is the format Z-Image's Qwen3-4B encoder was trained on. Always output both blocks.
   230	
   231	  ## YOUR CONTEXT BLOCK
   232	
   233	  Set the photographic persona and technical constraints. Describe the resulting LOOK in plain language — an LLM encoder responds to described effect, NOT camera jargon like "85mm f/1.4":
   234	  - Depth of field / framing as EFFECT (e.g. "tight portrait framing, background melting into soft creamy blur, only the eyes tack-sharp") — NOT focal lengths or f-stops
   235	  - Perspective as EFFECT (e.g. "gentle facial compression" for a tele look; "expansive wide framing with slight edge stretch" for a wide look)
   236	  - Lighting approach (e.g. "soft diffused window light, warm tungsten fill")
   237	  - Skin/texture style (e.g. "visible pores, peach fuzz, natural imperfections")
   238	  - Aesthetic (e.g. "intimate candid photography", "editorial portrait")
   239	  - Film stock if relevant (e.g. "Kodak Portra 400 color palette")
   240	
   241	  CRITICAL: Do NOT mention camera body brands, focal-length numbers ("85mm"), or f-stops ("f/1.4") — describe the depth of field, bokeh, and compression they produce, in words.
   242	
   243	  Keep this block 20-40 words.
   244	
   245	  ## YOUR PHOTO BLOCK
   246	
   247	  Describe WHAT to render. Subject, action, composition, environment.
   248	
   249	  ### Character traits: WEAVE THROUGH THE SCENE
   250	  When a character description is provided in context, treat it as canonical reference:
   251	  - Preserve ALL physical traits — skin tone, build, height, hair, eyes, distinguishing features
   252	  - Weave traits naturally as modifiers throughout the scene, not as a preamble
   253	  - Never contradict the character description
   254	
   255	  ### Facial detail matches face visibility
   256	  - Face NOT visible: NO facial features. Hair and pose only.
   257	  - Face partially visible: 1-2 traits max.
   258	  - Face IS the subject: full facial detail.
   259	
   260	  ## HARD RULES
   261	
   262	  1. ALWAYS output in YOUR CONTEXT: / YOUR PHOTO: format.
   263	  2. Narrative sentences only. NEVER comma-separated keyword lists.
   264	  3. NO quality keywords: masterpiece, best quality, 8k, ultra-detailed, hyperrealistic.
   265	  4. NO negatives. State what IS there only.
   266	  5. Skin tone early for non-white subjects.
   267	  6. Describe optical EFFECT (depth of field, bokeh, compression, framing) in prose — NEVER focal-length numbers, f-stops, or camera body brands.
   268	  7. Preserve user's compositional intent.
   269	  8. Target: YOUR CONTEXT 20-40 words, YOUR PHOTO 60-140 words. Front-load subject + action.
   270	  9. Character name AT MOST ONCE. Pronouns after.
   271	  """
   272	
   273	  static let systemPromptNeutral = """
   274	  You are an expert prompt optimizer for Z-Image Turbo. You rewrite SFW image prompts into Z-Image-native narrative form.
   275	
   276	  \(zImageRules)
   277	
   278	  ## SFW MODE
   279	
   280	  This is safe-for-work content. Subject is fully clothed. No nudity, no suggestive poses. Focus on character presence, environment, mood, and lighting.
     1	// LTX2VideoGenerator.swift — Server-callable local LTX-2 video generation
     2	//
     3	// Lifts the proven `ComfyBox ltx2-i2v` CLI flow into a reusable service so the
     4	// warm server can generate video locally (text-to-video and image-to-video)
     5	// instead of only via the Replicate cloud proxy. Models are loaded lazily and
     6	// cached; frame/chunk math and request validation are pure so they're testable
     7	// without the 38 GB model.
     8	
     9	import Foundation
    10	import Logging
    11	import MLX
    12	import MLXNN
    13	
    14	#if canImport(CoreGraphics) && canImport(ImageIO)
    15	import CoreGraphics
    16	import ImageIO
    17	#endif
    18	
    19	/// One LoRA to merge into the LTX-2 transformer for a render, applied in
    20	/// the order given. Mirrors the image side's {path, scale} shape so LTX
    21	/// video LoRAs are managed the same way as every other model's.
    22	public struct LTX2LoRAReference: Sendable, Equatable {
    23	    public var path: String
    24	    public var scale: Float
    25	    public init(path: String, scale: Float = 1.0) {
    26	        self.path = path
    27	        self.scale = scale
    28	    }
    29	}
    30	
    31	/// Parameters for one local LTX-2 video generation.
    32	public struct LTX2VideoRequest: Sendable {
    33	    public var prompt: String
    34	    public var negativePrompt: String?
    35	    /// nil → text-to-video; a path → image-to-video conditioned on it.
    36	    public var initImagePath: String?
    37	    public var width: Int
    38	    public var height: Int
    39	    /// Frames per chunk; must be 1 + 8k (9, 17, 25, …, 97).
    40	    public var framesPerChunk: Int
    41	    public var steps: Int
    42	    public var seed: UInt64
    43	    /// I2V conditioning strength (0–1).
    44	    public var strength: Float
    45	    /// Conditioning compression (libx264 CRF) override; nil = env/default.
    46	    /// Higher = more motion (frozen-still regime at 0-2), lower = more fidelity.
    47	    public var imgCompression: Int?
    48	    /// CFG guidance override; nil = env/config default. >1 amplifies the
    49	    /// action-prompt direction (primary motion lever, Codex 2026-07-26).
    50	    public var guidance: Float?
    51	    /// Identity re-anchor strength for CONTINUATION chunks (0 = off). Each
    52	    /// continuation chunk conditions on the previous chunk\u{27}s last frame at
    53	    /// frame 0 (hard continuity) AND the ORIGINAL source image at the chunk\u{27}s
    54	    /// last frame at this strength (soft identity pull) \u{2014} counters the
    55	    /// cumulative subject/scene drift of tail-to-head chaining (#219).
    56	    public var identityAnchorStrength: Float
    57	
    58	    /// Frames between mid-pass identity re-anchors for a LONG single/first pass
    59	    /// (0 = off). With only a frame-0 anchor, peripheral subjects (e.g. a
    60	    /// partner's face) drift and melt over a long pass; re-splicing the source
    61	    /// at this interval at `identityAnchorStrength` holds EVERY face. #partnered
    62	    public var identityReAnchorInterval: Int
    63	    /// Target duration; >0 generates continuation chunks (I2V only).
    64	    public var extendToSeconds: Float
    65	    public var fps: Int
    66	    /// Deprecated single-LoRA fields — kept for wire/call-site back-compat.
    67	    /// New callers should use `loras` instead. Folded into `effectiveLoRAs`.
    68	    public var loraPath: String?
    69	    public var loraStrength: Float
    70	    /// LoRAs merged into the transformer for this render, applied in order.
    71	    public var loras: [LTX2LoRAReference]
    72	    public var outputPath: String
    73	    /// Tier A tuning overrides (task #9 Phase 2) — nil fields defer to
    74	    /// preset > configFile > env > builtin. `presetTuning` is the preset's
    75	    /// block, resolved by the server and carried so the generator can build
    76	    /// the full five-level resolution.
    77	    public var tuning: LTX2VideoTuning?
    78	    public var presetTuning: LTX2VideoTuning?
    79	    /// Generate synchronized audio (task #21). T2V single-chunk only in v1;
    80	    /// loads the audio branch (+~11GiB) into the transformer on first use.
    81	    public var audio: Bool
    82	
    83	    /// `loras`, with the deprecated single `loraPath`/`loraStrength` (if set)
    84	    /// prepended — the single field always applied first, matching the old
    85	    /// single-LoRA behavior when only it is set.
    86	    public var effectiveLoRAs: [LTX2LoRAReference] {
    87	        var result: [LTX2LoRAReference] = []
    88	        if let loraPath, !loraPath.isEmpty {
    89	            result.append(LTX2LoRAReference(path: loraPath, scale: loraStrength))
    90	        }
    91	        result.append(contentsOf: loras)
    92	        return result
    93	    }
    94	
    95	    public init(
    96	        prompt: String,
    97	        negativePrompt: String? = nil,
    98	        initImagePath: String? = nil,
    99	        width: Int = 704,
   100	        height: Int = 448,
   101	        framesPerChunk: Int = 97,
   102	        steps: Int = 8,
   103	        seed: UInt64 = 42,
   104	        strength: Float = 1.0,
   105	        imgCompression: Int? = nil,
   106	        guidance: Float? = nil,
   107	        identityAnchorStrength: Float = 0,
   108	        identityReAnchorInterval: Int = 0,
   109	        extendToSeconds: Float = 0,
   110	        fps: Int = 24,
   111	        loraPath: String? = nil,
   112	        loraStrength: Float = 1.0,
   113	        loras: [LTX2LoRAReference] = [],
   114	        outputPath: String,
   115	        tuning: LTX2VideoTuning? = nil,
   116	        presetTuning: LTX2VideoTuning? = nil,
   117	        audio: Bool = false
   118	    ) {
   119	        self.audio = audio
   120	        self.prompt = prompt
   121	        self.negativePrompt = negativePrompt
   122	        self.initImagePath = initImagePath
   123	        self.width = width
   124	        self.height = height
   125	        self.framesPerChunk = framesPerChunk
   126	        self.steps = steps
   127	        self.seed = seed
   128	        self.strength = strength
   129	        self.imgCompression = imgCompression
   130	        self.guidance = guidance
   131	        self.identityAnchorStrength = identityAnchorStrength
   132	        self.identityReAnchorInterval = identityReAnchorInterval
   133	        self.extendToSeconds = extendToSeconds
   134	        self.fps = fps
   135	        self.loraPath = loraPath
   136	        self.loraStrength = loraStrength
   137	        self.loras = loras
   138	        self.outputPath = outputPath
   139	        self.tuning = tuning
   140	        self.presetTuning = presetTuning
   141	    }
   142	}
   143	
   144	public struct LTX2VideoResult: Sendable {
   145	    public let outputPath: String
   146	    public let frameCount: Int
   147	    public let durationSeconds: Float
   148	    public let elapsedSeconds: Double
   149	}
   150	
   151	public enum LTX2VideoError: Error, LocalizedError {
   152	    case invalidFrameCount(Int)
   153	    case invalidDimensions(Int, Int)
   154	    case weightsMissing(String)
   155	    case imageLoadFailed(String)
   156	    case unsupportedPlatform
   157	    case audioUnsupported(String)
   158	
   159	    public var errorDescription: String? {
   160	        switch self {
   161	        case .invalidFrameCount(let n):
   162	            return "LTX-2 frames must be 1 + 8k (9, 17, 25, …, 97); got \(n)."
   163	        case .invalidDimensions(let w, let h):
   164	            return "LTX-2 width/height must be divisible by 32; got \(w)x\(h)."
   165	        case .weightsMissing(let path):
   166	            return "LTX-2 weights not found: \(path)"
   167	        case .imageLoadFailed(let path):
   168	            return "Failed to load init image: \(path)"
   169	        case .unsupportedPlatform:
   170	            return "LTX-2 video requires CoreGraphics/ImageIO (macOS)."
   171	        case .audioUnsupported(let why):
   172	            return "LTX-2 audio: \(why)"
   173	        }
   174	    }
   175	}
   176	
   177	public final class LTX2VideoGenerator {
   178	    public struct Configuration: Sendable {
   179	        /// Directory holding transformer / vae_{encoder,decoder} / connector.
   180	        public var weightsDir: String
   181	        /// Gemma-3 tokenizer + text-encoder snapshot directory.
   182	        public var gemmaPath: String
   183	        /// Transformer weights filename inside `weightsDir`.
   184	        public var transformerFile: String
   185	
   186	        public init(
   187	            weightsDir: String,
   188	            gemmaPath: String,
   189	            transformerFile: String = "transformer-distilled.safetensors"
   190	        ) {
   240	    }
   241	
   242	    public struct ChunkPlan: Equatable, Sendable {
   243	        public let totalChunks: Int
   244	        public let totalFrames: Int
   245	        public let durationSeconds: Float
   246	    }
   247	
   248	    /// How many chunks and frames a request produces. Each continuation chunk
   249	    /// re-uses the previous chunk's last frame, so it adds `framesPerChunk - 1`
   250	    /// new frames. `extendToSeconds == 0` → a single chunk.
   251	    public static func chunkPlan(framesPerChunk: Int, extendToSeconds: Float, fps: Int) -> ChunkPlan {
   252	        let totalChunks: Int
   253	        if extendToSeconds > 0 {
   254	            let targetFrames = Int(extendToSeconds * Float(fps))
   255	            let continuations = max(0, Int(ceil(Float(targetFrames - framesPerChunk) / Float(framesPerChunk - 1))))
   256	            totalChunks = 1 + continuations
   257	        } else {
   258	            totalChunks = 1
   259	        }
   260	        let totalFrames = framesPerChunk + (framesPerChunk - 1) * (totalChunks - 1)
   261	        return ChunkPlan(
   262	            totalChunks: totalChunks,
   263	            totalFrames: totalFrames,
   264	            durationSeconds: Float(totalFrames) / Float(fps)
   265	        )
   266	    }
   267	
   268	    /// Validate a request without loading anything.
   269	    public func validate(_ request: LTX2VideoRequest) throws {
   270	        guard Self.isValidFrameCount(request.framesPerChunk) else {
   271	            throw LTX2VideoError.invalidFrameCount(request.framesPerChunk)
   272	        }
   273	        if request.audio {
   274	            // Audio scope: single-chunk T2V and I2V. Silently downgrading (the
   275	            // first cut) shipped MP4s whose audio stopped after chunk 0 or
   276	            // never existed — reject unsupported shapes loudly instead
   277	            // (Codex 2026-08-04 #4).
   278	            let plan = Self.chunkPlan(
   279	                framesPerChunk: request.framesPerChunk,
   280	                extendToSeconds: request.extendToSeconds, fps: request.fps)
   281	            if plan.totalChunks > 1 {
   282	                throw LTX2VideoError.audioUnsupported(
   283	                    "audio is not yet supported for chunked renders (\(plan.totalChunks) chunks requested; keep duration within one \(request.framesPerChunk)-frame chunk)")
   284	            }
   285	            if request.identityAnchorStrength > 0, request.identityReAnchorInterval > 0,
   286	               request.framesPerChunk > request.identityReAnchorInterval + 1 {
   287	                throw LTX2VideoError.audioUnsupported(
   288	                    "audio is not yet supported with mid-pass identity re-anchoring (multi-keyframe path)")
   289	            }
   290	        }
   291	        guard Self.areValidDimensions(width: request.width, height: request.height) else {
   292	            throw LTX2VideoError.invalidDimensions(request.width, request.height)
   293	        }
   294	        let weightsURL = resolveWeightsFileURL()
   295	        guard FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: weightsURL.path) else {
   296	            throw LTX2VideoError.weightsMissing(weightsURL.path)
   297	        }
   298	    }
   299	
   300	    /// Resolve the checkpoint file to load. Prefers the configured per-component
   301	    /// transformer file; if that's absent, falls back to a JoyAI-Echo monolith
   302	    /// (`*.safetensors` in `weightsDir` that isn't one of the per-component
   303	    /// VAE/connector files). Returns the configured path (possibly nonexistent)
   304	    /// as a last resort so callers surface a clear `weightsMissing` error.
   305	    private func resolveWeightsFileURL() -> URL {
   306	        let configured = URL(fileURLWithPath:
   307	            (config.weightsDir as NSString).appendingPathComponent(config.transformerFile))
   308	        if FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: configured.path) { return configured }
   309	
   310	        let fm = FileManager.default
   311	        if let entries = try? fm.contentsOfDirectory(
   312	            at: URL(fileURLWithPath: config.weightsDir),
   313	            includingPropertiesForKeys: nil
   314	        ) {
   315	            let perComponent: Set<String> = [
   520	            try textEncoder.loadWeights(
   521	                modelPath: URL(fileURLWithPath: modelDir),
   522	                textEncoderPath: URL(fileURLWithPath: config.gemmaPath)
   523	            )
   524	        }
   525	        MLX.eval(textEncoder.parameters())
   526	
   527	        // Reference ComfyUI-LTXVideo workflows always decode through
   528	        // VAEDecodeTiled, never a plain single-pass decode — the decoder
   529	        // likely relies on windowed/local processing that the (already
   530	        // implemented, just never enabled) tiled path is designed for.
   531	        // Running it as one giant pass is the leading suspect for the
   532	        // uniform grid/mesh artifact seen in every local I2V test tonight.
   533	        // Two-stage refine (Phase 3): load the spatial latent upsampler if enabled.
   534	        // ltx-2.3-spatial-upscaler-x2-1.1 keys map 1:1 to LTX2LatentUpsampler.
   535	        var upsampler: LTX2LatentUpsampler? = nil
   536	        if ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_TWO_STAGE"] == "1",
   537	           let upPath = ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_UPSAMPLER_PATH"],
   538	           FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: upPath) {
   539	            upsampler = Self.loadUpsampler(path: upPath, logger: logger)
   540	        }
   541	        // Tiled/chunked VAE decode is OOM-safe on long/large clips but seams on
   542	        // fast motion (spatial-tile mosaic + temporal-window jitter). Plain
   543	        // single-pass decode (as ComfyUI does) is clean but memory-heavier.
   544	        // LTX2_TILED_DECODE=0 selects plain decode. Default stays tiled.
   545	        let tiled = ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_TILED_DECODE"] != "0"
   546	        // NOTE: the warm pipeline is built ONCE here, so per-request fps cannot
   547	        // flow through config.fps (it would bake in the first request's value).
   548	        // The temporal-RoPE conditioning fps (the motion dial) is therefore
   549	        // controlled per-render via LTX2_COND_FPS (read fresh in createPositionGrid).
   550	        let pipelineConfig = LTX2PipelineConfig(modelPath: modelDir, pipelineType: .distilled, hasPromptAdaLN: true, tiledDecode: tiled)
   551	        self.pipeline = LTX2Pipeline(vae: vae, textEncoder: textEncoder, transformer: transformer, config: pipelineConfig, upsampler: upsampler)
   552	        self.tokenizer = try LTX2GemmaTokenizer.load(from: URL(fileURLWithPath: config.gemmaPath), maxLength: 128)
   553	        isLoaded = true
   554	        loadedLoraKey = wantKey
   555	        logger.info("LTX-2: models ready.")
   556	    }
   557	
   558	
   559	    /// Load + validate the spatial latent upsampler. Shared by the load-time
   560	    /// path (env) and the per-request lazy path (finding #18). Returns nil —
   561	    /// loudly — when the file's keys do not bind the module completely: a
   562	    /// partially bound upsampler renders a periodic mesh (2026-08-01).
   563	    static func loadUpsampler(path: String, logger: Logger) -> LTX2LatentUpsampler? {
   564	        let up = LTX2LatentUpsampler()
   565	        guard let w = try? MLX.loadArrays(url: URL(fileURLWithPath: path)) else {
   566	            logger.error("LTX-2: upsampler file unreadable: \(path)")
   567	            return nil
   568	        }
   569	        // Checkpoint stores conv weights in PyTorch layout (out, in, *spatial);
   570	        // MLX conv layers are channels-last. Permute conv weights by ndim;
   571	        // 1D params pass through untouched. Sequential index 0 renames to the
   572	        // named `conv` child.
   573	        let remapped: [(String, MLXArray)] = w.map { (rawKey, v) in
   574	            let key = rawKey.hasPrefix("upsampler.0.")
   575	                ? "upsampler.conv." + rawKey.dropFirst("upsampler.0.".count)
   576	                : rawKey
   577	            if key.hasSuffix(".weight") {
   578	                if v.ndim == 5 { return (key, v.transposed(0, 2, 3, 4, 1)) }
   579	                if v.ndim == 4 { return (key, v.transposed(0, 2, 3, 1)) }
   580	            }
   581	            return (key, v)
   582	        }
   583	        let expected = Set(up.parameters().flattened().map { $0.0 })
   584	        let bound = remapped.filter { expected.contains($0.0) }.count
   585	        guard bound == expected.count else {
   586	            let sample = remapped.map(\.0).filter { !expected.contains($0) }.prefix(3)
   587	            logger.error("""
   588	                LTX-2: upsampler weights do NOT match the module — bound \(bound)/\(expected.count) \
   589	                parameters from \(w.count) file tensors (unmatched e.g. \(Array(sample))). \
   590	                Two-stage refine stays OFF; use the official Lightricks \
   591	                ltx-2.3-spatial-upscaler-x2-1.1.safetensors (bare keys, PyTorch layout).
   592	                """)
   593	            return nil
   594	        }
   595	        do {
   596	            try up.update(parameters: ModuleParameters.unflattened(remapped), verify: [.shapeMismatch])
   597	        } catch {
   598	            logger.error("LTX-2: upsampler update failed: \(error)")
   599	            return nil
   600	        }
   601	        MLX.eval(up.parameters())
   602	        logger.info("LTX-2: two-stage refine upsampler loaded (bound \(bound)/\(expected.count) parameters)")
   603	        return up
   604	    }
   605	
   606	    /// Free the loaded models.
   607	    public func unload() {
   608	        pipeline = nil
   609	        tokenizer = nil
   610	        isLoaded = false
   611	        loadedLoraKey = nil
   612	    }
   613	
   614	    // MARK: - Generate
   615	
   616	    public func generate(
   617	        _ request: LTX2VideoRequest,
   618	        progress: ((Int, Int, Int, Int) -> Void)? = nil   // (chunk, totalChunks, step, totalSteps)
   619	    ) throws -> LTX2VideoResult {
   620	        #if canImport(CoreGraphics) && canImport(ImageIO)
   621	        // Memory-leak fix (2026-07-18): the video render path never freed MLX
   622	        // activation buffers, so idle mem climbed ~20GB -> 110GB+ across renders
   623	        // until every render hit `Memory pressure` mid-flight and the shedding
   624	        // corrupted the output into rainbow noise. Clear the MLX cache before
   625	        // (drop leftover image-gen buffers, freeing headroom) and after (this
   626	        // render's activations, via defer) EVERY render.
   627	        GPU.clearCache()
   628	        defer { GPU.clearCache() }
   629	        try validate(request)
   630	        // validate() has already rejected unsupported audio modes (i2v /
   631	        // multi-chunk), so this is simply the request flag.
   632	        let wantAudio = request.audio
   633	        try load(loras: request.effectiveLoRAs, audio: wantAudio)
   634	        guard let pipeline, let tokenizer else { throw LTX2VideoError.weightsMissing(config.weightsDir) }
   635	
   636	        // One greppable line per render: every Tier A/B param + provenance
   637	        // (task #9 Phase 1). Invalid resolutions log separately and LOUDLY.
   638	        // Phase 2: the TYPED resolution is authoritative — refresh it onto
   639	        // the pipeline so render code reads it instead of raw env (Codex
   640	        // finding #14). Request/preset tuning joins in the wire-format
   641	        // increment; until then this resolves configFile > env > builtin.
   642	        let typedConfig = LTX2ConfigResolver.resolveTyped(request: request.tuning, preset: request.presetTuning)
   643	        pipeline.resolvedConfig = typedConfig
   644	        let resolved = typedConfig.params
   645	        // Finding #18: two_stage was load-time only — a request could not turn
   646	        // it on without a server restart. Lazy-load the upsampler on the first
   647	        // request that resolves twoStage=true.
   648	        if typedConfig.twoStage, pipeline.upsampler == nil,
   649	           !typedConfig.upsamplerPath.isEmpty,
   650	           FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: typedConfig.upsamplerPath) {
   651	            pipeline.upsampler = Self.loadUpsampler(path: typedConfig.upsamplerPath, logger: logger)
   652	        }
   653	        let summary = resolved.map { "\($0.name)=\($0.value)(\($0.source.rawValue))" }.joined(separator: " ")
   654	        logger.info("[LTX2] effective-config: \(summary)")
   655	        for p in resolved where !p.valid {
   656	            logger.error("[LTX2] CONFIG REJECTED: \(p.name) — \(p.note ?? "invalid") — using \(p.value) (\(p.source.rawValue))")
   657	        }
   658	
   659	        let plan = Self.chunkPlan(
   660	            framesPerChunk: request.framesPerChunk,
   661	            extendToSeconds: request.extendToSeconds, fps: request.fps)
   662	
   663	        let batch = tokenizer.encode(prompt: request.prompt, maxLength: 128)
   664	        MLX.eval(batch.inputIds, batch.attentionMask)
   665	
   666	        // Negative prompt: tokenize when provided, or default to the PinkCherry
   667	        // workflow negative when a CFG++ sampler is active (CFG++ requires a
   668	        // negative pass every step even at cfg=1).
   669	        let negText: String? = {
   670	            if let n = request.negativePrompt, !n.isEmpty { return n }
   671	            return pipeline.resolvedConfig.samplerIsCfgPP
   672	                ? "subtitle, caption, text, text on screen, watermark, logo, timestamp, distorted sound, saturated sound, loud noises, static"
   673	                : nil
   674	        }()
   675	        let negBatch = negText.map { tokenizer.encode(prompt: $0, maxLength: 128) }
   676	        if let negBatch { MLX.eval(negBatch.inputIds, negBatch.attentionMask) }
   677	
   678	        let start = CFAbsoluteTimeGetCurrent()
   679	        var allFrames: [CGImage] = []
   680	        var audioLatents: MLXArray? = nil
   681	
   682	        // Center-crop a CGImage to the target aspect ratio, matching ComfyUI's
   683	        // ImageScale crop="center" (workflow nodes 7 and 19). Our plain resize
   684	        // STRETCHES on aspect mismatch — seed stills are often 9:16 (0.5625)
   685	        // against 384x640 (0.6), a ~7% vertical squash that distorts the
   686	        // conditioning content vs the workflow's crop.
   687	        func centerCropped(_ cg: CGImage, targetW: Int, targetH: Int) -> CGImage {
   688	            let srcW = Double(cg.width), srcH = Double(cg.height)
   689	            let targetAspect = Double(targetW) / Double(targetH)
   690	            let srcAspect = srcW / srcH
   691	            var cropW = srcW, cropH = srcH
   692	            if srcAspect > targetAspect {
   693	                cropW = srcH * targetAspect
   694	            } else {
   695	                cropH = srcW / targetAspect
   696	            }
   697	            let rect = CGRect(
   698	                x: ((srcW - cropW) / 2).rounded(.down),
   699	                y: ((srcH - cropH) / 2).rounded(.down),
   700	                width: cropW.rounded(), height: cropH.rounded())
   701	            return cg.cropping(to: rect) ?? cg
   702	        }
   703	
   704	        // Conditioning compression (libx264 CRF), function-scoped so BOTH the
   705	        // initial seed AND the chained continuation-chunk seeds get it. Chained
   706	        // seeds that skip it condition on a PRISTINE generated frame = the
   707	        // frozen-image regime → motion collapses across chunks (2026-07-26:
   708	        // comp30 chunk1 action-zone 6.11, chunks 2-3 crash to 2.2 because the
   709	        // chained frame was uncompressed). Higher = more motion.
   710	        let conditioningCompression = request.imgCompression
   850	                                if rr>=0 && rr<latH && cc>=0 && cc<latW { acc += mask[rr*latW+cc]; cnt += 1 }
   851	                            }}
   852	                            blurred[row*latW+col] = acc/cnt
   853	                        }
   854	                    }
   855	                    mask = blurred
   856	                }
   857	                faceAnchorMask = MLXArray(mask, [1, 1, 1, latH, latW])
   858	                logger.info("Face-anchor: \(rects.count) face(s) detected, strength \(faceAnchorStrength)")
   859	            }
   860	        }
   861	
   862	        for chunk in 0..<plan.totalChunks {
   863	            let chunkSeed = request.seed + UInt64(chunk)
   864	            let output: LTX2PipelineOutput
   865	            if let image = currentImage {
   866	                if chunk > 0, request.identityAnchorStrength > 0, let anchor = sourceImage {
   867	                    // Continuation chunks drift chunk-by-chunk (each only sees
   868	                    // the previous tail). Splice the original source in at the
   869	                    // chunk\u{27}s last frame at reduced strength \u{2014} a soft pull
   870	                    // back toward the subject \u{2014} while frame 0 stays the hard
   871	                    // continuity anchor. First real caller of
   872	                    // generateMultiKeyframe (see docs/ltx2-multi-keyframe-fdd.md).
   873	                    output = pipeline.generateMultiKeyframe(
   874	                        inputIds: batch.inputIds, attentionMask: batch.attentionMask,
   875	                        keyframes: [
   876	                            .init(image: image, videoFrameIndex: 0, strength: request.strength),
   877	                            .init(image: anchor, videoFrameIndex: request.framesPerChunk - 1,
   878	                                  strength: request.identityAnchorStrength),
   879	                        ],
   880	                        width: request.width, height: request.height,
   881	                        numFrames: request.framesPerChunk, steps: request.steps, seed: chunkSeed,
   882	                        guidance: request.guidance,
   883	                        negativeInputIds: negBatch?.inputIds,
   884	                        negativeAttentionMask: negBatch?.attentionMask,
   885	                        progressCallback: { s, t in progress?(chunk, plan.totalChunks, s, t) })
   886	                } else if request.identityAnchorStrength > 0,
   887	                          request.identityReAnchorInterval > 0,
   888	                          request.framesPerChunk > request.identityReAnchorInterval + 1,
   889	                          let src = sourceImage {
   890	                    // Single/long first pass: with only a frame-0 anchor, peripheral
   891	                    // subjects (a partner's face) drift and melt over the pass. Re-splice
   892	                    // the ORIGINAL source at fixed intervals at reduced strength — soft
   893	                    // identity pulls that hold EVERY face across the whole pass without
   894	                    // freezing motion. Same primitive as the continuation-chunk anchor.
   895	                    var keyframes: [LTX2Pipeline.Keyframe] = [
   896	                        .init(image: image, videoFrameIndex: 0, strength: request.strength)
   897	                    ]
   898	                    var f = request.identityReAnchorInterval
   899	                    while f < request.framesPerChunk - 1 {
   900	                        keyframes.append(.init(image: src, videoFrameIndex: f,
   901	                                               strength: request.identityAnchorStrength))
   902	                        f += request.identityReAnchorInterval
   903	                    }
   904	                    output = pipeline.generateMultiKeyframe(
   905	                        inputIds: batch.inputIds, attentionMask: batch.attentionMask,
   906	                        keyframes: keyframes,
   907	                        width: request.width, height: request.height,
   908	                        numFrames: request.framesPerChunk, steps: request.steps, seed: chunkSeed,
   909	                        guidance: request.guidance,
   910	                        negativeInputIds: negBatch?.inputIds,
   911	                        negativeAttentionMask: negBatch?.attentionMask,
   912	                        progressCallback: { s, t in progress?(chunk, plan.totalChunks, s, t) })
   913	                } else {
   914	                    output = pipeline.generateI2V(
   915	                        inputIds: batch.inputIds, attentionMask: batch.attentionMask,
   916	                        image: image, strength: request.strength,
   917	                        width: request.width, height: request.height,
   918	                        numFrames: request.framesPerChunk, steps: request.steps, seed: chunkSeed,
   919	                        guidance: request.guidance,
   920	                        negativeInputIds: negBatch?.inputIds,
   921	                        negativeAttentionMask: negBatch?.attentionMask,
   922	                        faceAnchorMask: chunk == 0 ? faceAnchorMask : nil,
   923	                        faceAnchorStrength: faceAnchorStrength,
   924	                        refineAnchorImage: chunk == 0 ? refineAnchorImage : nil,
   925	                        audioSeconds: wantAudio && chunk == 0
   926	                            ? Float(request.framesPerChunk) / Float(request.fps) : nil,
   927	                        progressCallback: { s, t in progress?(chunk, plan.totalChunks, s, t) })
   928	                }
   929	            } else {
   930	                output = pipeline.generateT2V(
   931	                    inputIds: batch.inputIds, attentionMask: batch.attentionMask,
   932	                    width: request.width, height: request.height,
   933	                    numFrames: request.framesPerChunk, steps: request.steps, seed: chunkSeed,
   934	                    // Every i2v variant above forwards request.guidance; this call
   935	                    // omitted it, so the daemon's T2V_GUIDANCE=3.5 (Todd 2026-07-30)
   936	                    // silently fell back to the distilled default 1.0 — t2v ran
   937	                    // with CFG OFF in prod for three days (found 2026-08-02 while
   938	                    // chasing extra limbs; cfg 1.0 under-drives t2v anatomy).
   939	                    guidance: request.guidance,
   940	                    negativeInputIds: negBatch?.inputIds,
   941	                    negativeAttentionMask: negBatch?.attentionMask,
   942	                    audioSeconds: wantAudio && chunk == 0
   943	                        ? Float(request.framesPerChunk) / Float(request.fps) : nil,
   944	                    progressCallback: { s, t in progress?(chunk, plan.totalChunks, s, t) })
   945	            }
   946	
   947	            let chunkFrames = LTX2PostProcess.framesToImages(from: output.decoded, colorAnchor: pipeline.resolvedConfig.colorAnchor)
   948	            allFrames.append(contentsOf: chunk == 0 ? chunkFrames : Array(chunkFrames.dropFirst()))
   949	            if let al = output.audioLatents { audioLatents = al }
   950	
   951	            // Re-feed the last frame as the seed for the next continuation chunk.
   952	            if chunk < plan.totalChunks - 1 {
   953	                let t = output.decoded.dim(2)
   954	                var lastFrame = output.decoded[0..., 0..., (t - 1)..<t, 0..., 0...].squeezed(axis: 2)
   955	                // Two-stage refine decodes at 2x the base resolution. The
   956	                // chained seed MUST be at base resolution: feeding the 2x frame
   957	                // VAE-encodes to a conditioning latent at 2x latent dims and
   958	                // applyConditioning fatalErrors on the shape mismatch —
   959	                // instant process death, no log. EVERY multi-chunk crash of
   960	                // 2026-07-25/26 (10:48, 01:28, 02:57, 03:16) died exactly here,
   961	                // seconds after "Encoding N keyframe image(s)".
   962	                if lastFrame.dim(2) != request.height || lastFrame.dim(3) != request.width {
   963	                    let squeezed = lastFrame.squeezed(axis: 0)
   964	                    let resized = try QwenImageIO.resize(
   965	                        rgbArray: squeezed,
   966	                        targetHeight: request.height, targetWidth: request.width)
   967	                    // Lanczos overshoot can leave values outside [0,1]; the
   968	                    // *2-1 normalization below would push them out of the
   969	                    // VAE's expected range (Codex review 2026-07-26).
   970	                    lastFrame = MLX.clip(resized.expandedDimensions(axis: 0), min: MLXArray(Float(0)), max: MLXArray(Float(1)))
   971	                    logger.info("Chunk seed downscaled from refine resolution to \(request.width)x\(request.height) for chaining")
   972	                }
   973	                // Apply the SAME conditioning compression the initial seed got,
   974	                // so continuation chunks read the chained frame as mid-motion
   975	                // video (not a pristine still that freezes). Round-trip the
   976	                // [0,1] frame through H.264; fall back to the raw frame if the
   977	                // codec path fails (never block a render on the preprocess).
   978	                if conditioningCompression > 0,
   979	                   let cg = try? QwenImageIO.image(from: lastFrame),
   980	                   let rt = try? LTX2PostProcess.h264RoundTrip(cg, compression: conditioningCompression),

     1	// LTX2GemmaTokenizer.swift — Gemma 3 tokenizer for LTX-2 text encoding
     2	// Phase 2 of the LTX-2 Swift/MLX port
     3	//
     4	// Wraps the swift-transformers library to provide Gemma 3 tokenization
     5	// compatible with the LTX-2 text encoder pipeline.
     6	//
     7	// Key differences from the QwenTokenizer:
     8	// - Left-padded (not right-padded) to match Python HuggingFace default for Gemma
     9	// - pad_token_id = 0
    10	// - bos_token_id = 2 (prepended automatically)
    11	// - No chat template prefix/suffix — raw text tokenization
    12	// - Default max_length = 128 (matching Python reference)
    13	
    14	import Foundation
    15	import Hub
    16	import MLX
    17	import Tokenizers
    18	
    19	// MARK: - Gemma Token Batch
    20	
    21	/// Tokenized batch output for Gemma 3.
    22	public struct GemmaTokenBatch {
    23	  /// Token IDs `[B, S]` as int32, left-padded.
    24	  public let inputIds: MLXArray
    25	
    26	  /// Attention mask `[B, S]` as int32 (1 = valid, 0 = pad).
    27	  public let attentionMask: MLXArray
    28	}
    29	
    30	// MARK: - Gemma Tokenizer
    31	
    32	/// Gemma 3 tokenizer for the LTX-2 text encoder pipeline.
    33	///
    34	/// Loads tokenizer configuration from a local HuggingFace model directory
    35	/// (e.g. `~/.cache/huggingface/hub/models--unsloth--gemma-3-12b-it/snapshots/...`).
    36	///
    37	/// Produces LEFT-padded token batches matching the Python reference:
    38	/// ```python
    39	/// tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors="pt", padding="max_length",
    40	///           max_length=128, truncation=True)
    41	/// ```
    42	public final class LTX2GemmaTokenizer {
    43	  private let tokenizer: Tokenizer
    44	  private let encodeFunction: (String) -> [Int]
    45	
    46	  /// Pad token ID (0 for Gemma 3).
    47	  public let padTokenId: Int
    48	
    49	  /// BOS token ID (2 for Gemma 3).
    50	  public let bosTokenId: Int
    51	
    52	  /// Maximum sequence length.
    53	  public let maxLength: Int
    54	
    55	  public init(
    56	    tokenizer: Tokenizer,
    57	    padTokenId: Int = 0,
    58	    bosTokenId: Int = 2,
    59	    maxLength: Int = 128,
    60	    encode: @escaping (String) -> [Int]
    61	  ) {
    62	    self.tokenizer = tokenizer
    63	    self.padTokenId = padTokenId
    64	    self.bosTokenId = bosTokenId
    65	    self.maxLength = maxLength
    66	    self.encodeFunction = encode
    67	  }
    68	
    69	  /// Load the Gemma tokenizer from a local HuggingFace model directory.
    70	  ///
    71	  /// The directory must contain `tokenizer_config.json`, `tokenizer.json`,
    72	  /// and optionally `added_tokens.json`.
    73	  ///
    74	  /// - Parameters:
    75	  ///   - directory: Path to the model directory.
    76	  ///   - maxLength: Maximum sequence length (default 128).
    77	  /// - Returns: Configured `LTX2GemmaTokenizer`.
    78	  public static func load(
    79	    from directory: URL,
    80	    maxLength: Int = 128
    81	  ) throws -> LTX2GemmaTokenizer {
    82	    let tokenizerConfigURL = directory.appendingPathComponent("tokenizer_config.json")
    83	    let tokenizerDataURL = directory.appendingPathComponent("tokenizer.json")
    84	
    85	    guard FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: directory.path) else {
    86	      throw GemmaTokenizerError.directoryNotFound(directory)
    87	    }
    88	    guard FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: tokenizerConfigURL.path) else {
    89	      throw GemmaTokenizerError.fileNotFound(tokenizerConfigURL)
    90	    }
    91	    guard FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: tokenizerDataURL.path) else {
    92	      throw GemmaTokenizerError.fileNotFound(tokenizerDataURL)
    93	    }
    94	
    95	    let hubApi = HubApi.shared
    96	    let tokenizerConfig = try hubApi.configuration(fileURL: tokenizerConfigURL)
    97	    let tokenizerData = try hubApi.configuration(fileURL: tokenizerDataURL)
    98	
    99	    let tokenizer = try AutoTokenizer.from(
   100	      tokenizerConfig: tokenizerConfig,
   101	      tokenizerData: tokenizerData,
   102	      strict: false  // Gemma may have non-standard tokenizer class name
   103	    )
   104	
   105	    // Resolve pad token ID
   106	    let padTokenId: Int
   107	    if let padStr = tokenizerConfig["pad_token"].string(),
   108	      let padId = tokenizer.convertTokenToId(padStr)
   109	    {
   110	      padTokenId = padId
   111	    } else {
   112	      padTokenId = 0  // Gemma default
   113	    }
   114	
   115	    // Resolve BOS token ID
   116	    let bosTokenId = tokenizer.bosTokenId ?? 2
   117	
   118	    return LTX2GemmaTokenizer(
   119	      tokenizer: tokenizer,
   120	      padTokenId: padTokenId,
   121	      bosTokenId: bosTokenId,
   122	      maxLength: maxLength,
   123	      encode: { text in tokenizer.encode(text: text) }
   124	    )
   125	  }
   126	
   127	  /// Tokenize a prompt with LEFT padding to maxLength.
   128	  ///
   129	  /// Matches the Python tokenizer behavior:
   130	  /// ```python
   131	  /// tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors="pt", padding="max_length",
   132	  ///           max_length=128, truncation=True)
   133	  /// ```
   134	  ///
   135	  /// - Parameters:
   136	  ///   - prompt: Text prompt to tokenize.
   137	  ///   - maxLength: Override max length (default uses instance maxLength).
   138	  /// - Returns: `GemmaTokenBatch` with left-padded input_ids and attention_mask.
   139	  public func encode(prompt: String, maxLength: Int? = nil) -> GemmaTokenBatch {
   140	    return encode(prompts: [prompt], maxLength: maxLength)
   141	  }
   142	
   143	  /// Tokenize multiple prompts with LEFT padding to maxLength.
   144	  ///
   145	  /// - Parameters:
   146	  ///   - prompts: Text prompts to tokenize.
   147	  ///   - maxLength: Override max length.
   148	  /// - Returns: `GemmaTokenBatch` with left-padded input_ids and attention_mask.
   149	  public func encode(prompts: [String], maxLength: Int? = nil) -> GemmaTokenBatch {
   150	    precondition(!prompts.isEmpty, "At least one prompt must be provided.")
   151	
   152	    let targetLength = min(maxLength ?? self.maxLength, self.maxLength)
   153	
   154	    var allIds: [[Int]] = []
   155	    var allMasks: [[Int]] = []
   156	
   157	    for prompt in prompts {
   158	      // Tokenize (swift-transformers handles BOS automatically for Gemma)
   159	      var tokens = encodeFunction(prompt)
   160	
   161	      // Truncate if needed
   162	      if tokens.count > targetLength {
   163	        tokens = Array(tokens.prefix(targetLength))
   164	      }
   165	
   166	      // LEFT padding: pad tokens go at the beginning
   167	      let paddingCount = max(0, targetLength - tokens.count)
   168	      let paddedIds = Array(repeating: padTokenId, count: paddingCount) + tokens
   169	      let mask = Array(repeating: 0, count: paddingCount) + Array(repeating: 1, count: tokens.count)
   170	
   171	      allIds.append(paddedIds)
   172	      allMasks.append(mask)
   173	    }
   174	
   175	    let batchSize = prompts.count
   176	    let flatIds = allIds.flatMap { $0 }
   177	    let flatMask = allMasks.flatMap { $0 }
   178	
   179	    let inputIds = MLXArray(flatIds.map { Int32($0) }, [batchSize, targetLength])
   180	    let attentionMask = MLXArray(flatMask.map { Int32($0) }, [batchSize, targetLength])
   181	
   182	    return GemmaTokenBatch(inputIds: inputIds, attentionMask: attentionMask)
   183	  }
   184	
   185	  /// Decode token IDs back to text.
   186	  public func decode(tokens: [Int]) -> String {
   187	    tokenizer.decode(tokens: tokens)
   188	  }
   189	}
   190	
   191	// MARK: - Errors
   192	
   193	public enum GemmaTokenizerError: Error, LocalizedError {
   194	  case directoryNotFound(URL)
   195	  case fileNotFound(URL)
   196	
   197	  public var errorDescription: String? {
   198	    switch self {
   199	    case .directoryNotFound(let url):
   200	      return "Gemma tokenizer directory not found: \(url.path)"
   201	    case .fileNotFound(let url):
   202	      return "Required tokenizer file not found: \(url.path)"
   203	    }
   204	  }
   205	}
     1	// LTX2TextEncoder.swift — Top-level text encoder for the LTX-2 video generation model
     2	// Phase 2 of the LTX-2 Swift/MLX port
     3	//
     4	// Orchestrates the full text encoding pipeline:
     5	//   Tokenize -> Gemma 3 forward (all hidden states) -> Feature Extract -> 1D Connector -> Embeddings
     6	//
     7	// Output:
     8	//   - videoEmbeddings: (B, seqLen, 4096) — for cross-attention in the video transformer
     9	//   - audioEmbeddings: (B, seqLen, 2048) — for cross-attention in the audio transformer
    10	//
    11	// Supports both LTX-2 (original) and LTX-2.3 (prompt adaln) variants.
    12	//
    13	// Reference: text_encoder.py class LTX2TextEncoder
    14	
    15	import Foundation
    16	import MLX
    17	import MLXFast
    18	import MLXNN
    19	
    20	// MARK: - Text Encoder Output
    21	
    22	/// Output from the LTX-2 text encoder.
    23	public struct LTX2TextEncoderOutput {
    24	  /// Video embeddings for cross-attention `[B, S, videoDim]`.
    25	  public let videoEmbeddings: MLXArray
    26	
    27	  /// Audio embeddings for cross-attention `[B, S, audioDim]`.
    28	  public let audioEmbeddings: MLXArray
    29	
    30	  /// Attention mask `[B, S]` (1 = valid, 0 = pad).
    31	  public let attentionMask: MLXArray
    32	}
    33	
    34	// MARK: - LTX-2 Text Encoder
    35	
    36	/// Top-level LTX-2 text encoder.
    37	///
    38	/// Orchestrates the complete text encoding pipeline:
    39	/// 1. Tokenize input prompt
    40	/// 2. Run Gemma 3 forward pass extracting all 49 hidden states
    41	/// 3. Feature extraction (V1 or V2 normalization + projection)
    42	/// 4. 1D connector (transformer blocks + register tokens + RoPE)
    43	/// 5. Output dual video/audio embeddings
    44	///
    45	/// The text encoder is a heavyweight module (~24 GB at fp16, ~6 GB at Q4).
    46	/// It is loaded separately from the main diffusion transformer.
    47	public final class LTX2TextEncoder: Module {
    48	  public let config: LTX2TextEncoderConfig
    49	
    50	  /// Gemma 3 12B language model backbone
    51	  let languageModel: LTX2GemmaModel
    52	
    53	  /// V1 feature extractor (LTX-2 original)
    54	  @ModuleInfo(key: "feature_extractor") var featureExtractorV1: LTX2FeatureExtractorV1?
    55	
    56	  /// V2 feature extractor (LTX-2.3)
    57	  @ModuleInfo(key: "feature_extractor_v2") var featureExtractorV2: LTX2FeatureExtractorV2?
    58	
    59	  /// Video embeddings connector
    60	  @ModuleInfo(key: "video_embeddings_connector") var videoConnector: LTX2Connector1D
    61	
    62	  /// Audio embeddings connector
    63	  @ModuleInfo(key: "audio_embeddings_connector") var audioConnector: LTX2Connector1D
    64	
    65	  public init(config: LTX2TextEncoderConfig = LTX2TextEncoderConfig()) {
    66	    self.config = config
    67	
    68	    self.languageModel = LTX2GemmaModel(config: config.gemma)
    69	
    70	    if config.hasPromptAdaLN {
    71	      // LTX-2.3: V2 feature extractor with separate video/audio projections
    72	      self._featureExtractorV2.wrappedValue = LTX2FeatureExtractorV2(
    73	        config: config.featureExtractor)
    74	      self._featureExtractorV1.wrappedValue = nil
    75	    } else {
    76	      // LTX-2: V1 feature extractor with shared projection
    77	      self._featureExtractorV1.wrappedValue = LTX2FeatureExtractorV1(
    78	        inputDim: config.featureExtractor.inputDim,
    79	        outputDim: config.hiddenDim
    80	      )
    81	      self._featureExtractorV2.wrappedValue = nil
    82	    }
    83	
    84	    self._videoConnector.wrappedValue = LTX2Connector1D(config: config.videoConnector)
    85	    self._audioConnector.wrappedValue = LTX2Connector1D(config: config.audioConnector)
    86	  }
    87	
    88	  /// Encode a tokenized input to video and audio embeddings.
    89	  ///
    90	  /// This is the main encoding function that takes pre-tokenized input
    91	  /// (input IDs and attention mask) and runs the full pipeline.
    92	  ///
    93	  /// - Parameters:
    94	  ///   - inputIds: Token IDs `[B, S]`.
    95	  ///   - attentionMask: Padding mask `[B, S]` (1 = valid, 0 = pad).
    96	  ///   - returnAudioEmbeddings: If true, computes audio embeddings too.
    97	  /// - Returns: `LTX2TextEncoderOutput` with video and audio embeddings.
    98	  public func encode(
    99	    inputIds: MLXArray,
   100	    attentionMask: MLXArray,
   101	    returnAudioEmbeddings: Bool = true
   102	  ) -> LTX2TextEncoderOutput {
   103	    // Step 1: Gemma 3 forward pass — extract ALL hidden states
   104	    let allHiddenStates = languageModel(
   105	      inputIds: inputIds,
   106	      attentionMask: attentionMask,
   107	      outputHiddenStates: true
   108	    )
   109	
   110	    let videoEmbeddings: MLXArray
   111	    let audioEmbeddings: MLXArray
   112	
   113	    if config.hasPromptAdaLN {
   114	      // LTX-2.3: V2 feature extraction with separate video/audio paths
   115	      guard let extractor = featureExtractorV2 else {
   116	        fatalError("V2 feature extractor not initialized for prompt adaln mode")
   117	      }
   118	
   119	      // Video features
   120	      let videoFeatures = extractor.videoFeatures(
   121	        hiddenStates: allHiddenStates,
   122	        attentionMask: attentionMask
   123	      )
   124	
   125	      // Build additive mask for connector
   126	      let additiveMask = buildAdditiveMask(
   127	        attentionMask: attentionMask, dtype: videoFeatures.dtype)
   128	
   129	      let (videoOut, _) = videoConnector(
   130	        hiddenStates: videoFeatures, attentionMask: additiveMask)
   131	      videoEmbeddings = videoOut
   132	
   133	      if returnAudioEmbeddings {
   134	        let audioFeatures = extractor.audioFeatures(
   135	          hiddenStates: allHiddenStates,
   136	          attentionMask: attentionMask
   137	        )
   138	        let audioMask = buildAdditiveMask(
   139	          attentionMask: attentionMask, dtype: audioFeatures.dtype)
   140	        let (audioOut, _) = audioConnector(
   141	          hiddenStates: audioFeatures, attentionMask: audioMask)
   142	        audioEmbeddings = audioOut
   143	      } else {
   144	        audioEmbeddings = MLX.zeros([inputIds.dim(0), inputIds.dim(1), config.audioDim])
   145	      }
   146	
   147	    } else {
   148	      // LTX-2 original: V1 feature extraction with shared features
   149	      guard let extractor = featureExtractorV1 else {
   150	        fatalError("V1 feature extractor not initialized for non-prompt-adaln mode")
   151	      }
   152	
   153	      let features = extractor(
   154	        hiddenStates: allHiddenStates,
   155	        attentionMask: attentionMask
   156	      )
   157	
   158	      let additiveMask = buildAdditiveMask(
   159	        attentionMask: attentionMask, dtype: features.dtype)
   160	
   161	      let (videoOut, _) = videoConnector(
   162	        hiddenStates: features, attentionMask: additiveMask)
   163	      videoEmbeddings = videoOut
   164	
   165	      if returnAudioEmbeddings {
   166	        let (audioOut, _) = audioConnector(
   167	          hiddenStates: features, attentionMask: additiveMask)
   168	        audioEmbeddings = audioOut
   169	      } else {
   170	        audioEmbeddings = MLX.zeros([inputIds.dim(0), inputIds.dim(1), config.audioDim])
   171	      }
   172	    }
   173	
   174	    return LTX2TextEncoderOutput(
   175	      videoEmbeddings: videoEmbeddings,
   176	      audioEmbeddings: audioEmbeddings,
   177	      attentionMask: attentionMask
   178	    )
   179	  }
   180	
   181	  /// Build additive attention mask for the connector.
   182	  ///
   183	  /// Converts binary mask `[B, S]` to additive mask `[B, 1, 1, S]`:
   184	  /// - 1 -> 0 (attend)
   185	  /// - 0 -> -1e9 (mask)
   186	  private func buildAdditiveMask(
   187	    attentionMask: MLXArray, dtype: DType
   188	  ) -> MLXArray {
   189	    let additive = (attentionMask.asType(dtype) - 1) * 1e9
   190	    return additive
   191	      .reshaped(attentionMask.dim(0), 1, 1, attentionMask.dim(1))
   192	  }
   193	
   194	  // MARK: - Weight Loading
   195	
   196	  /// Load weights for the complete text encoder.
   197	  ///
   198	  /// Handles two weight layout formats:
   199	  /// 1. Split model: `connector.safetensors` for projection weights
   200	  /// 2. Monolithic: single safetensors at root
   201	  ///
   202	  /// Gemma 3 weights are loaded separately from either:
   203	  /// - MLX community quantized model (e.g. mlx-community/gemma-3-12b-it-4bit)
   204	  /// - Full precision model from HuggingFace cache
   205	  ///
   206	  /// - Parameters:
   207	  ///   - modelPath: Path to the LTX-2 model directory (contains connector.safetensors).
   208	  ///   - textEncoderPath: Path to the Gemma 3 weights directory.
   209	  ///     If nil, looks for `text_encoder/` subdirectory under modelPath.
   210	  public func loadWeights(
   211	    modelPath: URL,
   212	    textEncoderPath: URL? = nil
   213	  ) throws {
   214	    // Load Gemma 3 language model weights
   215	    let gemmaPath: URL
   216	    if let tePath = textEncoderPath {
   217	      let subDir = tePath.appendingPathComponent("text_encoder")
   218	      gemmaPath = FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: subDir.path) ? subDir : tePath
   219	    } else {
   220	      gemmaPath = modelPath.appendingPathComponent("text_encoder")
   221	    }
   222	
   223	    try loadGemmaWeights(from: gemmaPath)
   224	
   225	    // Load connector and feature extractor weights
   226	    try loadProjectionWeights(from: modelPath)
   227	  }
   228	
   229	  /// Load text-encoder weights from a JoyAI-Echo monolithic checkpoint.
   230	  ///
   231	  /// Gemma weights still live in their own directory (`gemmaPath`); the
   232	  /// connectors and aggregate-embed projections come from the already-loaded
   233	  /// monolith tensor dict instead of a separate `connector.safetensors`.
   234	  ///
   235	  /// Echo namespaces the connectors under `model.diffusion_model.` and the
   236	  /// aggregate embeds under a bare `text_embedding_projection.` prefix (no
   237	  /// `connector.` prefix). `normalizeMonolithProjectionKeys` rewrites those into
   238	  /// the bare layout the existing `hasConnectorPrefix: false` loaders expect, so
   239	  /// they are reused unchanged.
   240	  ///
   241	  /// - Parameters:
   242	  ///   - gemmaPath: Path to the Gemma 3 weights directory.
   243	  ///   - monolithTensors: The full Echo checkpoint tensor dict (subset filtered here).
   244	  public func loadWeightsFromMonolith(
   245	    gemmaPath: URL,
   246	    monolithTensors: [String: MLXArray]
   247	  ) throws {
   248	    let resolvedGemma: URL = {
   249	      let subDir = gemmaPath.appendingPathComponent("text_encoder")
   250	      return FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: subDir.path) ? subDir : gemmaPath
     1	// LTX2FeatureExtractor.swift — Gemma 3 hidden state feature extraction
     2	// Phase 2 of the LTX-2 Swift/MLX port
     3	//
     4	// Extracts and combines hidden states from all 49 Gemma 3 layers into
     5	// features suitable for the 1D connector. Two variants:
     6	//
     7	// V1 (LTX-2 original): 8 * (x - mean) / range normalization, single linear projection
     8	// V2 (LTX-2.3): Per-token RMSNorm, rescale normalization, separate video/audio projections
     9	//
    10	// Reference: text_encoder.py classes GemmaFeaturesExtractor, GemmaFeaturesExtractorV2,
    11	//            and functions norm_and_concat_hidden_states, norm_and_concat_per_token_rms
    12	
    13	import Foundation
    14	import MLX
    15	import MLXFast
    16	import MLXNN
    17	
    18	// MARK: - V1 Feature Extractor
    19	
    20	/// V1 feature extractor (LTX-2): 8 * (x - mean) / range normalization + linear projection.
    21	///
    22	/// Process:
    23	/// 1. Stack all hidden states: (B, T, D, L) where L = number of layers
    24	/// 2. Compute masked mean and range per layer
    25	/// 3. Normalize: 8 * (x - mean) / range
    26	/// 4. Flatten: (B, T, D*L)
    27	/// 5. Linear projection: (B, T, D*L) -> (B, T, outputDim)
    28	///
    29	/// Weight key mapping:
    30	/// - `aggregate_embed.weight`
    31	public final class LTX2FeatureExtractorV1: Module {
    32	  @ModuleInfo(key: "aggregate_embed") var aggregateEmbed: Linear
    33	
    34	  public init(inputDim: Int, outputDim: Int) {
    35	    self._aggregateEmbed.wrappedValue = Linear(inputDim, outputDim, bias: false)
    36	  }
    37	
    38	  /// Extract features from stacked hidden states.
    39	  ///
    40	  /// - Parameters:
    41	  ///   - hiddenStates: List of hidden state arrays, each `[B, T, D]`.
    42	  ///   - attentionMask: Binary attention mask `[B, T]` (1 = valid, 0 = pad).
    43	  /// - Returns: Projected features `[B, T, outputDim]`.
    44	  public func callAsFunction(
    45	    hiddenStates: [MLXArray],
    46	    attentionMask: MLXArray
    47	  ) -> MLXArray {
    48	    let normed = LTX2FeatureExtractor.normAndConcatHiddenStates(
    49	      hiddenStates: hiddenStates,
    50	      attentionMask: attentionMask,
    51	      paddingSide: "left"
    52	    )
    53	    return aggregateEmbed(normed)
    54	  }
    55	}
    56	
    57	// MARK: - V2 Feature Extractor
    58	
    59	/// V2 feature extractor (LTX-2.3): per-token RMSNorm + rescale normalization
    60	/// with separate video and audio projection heads.
    61	///
    62	/// Process:
    63	/// 1. Stack hidden states: (B, T, D, L)
    64	/// 2. Per-token RMSNorm across hidden dimension
    65	/// 3. Flatten: (B, T, D*L)
    66	/// 4. Rescale: x * sqrt(target_dim / embedding_dim)
    67	/// 5. Project through video or audio linear head
    68	///
    69	/// Weight key mapping:
    70	/// - `video_aggregate_embed.weight`, `video_aggregate_embed.bias`
    71	/// - `audio_aggregate_embed.weight`, `audio_aggregate_embed.bias`
    72	public final class LTX2FeatureExtractorV2: Module {
    73	  let embeddingDim: Int
    74	
    75	  @ModuleInfo(key: "video_aggregate_embed") var videoAggregateEmbed: Linear
    76	  @ModuleInfo(key: "audio_aggregate_embed") var audioAggregateEmbed: Linear
    77	
    78	  public init(config: LTX2FeatureExtractorConfig) {
    79	    self.embeddingDim = config.embeddingDim
    80	    self._videoAggregateEmbed.wrappedValue = Linear(
    81	      config.inputDim, config.videoOutputDim, bias: config.bias)
    82	    self._audioAggregateEmbed.wrappedValue = Linear(
    83	      config.inputDim, config.audioOutputDim, bias: config.bias)
    84	  }
    85	
    86	  /// Extract video features from hidden states.
    87	  ///
    88	  /// - Parameters:
    89	  ///   - hiddenStates: List of hidden state arrays, each `[B, T, D]`.
    90	  ///   - attentionMask: Binary attention mask `[B, T]` (1 = valid, 0 = pad).
    91	  /// - Returns: Projected video features `[B, T, videoOutputDim]`.
    92	  public func videoFeatures(
    93	    hiddenStates: [MLXArray],
    94	    attentionMask: MLXArray
    95	  ) -> MLXArray {
    96	    let normed = LTX2FeatureExtractor.normAndConcatPerTokenRMS(
    97	      hiddenStates: hiddenStates,
    98	      attentionMask: attentionMask
    99	    )
   100	    let targetDim = videoAggregateEmbed.weight.dim(0)
   101	    let rescaled = LTX2FeatureExtractor.rescaleNorm(
   102	      normed, targetDim: targetDim, sourceDim: embeddingDim)
   103	    return videoAggregateEmbed(rescaled)
   104	  }
   105	
   106	  /// Extract audio features from hidden states.
   107	  ///
   108	  /// - Parameters:
   109	  ///   - hiddenStates: List of hidden state arrays, each `[B, T, D]`.
   110	  ///   - attentionMask: Binary attention mask `[B, T]` (1 = valid, 0 = pad).
   111	  /// - Returns: Projected audio features `[B, T, audioOutputDim]`.
   112	  public func audioFeatures(
   113	    hiddenStates: [MLXArray],
   114	    attentionMask: MLXArray
   115	  ) -> MLXArray {
   116	    let normed = LTX2FeatureExtractor.normAndConcatPerTokenRMS(
   117	      hiddenStates: hiddenStates,
   118	      attentionMask: attentionMask
   119	    )
   120	    let targetDim = audioAggregateEmbed.weight.dim(0)
   121	    let rescaled = LTX2FeatureExtractor.rescaleNorm(
   122	      normed, targetDim: targetDim, sourceDim: embeddingDim)
   123	    return audioAggregateEmbed(rescaled)
   124	  }
   125	}
   126	
   127	// MARK: - Static Normalization Functions
   128	
   129	/// Static helper functions for feature extraction normalization.
   130	public enum LTX2FeatureExtractor {
   131	
   132	  /// V1 normalization: 8 * (x - mean) / range, with masking for padded positions.
   133	  ///
   134	  /// Matches Python `norm_and_concat_hidden_states` function.
   135	  ///
   136	  /// - Parameters:
   137	  ///   - hiddenStates: List of hidden state arrays from Gemma layers.
   138	  ///   - attentionMask: Binary attention mask `[B, T]`.
   139	  ///   - paddingSide: "left" or "right" padding convention.
   140	  /// - Returns: Normalized and concatenated tensor `[B, T, D*L]`.
   141	  public static func normAndConcatHiddenStates(
   142	    hiddenStates: [MLXArray],
   143	    attentionMask: MLXArray,
   144	    paddingSide: String = "left"
   145	  ) -> MLXArray {
   146	    // Stack: (B, T, D, L)
   147	    let stacked = MLX.stacked(hiddenStates, axis: -1)
   148	    let dtype = stacked.dtype
   149	    let b = stacked.dim(0)
   150	    let t = stacked.dim(1)
   151	    let d = stacked.dim(2)
   152	    let numLayers = stacked.dim(3)
   153	
   154	    // Compute sequence lengths from attention mask
   155	    let sequenceLengths = MLX.sum(attentionMask, axis: -1)  // (B,)
   156	
   157	    // Build mask based on padding side
   158	    let tokenIndices = MLXArray(0..<t).expandedDimensions(axis: 0)  // (1, T)
   159	
   160	    let mask: MLXArray
   161	    if paddingSide == "right" {
   162	      mask = tokenIndices .< sequenceLengths.expandedDimensions(axis: 1)
   163	    } else {
   164	      let startIndices = MLXArray(t) - sequenceLengths.expandedDimensions(axis: 1)
   165	      mask = tokenIndices .>= startIndices
   166	    }
   167	
   168	    // (B, T) -> (B, T, 1, 1) for broadcasting
   169	    let mask4D = mask
   170	      .expandedDimensions(axis: 2)
   171	      .expandedDimensions(axis: 3)
   172	    let eps = MLXArray(Float(1e-6)).asType(dtype)
   173	
   174	    // Compute masked mean per layer
   175	    let masked = MLX.where(mask4D, stacked, MLX.zeros(like: stacked))
   176	    let denom = (sequenceLengths * MLXArray(d))
   177	      .reshaped(b, 1, 1, 1)
   178	      .asType(dtype)
   179	    let mean = MLX.sum(masked, axes: [1, 2], keepDims: true) / (denom + eps)
   180	
   181	    // Compute masked min/max per layer
   182	    let posInf = MLX.full(stacked.shape, values: MLXArray(Float.infinity), dtype: dtype)
   183	    let negInf = MLX.full(stacked.shape, values: MLXArray(-Float.infinity), dtype: dtype)
   184	    let xForMin = MLX.where(mask4D, stacked, posInf)
   185	    let xForMax = MLX.where(mask4D, stacked, negInf)
   186	    let xMin = MLX.min(xForMin, axes: [1, 2], keepDims: true)
   187	    let xMax = MLX.max(xForMax, axes: [1, 2], keepDims: true)
   188	    let rangeVal = xMax - xMin
   189	
   190	    // Normalize: 8 * (x - mean) / range
Sources/ZImage/LTX2/TextEncoder/LTX2GemmaModel.swift:134:    attentionMask: MLXArray?,
