   280	/// - `audio_embeddings_connector.learnable_registers`
   281	public final class LTX2Connector1D: Module {
   282	  let config: LTX2ConnectorConfig
   283	
   284	  @ModuleInfo(key: "transformer_1d_blocks") var blocks: [LTX2ConnectorTransformerBlock]
   285	
   286	  /// Learnable register tokens. Plain array, not a Module parameter — must be
   287	  /// loaded manually from weights (not auto-discovered by MLXNN).
   288	  public var learnableRegisters: MLXArray
   289	
   290	  public init(config: LTX2ConnectorConfig) {
   291	    self.config = config
   292	
   293	    self._blocks.wrappedValue = (0..<config.numLayers).map { _ in
   294	      LTX2ConnectorTransformerBlock(config: config)
   295	    }
   296	
   297	    if config.numLearnableRegisters > 0 {
   298	      self.learnableRegisters = MLX.zeros([config.numLearnableRegisters, config.dim])
   299	    } else {
   300	      self.learnableRegisters = MLX.zeros([0, config.dim])
   301	    }
   302	  }
   303	
   304	  /// Forward pass through the 1D connector.
   305	  ///
   306	  /// - Parameters:
   307	  ///   - hiddenStates: Text features `[B, S, dim]`.
   308	  ///   - attentionMask: Optional additive mask `[B, 1, 1, S]` (0 = attend, -1e9 = pad).
   309	  /// - Returns: `(processedHiddenStates, processedMask)` with same shapes.
   310	  public func callAsFunction(
   311	    hiddenStates: MLXArray,
   312	    attentionMask: MLXArray? = nil
   313	  ) -> (MLXArray, MLXArray?) {
   314	    var h = hiddenStates
   315	    var mask = attentionMask
   316	
   317	    // Replace padded tokens with learnable registers
   318	    if config.numLearnableRegisters > 0, let attMask = mask {
   319	      (h, mask) = replacePaddedWithRegisters(hiddenStates: h, attentionMask: attMask)
   320	    }
   321	
   322	    // Compute SPLIT RoPE frequencies
   323	    let seqLen = h.dim(1)
   324	    let pe = precomputeFreqsCIS(seqLen: seqLen, dtype: h.dtype)
   325	
   326	    // Process through transformer blocks
   327	    for block in blocks {
   328	      h = block(h, pe: pe)
   329	    }
   330	
   331	    // Final weight-free RMSNorm
   332	    let dim = h.dim(-1)
   333	    let ones = MLXArray.ones([dim])
   334	    h = MLXFast.rmsNorm(h, weight: ones, eps: 1e-6)
   335	
   336	    return (h, mask)
   337	  }
   338	
   339	  // MARK: - RoPE Frequency Computation
   340	
   341	  /// Compute SPLIT RoPE frequencies for the connector.
   342	  ///
   343	  /// Returns `(cos, sin)` each shaped `[1, numHeads, seqLen, headDim//2]`.
   344	  ///
   345	  /// The frequency computation follows the Python `_precompute_freqs_cis`:
   346	  /// 1. Generate log-spaced indices from 1.0 to theta
   347	  /// 2. Scale by pi/2
   348	  /// 3. Compute fractional positions scaled to [-1, 1]
   349	  /// 4. Outer product: scaled_positions * indices
   350	  /// 5. Pad if needed (when n_elem < dim/2)
   351	  /// 6. cos/sin, reshape to per-head layout
   352	  private func precomputeFreqsCIS(
   353	    seqLen: Int,
   354	    dtype: DType
   355	  ) -> (cos: MLXArray, sin: MLXArray) {
   356	    let dim = config.innerDim  // numHeads * headDim
   357	    let theta = config.positionalEmbeddingTheta
   358	    let maxPos = config.positionalEmbeddingMaxPos
   359	    let nElem = 2 * maxPos.count  // typically 2
   360	
   361	    let numIndices = dim / nElem
   362	
   363	    // Generate log-spaced indices: theta^linspace(0, 1, numIndices) * pi/2
   364	    var indices = [Float](repeating: 0, count: numIndices)
   365	    for i in 0..<numIndices {
   366	      let t = numIndices > 1 ? Float(i) / Float(numIndices - 1) : 0.0
   367	      indices[i] = powf(theta, t) * (Float.pi / 2.0)
   368	    }
   369	
   370	    // Generate positions and scale to [-1, 1]
   371	    var positions = [Float](repeating: 0, count: seqLen)
   372	    for i in 0..<seqLen {
   373	      let fractional = Float(i) / Float(maxPos[0])
   374	      positions[i] = fractional * 2.0 - 1.0
   375	    }
   376	
   377	    // Outer product: scaled_positions * indices -> (seqLen, numIndices)
   378	    var freqs = [Float](repeating: 0, count: seqLen * numIndices)
   379	    for i in 0..<seqLen {
   380	      for j in 0..<numIndices {
   381	        freqs[i * numIndices + j] = positions[i] * indices[j]
   382	      }
   383	    }
   384	
   385	    // cos/sin
   386	    let expectedFreqs = dim / 2
   387	    let padSize = expectedFreqs - numIndices
   388	
   389	    var cosFreqs = [Float](repeating: 0, count: seqLen * expectedFreqs)
   390	    var sinFreqs = [Float](repeating: 0, count: seqLen * expectedFreqs)
   391	
   392	    for i in 0..<seqLen {
   393	      // Padding at the front (cos = 1, sin = 0)
   394	      for j in 0..<padSize {
   395	        cosFreqs[i * expectedFreqs + j] = 1.0
   396	        sinFreqs[i * expectedFreqs + j] = 0.0
   397	      }
   398	      // Computed frequencies
   399	      for j in 0..<numIndices {
   400	        let f = freqs[i * numIndices + j]
   401	        cosFreqs[i * expectedFreqs + padSize + j] = cosf(f)
   402	        sinFreqs[i * expectedFreqs + padSize + j] = sinf(f)
   403	      }
   404	    }
   405	
   406	    // Reshape: (seqLen, expectedFreqs) -> (seqLen, numHeads, headDim/2) -> (1, numHeads, seqLen, headDim/2)
   407	    let halfHeadDim = config.headDim / 2
   408	    let cosArr = MLXArray(cosFreqs, [seqLen, config.numHeads, halfHeadDim])
   409	    let sinArr = MLXArray(sinFreqs, [seqLen, config.numHeads, halfHeadDim])
   410	
   411	    // Transpose: (S, H, D/2) -> (H, S, D/2) -> (1, H, S, D/2)
   412	    let cosFinal = cosArr.transposed(1, 0, 2).expandedDimensions(axis: 0).asType(dtype)
   413	    let sinFinal = sinArr.transposed(1, 0, 2).expandedDimensions(axis: 0).asType(dtype)
   414	
   415	    return (cosFinal, sinFinal)
   416	  }
   417	
   418	  // MARK: - Register Replacement
   419	
   420	  /// Replace padded positions with learnable register tokens.
   421	  ///
   422	  /// For left-padded input, valid tokens are at the end. This function:
   423	  /// 1. Moves valid tokens to the front
   424	  /// 2. Fills remaining positions with tiled register tokens
   425	  /// 3. Resets the attention mask to all-zeros (no masking needed)
   426	  ///
   427	  /// Matches Python `_replace_padded_with_registers`.
   428	  private func replacePaddedWithRegisters(
   429	    hiddenStates: MLXArray,
   430	    attentionMask: MLXArray
   431	  ) -> (MLXArray, MLXArray?) {
   432	    let batchSize = hiddenStates.dim(0)
   433	    let seqLen = hiddenStates.dim(1)
   434	    let dim = hiddenStates.dim(2)
   435	    let dtype = hiddenStates.dtype
   436	
   437	    // Binary mask: 1 for valid, 0 for padded
   438	    // attentionMask is additive: 0 for valid, large negative for padded
   439	    let maskSquashed = attentionMask.squeezed(axis: 1).squeezed(axis: 1)  // (B, S)
   440	    let maskBinary = (maskSquashed .>= MLXArray(Float(-9000.0))).asType(.int32)
   441	
   442	    // Tile registers to fill sequence length
   443	    let numTiles = seqLen / config.numLearnableRegisters
   444	    let registers = MLX.tiled(learnableRegisters, repetitions: [numTiles, 1])
   445	      .asType(dtype)  // (seqLen, dim)
   446	
   447	    // Process each batch item
   448	    var resultList: [MLXArray] = []
   449	    for b in 0..<batchSize {
   450	      let maskB = maskBinary[b]  // (S,)
   451	      let hsB = hiddenStates[b]  // (S, dim)
   452	
   453	      let numValid = Int(MLX.sum(maskB).item(Int.self))
   454	      let padLength = seqLen - numValid
   455	
   456	      // Valid tokens are at the end (left-padded)
   457	      let validTokens = hsB[(seqLen - numValid)...]  // (numValid, dim)
   458	
   459	      // Build: valid tokens at front, registers at back
   460	      let adjusted: MLXArray
   461	      if padLength > 0 {
   462	        let padding = MLX.zeros([padLength, dim], dtype: dtype)
   463	        adjusted = MLX.concatenated([validTokens, padding], axis: 0)
   464	      } else {
   465	        adjusted = validTokens
   466	      }
   467	
   468	      // Flipped mask: 1s at front (valid), 0s at back (register positions)
   469	      let flippedMask: MLXArray
   470	      if padLength > 0 {
   471	        flippedMask = MLX.concatenated([
   472	          MLX.ones([numValid], dtype: .int32),
   473	          MLX.zeros([padLength], dtype: .int32),
   474	        ], axis: 0)
   475	      } else {
   476	        flippedMask = MLX.ones([seqLen], dtype: .int32)
   477	      }
   478	
   479	      // Combine: valid tokens where mask=1, registers where mask=0
   480	      let flippedMaskExpanded = flippedMask.expandedDimensions(axis: 1).asType(dtype)
   481	      let combined = flippedMaskExpanded * adjusted + (1 - flippedMaskExpanded) * registers
   482	      resultList.append(combined)
   483	    }
   484	
   485	    let result = MLX.stacked(resultList, axis: 0)
   486	
   487	    // Reset attention mask to all zeros (no masking after register replacement)
   488	    let newMask = MLX.zeros(like: attentionMask)
   489	
   490	    return (result, newMask)
   491	  }
   492	}
   190	    // Normalize: 8 * (x - mean) / range
   191	    let normed = 8 * (stacked - mean) / (rangeVal + eps)
   192	
   193	    // Flatten layers into feature dimension: (B, T, D*L)
   194	    var result = normed.reshaped(b, t, d * numLayers)
   195	
   196	    // Zero out padded positions
   197	    let maskFlat = MLX.broadcast(
   198	      mask.expandedDimensions(axis: 2),
   199	      to: [b, t, d * numLayers]
   200	    )
   201	    result = MLX.where(maskFlat, result, MLX.zeros(like: result))
   202	
   203	    return result
   204	  }
   205	
   206	  /// V2 normalization: Per-token RMSNorm across hidden dimension.
   207	  ///
   208	  /// Matches Python `norm_and_concat_per_token_rms` function.
   209	  ///
   210	  /// - Parameters:
   211	  ///   - hiddenStates: List of hidden state arrays from Gemma layers.
   212	  ///   - attentionMask: Binary attention mask `[B, T]`.
   213	  /// - Returns: Normalized and concatenated tensor `[B, T, D*L]`.
   214	  public static func normAndConcatPerTokenRMS(
   215	    hiddenStates: [MLXArray],
   216	    attentionMask: MLXArray
   217	  ) -> MLXArray {
   218	    // Stack: (B, T, D, L)
   219	    let encoded = MLX.stacked(hiddenStates, axis: -1)
   220	    let dtype = encoded.dtype
   221	    let b = encoded.dim(0)
   222	    let t = encoded.dim(1)
   223	    let d = encoded.dim(2)
   224	    let numLayers = encoded.dim(3)
   225	
   226	    // Per-token RMSNorm across hidden dimension: variance = mean(x^2) over dim D
   227	    let encodedF32 = encoded.asType(.float32)
   228	    let variance = MLX.mean(encodedF32 * encodedF32, axis: 2, keepDims: true)  // (B, T, 1, L)
   229	    let normed = (encodedF32 * MLX.rsqrt(variance + MLXArray(Float(1e-6)))).asType(dtype)
   230	
   231	    // Flatten layers: (B, T, D*L)
   232	    var result = normed.reshaped(b, t, d * numLayers)
   233	
   234	    // Zero out padded positions
   235	    let mask3D = attentionMask.expandedDimensions(axis: 2)  // (B, T, 1)
   236	    let maskBool = mask3D .> MLXArray(Float(0))
   237	    result = MLX.where(
   238	      MLX.broadcast(maskBool, to: result.shape),
   239	      result,
   240	      MLX.zeros(like: result)
   241	    )
   242	
   243	    return result
   244	  }
   245	
   246	  /// Rescale normalization: x * sqrt(target_dim / source_dim).
   247	  ///
   248	  /// Used in V2 feature extraction to compensate for dimension changes.
   249	  public static func rescaleNorm(
   250	    _ x: MLXArray, targetDim: Int, sourceDim: Int
   251	  ) -> MLXArray {
   252	    let scale = Float(targetDim) / Float(sourceDim)
   253	    return x * MLXArray(scale.squareRoot())
   254	  }
   255	}
   330	      scaleFactor: 1.0
   331	    )
   332	    self.rotaryEmbGlobal = Gemma3RoPE(
   333	      dim: config.headDim,
   334	      base: 10_000.0,
   335	      scaleFactor: 8.0  // rope_scaling.factor from config
   336	    )
   337	  }
   338	
   339	  /// Forward pass through the full Gemma 3 model.
   340	  ///
   341	  /// - Parameters:
   342	  ///   - inputIds: Token IDs `[B, S]`.
   343	  ///   - attentionMask: Padding mask `[B, S]` (1 = attend, 0 = pad).
   344	  ///   - outputHiddenStates: When true, returns all 49 hidden states.
   345	  /// - Returns: Array of hidden states `[embedding, layer0, ..., layer47]` where
   346	  ///   the final entry has the final RMSNorm applied. Each tensor is `[B, S, 3840]`.
   347	  public func callAsFunction(
   348	    inputIds: MLXArray,
   349	    attentionMask: MLXArray,
   350	    outputHiddenStates: Bool = true
   351	  ) -> [MLXArray] {
   352	    let seqLen = inputIds.dim(1)
   353	
   354	    // Token embedding with Gemma scaling (sqrt(hidden_size))
   355	    var h = embedTokens(inputIds)
   356	    let scaleFactor = MLXArray(Float(config.hiddenSize).squareRoot()).asType(h.dtype)
   357	    h = h * scaleFactor
   358	    eval(h)
   359	
   360	    // Build causal + padding attention mask
   361	    let fullCausalMask = LTX2GemmaModel.buildCausalMask(
   362	      seqLen: seqLen,
   363	      attentionMask: attentionMask,
   364	      dtype: h.dtype
   365	    )
   366	
   367	    // Pre-compute both RoPE variants
   368	    let globalCosSin = rotaryEmbGlobal(seqLen)
   369	    let localCosSin = rotaryEmbLocal(seqLen)
   370	
   371	    // Collect hidden states
   372	    var hiddenStatesList: [MLXArray] = outputHiddenStates ? [h] : []
   373	
   374	    let numLayers = layers.count
   375	    for (i, layer) in layers.enumerated() {
   376	      // Gemma 3 sliding window pattern:
   377	      // Every 6th layer (indices 5, 11, 17, ...) uses full attention + global RoPE
   378	      // All other layers use sliding window attention + local RoPE
   379	      let isGlobal = (i % config.slidingWindowPattern == config.slidingWindowPattern - 1)
   380	      let cosSin = isGlobal ? globalCosSin : localCosSin
   381	
   382	      h = layer(
   383	        hiddenStates: h,
   384	        attentionMask: fullCausalMask,
   385	        cosSin: cosSin
   386	      )
   387	      eval(h)
   388	
   389	      if outputHiddenStates && i < numLayers - 1 {
   390	        hiddenStatesList.append(h)
   391	      }
   392	    }
   393	
   394	    // Apply final RMSNorm
   395	    h = norm(h)
   396	    eval(h)
   397	
   398	    if outputHiddenStates {
   399	      hiddenStatesList.append(h)
   400	      return hiddenStatesList
   401	    }
   402	
   403	    return [h]
   404	  }
   405	
   406	  // MARK: - Attention Mask
   407	
   408	  /// Build a 4D causal + padding attention mask.
   409	  public static func buildCausalMask(
   410	    seqLen: Int,
   411	    attentionMask: MLXArray,
   412	    dtype: DType
   413	  ) -> MLXArray {
   414	    let batchSize = attentionMask.dim(0)
   415	    let minVal: Float = (dtype == .float16 || dtype == .bfloat16) ? -65504.0 : -1e9
   416	    let maskDtype: DType = .float32
   417	
   418	    // Padding mask
   419	    let keepMask = attentionMask .== MLXArray(1).asType(attentionMask.dtype)
   420	    let padOnes = MLX.zeros(attentionMask.shape, dtype: maskDtype)
   421	    let padNegInf = MLX.full(attentionMask.shape, values: MLXArray(minVal), dtype: maskDtype)
   422	    var paddingMask = MLX.where(keepMask, padOnes, padNegInf)
   423	    paddingMask = paddingMask.expandedDimensions(axis: 1).expandedDimensions(axis: 1)
   424	
   425	    // Causal mask
   426	    let idx = MLXArray(0..<seqLen).asType(.int32)
   427	    let j = idx.expandedDimensions(axis: 0)
   428	    let i = idx.expandedDimensions(axis: 1)
   429	    let triBool = j .> i
   430	    let zeros2D = MLX.zeros([seqLen, seqLen], dtype: maskDtype)
   431	    let negInf2D = MLX.full([seqLen, seqLen], values: MLXArray(minVal), dtype: maskDtype)
   432	    var causalMask = MLX.where(triBool, negInf2D, zeros2D)
   433	    causalMask = causalMask.expandedDimensions(axis: 0).expandedDimensions(axis: 0)
   434	    causalMask = MLX.broadcast(causalMask, to: [batchSize, 1, seqLen, seqLen])
   435	
   436	    return (causalMask + paddingMask).asType(dtype)
   437	  }
   438	
   439	  // MARK: - Weight Loading
   440	
   441	  /// Sanitize weight keys from safetensors format.
   442	  public static func sanitizeWeights(_ weights: [String: MLXArray]) -> [String: MLXArray] {
   443	    var sanitized: [String: MLXArray] = [:]
   444	    for (key, value) in weights {
   445	      var newKey: String? = nil

   152	  // MARK: - Text-to-Video
   153	
   154	  /// Generate a video from a text prompt.
   155	  ///
   156	  /// - Parameters:
   157	  ///   - inputIds: Pre-tokenized input IDs `[B, S]`.
   158	  ///   - attentionMask: Attention mask `[B, S]`.
   159	  ///   - width: Output width in pixels (must be divisible by 32).
   160	  ///   - height: Output height in pixels (must be divisible by 32).
   161	  ///   - numFrames: Number of output frames. Must be `1 + 8k`.
   162	  ///   - steps: Number of denoising steps.
   163	  ///   - seed: Optional random seed for reproducibility.
   164	  ///   - guidance: CFG guidance scale. Overrides config if provided.
   165	  ///   - negativeInputIds: Negative prompt token IDs for CFG.
   166	  ///   - negativeAttentionMask: Negative prompt attention mask.
   167	  ///   - progressCallback: Called after each denoising step with `(currentStep, totalSteps)`.
   168	  /// - Returns: Pipeline output with decoded frames.
   169	  public func generateT2V(
   170	    inputIds: MLXArray,
   171	    attentionMask: MLXArray,
   172	    width: Int,
   173	    height: Int,
   174	    numFrames: Int,
   175	    steps: Int,
   176	    seed: UInt64? = nil,
   177	    guidance: Float? = nil,
   178	    negativeInputIds: MLXArray? = nil,
   179	    negativeAttentionMask: MLXArray? = nil,
   180	    audioSeconds: Float? = nil,
   181	    progressCallback: ((Int, Int) -> Void)? = nil
   182	  ) -> LTX2PipelineOutput {
   183	    let startTime = CFAbsoluteTimeGetCurrent()
   184	    let cfgScale = guidance ?? config.guidance
   185	
   186	    // Validate frame count
   187	    precondition((numFrames - 1) % 8 == 0, "numFrames must be 1 + 8k (got \(numFrames))")
   188	    precondition(width % spatialCompression == 0, "width must be divisible by \(spatialCompression)")
   189	    precondition(height % spatialCompression == 0, "height must be divisible by \(spatialCompression)")
   190	
   191	    logger.info("T2V generation: \(width)x\(height), \(numFrames) frames, \(steps) steps")
   192	
   193	    // Step 1: Encode prompt
   194	    logger.info("Encoding prompt...")
   195	    let wantAudio = audioSeconds != nil && transformer.hasAudio
   196	    let textOutput = textEncoder.encode(
   197	      inputIds: inputIds,
   198	      attentionMask: attentionMask,
   199	      returnAudioEmbeddings: wantAudio
   200	    )
   201	    eval(textOutput.videoEmbeddings)
   202	
   203	    // Encode negative prompt for CFG, or for CFG++ (needs it every step at cfg=1).
   204	    var negativeEmbeddings: MLXArray? = nil
   205	    var negativeAudioEmbeddings: MLXArray? = nil
   206	    if cfgScale > 1.0 || resolvedConfig.samplerIsCfgPP,
   207	       let negIds = negativeInputIds, let negMask = negativeAttentionMask {
   208	      logger.info("Encoding negative prompt (scale=\(cfgScale), cfgPP=\(resolvedConfig.samplerIsCfgPP))...")
   209	      let negOutput = textEncoder.encode(
   210	        inputIds: negIds,
   211	        attentionMask: negMask,
   212	        returnAudioEmbeddings: wantAudio
   213	      )
   214	      negativeEmbeddings = negOutput.videoEmbeddings
   215	      if wantAudio { negativeAudioEmbeddings = negOutput.audioEmbeddings }
   216	      eval(negativeEmbeddings!)
   217	    }
   218	
   219	    // NAG conditioning. The reference wires this as a separate model-patch
   220	    // input (`LTX2_NAG {nag_cond_video}`), NOT as the CFG negative — in that
   221	    // recipe CFG sits at 1.0 so the CFG negative is inert. With no dedicated
   222	    // NAG prompt we reuse the negative embeddings (same text in practice),
   223	    // encoding them here if CFG/CFG++ didn't already.
   224	    let nagConfig = resolvedConfig.nagConfig ?? .disabled
   225	    var nagEmbeddings: MLXArray? = nil
   226	    if nagConfig.isEnabled {
   227	      if let negIds = negativeInputIds, let negMask = negativeAttentionMask {
   228	        if let already = negativeEmbeddings {
   229	          nagEmbeddings = already
   230	        } else {
   231	          let out = textEncoder.encode(
   232	            inputIds: negIds, attentionMask: negMask, returnAudioEmbeddings: false)
   233	          eval(out.videoEmbeddings)
   234	          nagEmbeddings = out.videoEmbeddings
   235	        }
   236	        logger.info("NAG enabled (scale \(nagConfig.scale), alpha \(nagConfig.alpha), tau \(nagConfig.tau)).")
   237	      } else {
   238	        logger.warning("NAG configured but no negative prompt supplied — NAG requires a negative conditioning; skipping.")
   239	      }
   240	    }
   241	
   242	    // Step 2: Compute latent dimensions
   243	    let latH = height / spatialCompression
   244	    let latW = width / spatialCompression
   245	    let latF = (numFrames - 1) / temporalCompression + 1
   246	
   247	    logger.info("Latent dimensions: \(latF) frames x \(latH) x \(latW)")
   248	
   249	    // Step 3: Create initial noise
   250	    if let seed = seed {
   251	      MLXRandom.seed(seed)
   252	    }
   253	
   254	    let sigmas = getSigmaSchedule(steps: steps, latF: latF, latH: latH, latW: latW)
   255	
   256	    // Scale initial noise by sigma_max
   257	    let noise = MLXRandom.normal([1, 128, latF, latH, latW], dtype: .float32)
   258	    var latents = noise * MLXArray(sigmas[0])
   259	    eval(latents)
   260	
   261	    // Step 4: Build position grid for RoPE
   262	    let positions = createPositionGrid(
   263	      batchSize: 1, latF: latF, latH: latH, latW: latW
   264	    )
   265	
   266	    // Step 5: Precompute RoPE
   267	    let precomputedPE = ltx2PrecomputeFreqsCIS(
   268	      indicesGrid: positions,
   269	      dim: innerDim,
   270	      theta: transformer.positionalEmbeddingTheta,
   271	      maxPos: transformer.positionalEmbeddingMaxPos,
   272	      useMiddleIndicesGrid: transformer.useMiddleIndicesGrid,
   273	      numAttentionHeads: transformer.numHeads,
   274	      ropeMode: transformer.ropeMode,
   275	      doublePrecision: transformer.doublePrecisionRoPE
   276	    )
   277	    eval(precomputedPE.cos, precomputedPE.sin)
   278	
   279	    // Step 5b: Audio stream setup (task #21). Isolated RNG key so video
   280	    // renders are bit-identical with audio on/off; latent rate 25/s
   281	    // (sampleRate / (hop * downsample) = 16000/640).
   282	    var avState: LTX2AVDenoiseState? = nil
   283	    if let seconds = audioSeconds, wantAudio {
   284	      let ta = max(1, Int((seconds * 25).rounded(.up)))
   285	      // Seeded: isolated key (video bit-identical with audio on/off).
   286	      // Unseeded: global stream, so audio noise varies like everything else
   287	      // (Codex #9: keying off seed-0 froze unseeded audio noise).
   288	      let audioKey = seed.map { MLXRandom.key($0 &+ 0xA0D10) }
   289	      let audioNoise = MLXRandom.normal([1, 8, ta, 16], key: audioKey).asType(.float32)
   290	      let audioInit = audioNoise * MLXArray(sigmas[0])
   291	      let (_, audioCoords) = LTX2AudioPatchifier.patchify(audioInit)
   292	      let avPE = transformer.precomputeAVPositionalEmbeddings(
   293	        positions: positions, audioCoords: audioCoords)
   294	      avState = LTX2AVDenoiseState(
   295	        audioLatents: audioInit,
   296	        audioContext: textOutput.audioEmbeddings,
   297	        pe: avPE,
   298	        negativeAudioContext: negativeAudioEmbeddings,
   299	        audioNoiseKey: seed.map { MLXRandom.key($0 &+ 0xA0D12) })
   300	      logger.info("Audio stream enabled: \(ta) latent frames (\(seconds)s, negatives \(negativeAudioEmbeddings != nil ? "on" : "off")).")
   301	    }
   302	
   303	    // Step 6: Denoising loop
   304	    logger.info("Denoising (\(config.sampler) sampler, \(sigmas.count - 1) steps)...")
   305	    latents = denoisingLoop(
   306	      latents: latents,
   307	      positions: positions,
   308	      precomputedPE: precomputedPE,
   309	      textEmbeddings: textOutput.videoEmbeddings,
   310	      negativeEmbeddings: negativeEmbeddings,
   311	      sigmas: sigmas,
   312	      cfgScale: cfgScale,
   313	      state: nil,
   314	      nagEmbeddings: nagEmbeddings, nag: nagConfig,
   315	      avState: avState,
   316	      progressCallback: progressCallback
   317	    )
   318	
   319	    // Step 6b: Two-stage refine (env LTX2_TWO_STAGE) — identical machinery to the
   320	    // i2v refine, minus the frame-0 identity re-anchor (t2v has no source frame,
   321	    // so the refine runs free with state:nil). Upsample x2 -> flow re-noise ->
   322	    // short deterministic refine denoise.
   323	    if let ups = self.upsampler, resolvedConfig.twoStage {
   324	      MLX.GPU.clearCache()
   325	      // Gate-skip decode fix (2026-08-05): check BEFORE upsampling — see
   326	      // applyTwoStageRefine. Above the gate, keep base latents (native size).
   327	      let t2vPreVolume = latents.dim(2) * (latH * 2) * (latW * 2)
   328	      let t2vRefineMax = resolvedConfig.refineMaxVol
   329	      if t2vPreVolume > t2vRefineMax,
   330	         ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_REFINE_UPSCALE_ON_SKIP"] != "1" {
   331	        logger.info("T2V two-stage refine: SKIPPED entirely (volume \(t2vPreVolume) > \(t2vRefineMax)) — native-size decode (gate-skip fix 2026-08-05).")
   332	      } else {
   333	      logger.info("T2V two-stage refine: upsampling latents 2x...")
   334	      let stats = vae.decoder.perChannelStatistics
   335	      let upLatent = stats.normalize(ups(stats.unNormalize(latents.asType(.float32)))).asType(.float32)
   336	      eval(upLatent)
   337	      let t2vRefineVolume = upLatent.dim(2) * (latH * 2) * (latW * 2)
   338	      if t2vRefineVolume > t2vRefineMax {
   339	        logger.info("T2V two-stage refine: SKIPPED denoise (volume \(t2vRefineVolume) > \(t2vRefineMax)) — decoding upsampled latent directly (LTX2_REFINE_UPSCALE_ON_SKIP path).")
   340	        latents = upLatent
   341	      } else {
   342	      let rLatH = latH * 2, rLatW = latW * 2
   343	      let refineSigmas: [Float] = resolvedConfig.refineSigmasEffective
   344	      if let seed = seed { MLXRandom.seed(seed &+ 1000) }
   345	      let refNoise = MLXRandom.normal(upLatent.shape).asType(.float32)
   346	      let sigma0 = refineSigmas[0]
   347	      let refineInit = MLXArray(1 - sigma0) * upLatent + refNoise * MLXArray(sigma0)  // flow re-noise
   348	      // AUDIO BYPASSES PASS 2 (final listen verdict, Todd 2026-08-05:
   349	      // "refined is not better — it picks up and enhances ambient [noise]",
   350	      // agreeing with PinkCherry HF #8 and the grit metric; matched-seed
   351	      // pairs audio-ab-t2v-* / audio-ab-speech-*). Audio keeps its fully
   352	      // denoised stage-1 track; video still refines. LTX2_AUDIO_REFINE=1
   353	      // re-enables the joint refine for A/B.
   354	      let refineAudio = ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_AUDIO_REFINE"] == "1"
   355	      if refineAudio, let av = avState {
   356	        let aKey = seed.map { MLXRandom.key($0 &+ 0xA0D11) }
   357	        let aNoise = MLXRandom.normal(av.audioLatents.shape, key: aKey).asType(.float32)
   358	        av.audioLatents = MLXArray(1 - sigma0) * av.audioLatents + aNoise * MLXArray(sigma0)
   359	      }
   360	      let refinePos = createPositionGrid(batchSize: 1, latF: latF, latH: rLatH, latW: rLatW)
   361	      let refinePE = ltx2PrecomputeFreqsCIS(
   362	        indicesGrid: refinePos, dim: innerDim,
   363	        theta: transformer.positionalEmbeddingTheta,
   364	        maxPos: transformer.positionalEmbeddingMaxPos,
   365	        useMiddleIndicesGrid: transformer.useMiddleIndicesGrid,
   366	        numAttentionHeads: transformer.numHeads,
   367	        ropeMode: transformer.ropeMode,
   368	        doublePrecision: transformer.doublePrecisionRoPE)
   369	      eval(refinePE.cos, refinePE.sin)
   370	      logger.info("T2V two-stage refine: denoising at \(rLatW * spatialCompression)x\(rLatH * spatialCompression), \(refineSigmas.count - 1) steps...")
   371	      // Refine PEs change with the upsampled grid; rebuild the AV bundle too
   372	      // (audio-refine A/B path only — default keeps stage-1 audio untouched).
   373	      var refineAVState: LTX2AVDenoiseState? = nil
   374	      if refineAudio, let av = avState {
   375	        let (_, aCoords) = LTX2AudioPatchifier.patchify(av.audioLatents)
   376	        let avPE2 = transformer.precomputeAVPositionalEmbeddings(
   377	          positions: refinePos, audioCoords: aCoords)
   378	        refineAVState = LTX2AVDenoiseState(
   379	          audioLatents: av.audioLatents, audioContext: av.audioContext, pe: avPE2)
   380	      }
   381	      latents = denoisingLoop(
   382	        latents: refineInit, positions: refinePos, precomputedPE: refinePE,
   383	        textEmbeddings: textOutput.videoEmbeddings, negativeEmbeddings: negativeEmbeddings,
   384	        sigmas: refineSigmas, cfgScale: cfgScale, state: nil, nagEmbeddings: nagEmbeddings, nag: nagConfig,
   385	        forceDeterministic: ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_REFINE_DETERMINISTIC"] != "0",
   386	        avState: refineAVState,
   387	        progressCallback: progressCallback)
   388	      if refineAudio, let av = avState, let rav = refineAVState {
   389	        av.audioLatents = rav.audioLatents
   390	      }
   391	      eval(latents)
   392	      MLX.GPU.clearCache()
   393	      logger.info("T2V two-stage refine complete.")
   394	      }
   395	      }
   396	    }
   397	
   398	    // Step 7: Decode latents via VAE
   399	    logger.info("Decoding latents via VAE...")
   400	    let decoded = decodeAdaptive(latents)
   401	    eval(decoded)
   402	
   403	    // Convert from [-1, 1] to [0, 1] range (VAE outputs centered at 0)
   404	    let rescaled = (decoded.asType(.float32) + 1.0) / 2.0
   405	    let clamped = MLX.clip(rescaled, min: 0, max: 1)
   406	    eval(clamped)
   407	
   408	    let elapsed = CFAbsoluteTimeGetCurrent() - startTime
   409	    logger.info("Generation complete in \(String(format: "%.1f", elapsed))s")
   410	
   411	    var output = LTX2PipelineOutput(
   412	      decoded: clamped,
   413	      numFrames: numFrames,
   414	      width: width,
   415	      height: height,
   416	      elapsedSeconds: elapsed
   417	    )
   418	    output.audioLatents = avState?.audioLatents
   419	    return output
   420	  }
   421	
   422	  // MARK: - Text-to-Video with Pre-computed Embeddings
   423	
   424	  /// Generate a video from pre-computed text embeddings, bypassing the text encoder.
   425	  ///
   426	  /// Use this when you want to supply your own embeddings (e.g. from a cached
   427	  /// encoder run, or dummy embeddings for pipeline testing) without loading
   428	  /// the Gemma 3 text encoder weights.
   429	  ///
   430	  /// - Parameters:
   550	  ///
   551	  /// - Parameters:
   552	  ///   - inputIds: Pre-tokenized prompt IDs `[B, S]`.
   553	  ///   - attentionMask: Attention mask `[B, S]`.
   554	  ///   - image: Input image tensor `(1, 3, H, W)` in float32 [0, 1].
   555	  ///   - strength: Motion strength (0.0 = static, 1.0 = full motion). Default 1.0.
   556	  ///   - width: Output width in pixels.
   557	  ///   - height: Output height in pixels.
   558	  ///   - numFrames: Number of output frames. Must be `1 + 8k`.
   559	  ///   - steps: Number of denoising steps.
   560	  ///   - seed: Optional random seed.
   561	  ///   - guidance: CFG guidance scale.
   562	  ///   - negativeInputIds: Negative prompt token IDs for CFG.
   563	  ///   - negativeAttentionMask: Negative prompt attention mask.
   564	  ///   - progressCallback: Called after each step.
   565	  /// - Returns: Pipeline output with decoded frames.
   566	  public func generateI2V(
   567	    inputIds: MLXArray,
   568	    attentionMask: MLXArray,
   569	    image: MLXArray,
   570	    strength: Float = 1.0,
   571	    width: Int,
   572	    height: Int,
   573	    numFrames: Int,
   574	    steps: Int,
   575	    seed: UInt64? = nil,
   576	    guidance: Float? = nil,
   577	    negativeInputIds: MLXArray? = nil,
   578	    negativeAttentionMask: MLXArray? = nil,
   579	    faceAnchorMask: MLXArray? = nil,
   580	    faceAnchorStrength: Float = 0,
   581	    refineAnchorImage: MLXArray? = nil,
   582	    audioSeconds: Float? = nil,
   583	    progressCallback: ((Int, Int) -> Void)? = nil
   584	  ) -> LTX2PipelineOutput {
   585	    let startTime = CFAbsoluteTimeGetCurrent()
   586	    let cfgScale = guidance ?? config.guidance
   587	
   588	    precondition((numFrames - 1) % 8 == 0, "numFrames must be 1 + 8k (got \(numFrames))")
   589	
   590	    logger.info("I2V generation: \(width)x\(height), \(numFrames) frames, strength=\(strength)")
   591	
   592	    // Step 1: Encode prompt
   593	    logger.info("Encoding prompt...")
   594	    let wantAudio = audioSeconds != nil && transformer.hasAudio
   595	    let textOutput = textEncoder.encode(
   596	      inputIds: inputIds,
   597	      attentionMask: attentionMask,
   598	      returnAudioEmbeddings: wantAudio
   599	    )
   600	    eval(textOutput.videoEmbeddings)
   601	
   602	    var negativeEmbeddings: MLXArray? = nil
   603	    var negativeAudioEmbeddings: MLXArray? = nil
   604	    // CFG++ samplers need the negative embeddings every step even at cfg=1.
   605	    if cfgScale > 1.0 || resolvedConfig.samplerIsCfgPP,
   606	       let negIds = negativeInputIds, let negMask = negativeAttentionMask {
   607	      let negOutput = textEncoder.encode(
   608	        inputIds: negIds, attentionMask: negMask, returnAudioEmbeddings: wantAudio
   609	      )
   610	      negativeEmbeddings = negOutput.videoEmbeddings
   611	      if wantAudio { negativeAudioEmbeddings = negOutput.audioEmbeddings }
   612	      eval(negativeEmbeddings!)
   613	    }
   614	
   615	    // NAG conditioning — see generateT2V for the rationale. The reference
   616	    // recipe is i2v-centric, so this path matters at least as much.
   617	    let nagConfig = resolvedConfig.nagConfig ?? .disabled
   618	    var nagEmbeddings: MLXArray? = nil
   619	    if nagConfig.isEnabled {
   620	      if let negIds = negativeInputIds, let negMask = negativeAttentionMask {
   621	        if let already = negativeEmbeddings {
   622	          nagEmbeddings = already
   623	        } else {
   624	          let out = textEncoder.encode(
   625	            inputIds: negIds, attentionMask: negMask, returnAudioEmbeddings: false)
   626	          eval(out.videoEmbeddings)
   627	          nagEmbeddings = out.videoEmbeddings
   628	        }
   629	        logger.info("NAG enabled (scale \(nagConfig.scale), alpha \(nagConfig.alpha), tau \(nagConfig.tau)).")
   630	      } else {
   631	        logger.warning("NAG configured but no negative prompt supplied — NAG requires a negative conditioning; skipping.")
   632	      }
   633	    }
   634	
   635	    // Step 2: Encode input image via VAE
   636	    logger.info("Encoding input image via VAE...")
   637	    var imageInput = image
   638	    if imageInput.ndim == 4 {
   639	      imageInput = imageInput.expandedDimensions(axis: 2)
   640	    }
   641	    let imageLatent = vae.encode(imageInput)
   642	    eval(imageLatent)
   643	
   644	    // Step 3: Compute latent dimensions
   645	    let latH = height / spatialCompression
   646	    let latW = width / spatialCompression
   647	    let latF = (numFrames - 1) / temporalCompression + 1
   648	
   649	    // Step 4: Create initial noisy state with I2V conditioning
   650	    if let seed = seed {
   651	      MLXRandom.seed(seed)
   652	    }
   653	
   654	    let sigmas = getSigmaSchedule(steps: steps, latF: latF, latH: latH, latW: latW)
   655	
   656	    // Create initial state
   657	    var state = LTX2Conditioning.createInitialState(
   658	      shape: [1, 128, latF, latH, latW],
   659	      noiseScale: sigmas[0]
   660	    )
   661	
   662	    // Apply image conditioning at frame 0
   663	    let condition = LTX2VideoCondition(
   664	      latent: imageLatent,
   665	      frameIndex: 0,
   666	      strength: strength
   667	    )
   668	    state = LTX2Conditioning.applyConditioning(state: state, conditions: [condition])
   669	    if let fm = faceAnchorMask, faceAnchorStrength > 0 {
   670	      state.faceMask = fm
   671	      state.faceRef = imageLatent  // source-encoded latent = the face identity reference
   672	      state.faceAnchorStrength = faceAnchorStrength
   673	    }
   674	
   675	    // IC-control (union-control IC-LoRA): append the source-encoded latent as
   676	    // extra reference frames at temporal frame_idx 0, FROZEN (mask=1-strength).
   677	    // The merged union-control IC-LoRA makes self-attention lock structure to
   678	    // these reference frames -> coherent motion instead of morph. Env-gated
   679	    // LTX2_IC_CONTROL; MVP uses reference_downscale_factor=1 (no dilation).
   680	    // Mirrors ComfyUI LTXVAddGuide.append_keyframe (frame axis cat) +
   681	    // add_keyframe_index (temporal position -> frame_idx 0, handled in
   682	    // createPositionGrid via refFrames). Slice back to latF before decode.
   683	    var icRefFrames = 0
   684	    // IC-control (identity anchor) defaults ON for i2v: without it the subject
   685	    // morphs into a different person over the render. LTX2_IC_CONTROL=0 disables.
   686	    if resolvedConfig.icControl {
   687	      let refStrength = resolvedConfig.icRefStrength
   688	      icRefFrames = imageLatent.dim(2)
   689	      state.latent = MLX.concatenated([state.latent, imageLatent.asType(state.latent.dtype)], axis: 2)
   690	      state.cleanLatent = MLX.concatenated([state.cleanLatent, imageLatent.asType(state.cleanLatent.dtype)], axis: 2)
   691	      let refMask = MLX.broadcast(
   692	        MLXArray(Float(1.0 - refStrength)).reshaped(1, 1, 1, 1, 1),
   693	        to: [1, 1, icRefFrames, 1, 1])
   694	      state.denoiseMask = MLX.concatenated([state.denoiseMask, refMask], axis: 2)
   695	      logger.info("IC-control: appended \(icRefFrames) reference frame(s) @ strength \(refStrength)")
   696	    }
   697	    eval(state.latent, state.cleanLatent, state.denoiseMask)
   698	
   699	    // Step 5: Build position grid (extended by icRefFrames; ref frames get
   700	    // temporal frame_idx 0 inside createPositionGrid).
   701	    let positions = createPositionGrid(
   702	      batchSize: 1, latF: latF, latH: latH, latW: latW, refFrames: icRefFrames
   703	    )
   704	
   705	    // Precompute RoPE
   706	    let precomputedPE = ltx2PrecomputeFreqsCIS(
   707	      indicesGrid: positions,
   708	      dim: innerDim,
   709	      theta: transformer.positionalEmbeddingTheta,
   710	      maxPos: transformer.positionalEmbeddingMaxPos,
   711	      useMiddleIndicesGrid: transformer.useMiddleIndicesGrid,
   712	      numAttentionHeads: transformer.numHeads,
   713	      ropeMode: transformer.ropeMode,
   714	      doublePrecision: transformer.doublePrecisionRoPE
   715	    )
   716	    eval(precomputedPE.cos, precomputedPE.sin)
   717	
   718	    // Step 5b: Audio stream (task #21 i2v). Same setup as t2v; the per-token
   719	    // av_ca video conditioning inside callAV handles the i2v denoise mask
   720	    // (conditioned tokens at ~0). Cross PEs use the FULL positions grid incl.
   721	    // IC-control ref frames (they sit at t=0s, matching their video PE).
   722	    var avState: LTX2AVDenoiseState? = nil
   723	    if let seconds = audioSeconds, wantAudio {
   724	      let ta = max(1, Int((seconds * 25).rounded(.up)))
   725	      let audioKey = seed.map { MLXRandom.key($0 &+ 0xA0D10) }
   726	      let audioNoise = MLXRandom.normal([1, 8, ta, 16], key: audioKey).asType(.float32)
   727	      let audioInit = audioNoise * MLXArray(sigmas[0])
   728	      let (_, audioCoords) = LTX2AudioPatchifier.patchify(audioInit)
   729	      let avPE = transformer.precomputeAVPositionalEmbeddings(
   730	        positions: positions, audioCoords: audioCoords)
   731	      avState = LTX2AVDenoiseState(
   732	        audioLatents: audioInit,
   733	        audioContext: textOutput.audioEmbeddings,
   734	        pe: avPE,
   735	        negativeAudioContext: negativeAudioEmbeddings,
   736	        audioNoiseKey: seed.map { MLXRandom.key($0 &+ 0xA0D12) })
   737	      logger.info("Audio stream enabled (i2v): \(ta) latent frames (\(seconds)s, negatives \(negativeAudioEmbeddings != nil ? "on" : "off")).")
   738	    }
   739	
   740	    // Step 6: Denoising loop with I2V state
   741	    logger.info("Denoising with I2V conditioning...")
   742	    let latentsAll = denoisingLoop(
   743	      latents: state.latent,
   744	      positions: positions,
   745	      precomputedPE: precomputedPE,
   746	      textEmbeddings: textOutput.videoEmbeddings,
   747	      negativeEmbeddings: negativeEmbeddings,
   748	      sigmas: sigmas,
   749	      cfgScale: cfgScale,
   750	      state: state,
   751	      nagEmbeddings: nagEmbeddings, nag: nagConfig,
   752	      avState: avState,
   753	      progressCallback: progressCallback
   754	    )
   755	
   756	    // Drop the appended IC-control reference frames before decode (keep latF).
   757	    var latents = icRefFrames > 0
   758	      ? latentsAll[0..., 0..., 0..<latF, 0..., 0...]
   759	      : latentsAll
   760	
   761	    // Two-stage refine — shared with continuation chunks (applyTwoStageRefine).
   762	    latents = applyTwoStageRefine(
   763	      latents, latF: latF, latH: latH, latW: latW,
   764	      textEmbeddings: textOutput.videoEmbeddings,
   765	      negativeEmbeddings: negativeEmbeddings,
   766	      cfgScale: cfgScale, seed: seed,
   767	      refineAnchorImage: refineAnchorImage,
   768	      avState: avState,
   769	      progressCallback: progressCallback)
   770	
   771	    // Step 7: Decode
   772	    logger.info("Decoding latents via VAE...")
   773	    let decoded = decodeAdaptive(latents)
   774	    eval(decoded)
   775	
   776	    let rescaled = (decoded.asType(.float32) + 1.0) / 2.0
   777	    let clamped = MLX.clip(rescaled, min: 0, max: 1)
   778	    eval(clamped)
   779	
   780	    let elapsed = CFAbsoluteTimeGetCurrent() - startTime
   781	    logger.info("I2V generation complete in \(String(format: "%.1f", elapsed))s")
   782	
   783	    var output = LTX2PipelineOutput(
   784	      decoded: clamped,
   785	      numFrames: numFrames,
   786	      width: width,
   787	      height: height,
   788	      elapsedSeconds: elapsed
   789	    )
   790	    output.audioLatents = avState?.audioLatents
   791	    return output
   792	  }
   793	
   794	  // MARK: - Multi-Keyframe ("tween") Generation
   795	
   796	  /// One keyframe image anchoring the generated video at a specific point in
   797	  /// its timeline — the primitive behind multi-keyframe "tween" generation.
   798	  public struct Keyframe {
   799	    /// Normalized pixel image, same format `generateI2V`'s `image:` expects.
   800	    public let image: MLXArray
   801	    /// Target position in VIDEO frames (0 = first frame) — converted
   802	    /// internally to a latent-frame index via the VAE's temporal compression.
   803	    public let videoFrameIndex: Int
   804	    /// Denoising strength at this keyframe (1.0 = fully replace with the image).
   805	    public let strength: Float
   806	
   807	    public init(image: MLXArray, videoFrameIndex: Int, strength: Float = 1.0) {
   808	      self.image = image
   809	      self.videoFrameIndex = videoFrameIndex
   810	      self.strength = strength
  1070	
  1071	      let b = currentLatents.dim(0)
  1072	      let c = currentLatents.dim(1)
  1073	      let f = currentLatents.dim(2)
  1074	      let h = currentLatents.dim(3)
  1075	      let w = currentLatents.dim(4)
  1076	      let numTokens = f * h * w
  1077	
  1078	      // Flatten latents to token space: (B, C, F, H, W) -> (B, T, C)
  1079	      let latentsFlat = currentLatents
  1080	        .reshaped(b, c, -1)
  1081	        .transposed(0, 2, 1)
  1082	        .asType(dtype)
  1083	
  1084	      // Compute per-token timesteps
  1085	      let timesteps: MLXArray
  1086	      if let s = state {
  1087	        // I2V: per-token timesteps based on denoise mask
  1088	        let maskBroadcast = MLX.broadcast(
  1089	          s.denoiseMask.reshaped(b, 1, f, 1, 1),
  1090	          to: [b, 1, f, h, w]
  1091	        )
  1092	        let maskFlat = maskBroadcast.reshaped(b, numTokens)
  1093	        timesteps = MLXArray(sigma).asType(dtype) * maskFlat.asType(dtype)
  1094	      } else {
  1095	        timesteps = MLXArray(sigma).reshaped(1, 1).asType(dtype)
  1096	      }
  1097	
  1098	      let sigmaArray = MLXArray([sigma]).asType(dtype)
  1099	
  1100	      // Positive pass through transformer.
  1101	      // Pass PER-TOKEN timesteps so the model sees conditioned frames (mask=0)
  1102	      // at timestep 0 (clean) and generated frames at `sigma`. Without this the
  1103	      // transformer treats every token as equally noisy and cannot tell which
  1104	      // frame is the I2V conditioning frame — it discounts the image and
  1105	      // produces T2V-like output. Matches reference guided_denoise_loop which
  1106	      // passes `video_timesteps = denoise_mask * sigma` when the mask is
  1107	      // non-uniform. For T2V, `timesteps` is a scalar (1,1) → broadcast, so this
  1108	      // is a no-op there.
  1109	      // NAG rides ONLY the positive pass: it steers the conditional prediction
  1110	      // away from the negative concept inside cross-attention. Applying it to
  1111	      // the unconditional pass too would guide the baseline that CFG++ steps
  1112	      // along, double-counting the guidance.
  1113	      let velocityPos: MLXArray
  1114	      var avVelocityPos: MLXArray? = nil
  1115	      var avVelocityNeg: MLXArray? = nil
  1116	      if let av = avState {
  1117	        // Joint A/V pass (task #21): both velocities in one forward. NAG is
  1118	        // not available on this path yet (spec open item) — the joint model's
  1119	        // a2v cross-modal coupling replaces it as the video conditioning
  1120	        // difference. Audio updates HERE (positive pass only, plain Euler,
  1121	        // deterministic): CFG/STG passes below remain video-only and steer
  1122	        // only the video x0.
  1123	        let (vv, va) = transformer.callAV(
  1124	          latent: latentsFlat,
  1125	          audioLatents: av.audioLatents.asType(dtype),
  1126	          timestep: timesteps,
  1127	          videoSigmaMax: sigma,
  1128	          audioSigma: sigma,
  1129	          context: textEmbeddings.asType(dtype),
  1130	          audioContext: av.audioContext.asType(dtype),
  1131	          sigma: sigmaArray,
  1132	          pe: av.pe)
  1133	        velocityPos = vv
  1134	        avVelocityPos = va
  1135	      } else {
  1136	        velocityPos = transformer(
  1137	          latent: latentsFlat,
  1138	          timestep: timesteps,
  1139	          context: textEmbeddings.asType(dtype),
  1140	          positions: positions,
  1141	          sigma: sigmaArray,
  1142	          precomputedPE: precomputedPE,
  1143	          nagContext: nagEmbeddings?.asType(dtype),
  1144	          nag: nag
  1145	        )
  1146	      }
  1147	
  1148	      // Compute x0 (denoised) from velocity using per-token timesteps
  1149	      let latentsFlatF32 = currentLatents
  1150	        .reshaped(b, c, -1)
  1151	        .transposed(0, 2, 1)
  1152	      let timestepsF32 = timesteps.asType(.float32).expandedDimensions(axis: -1)
  1153	      var x0GuidedF32 = latentsFlatF32 - timestepsF32 * velocityPos.asType(.float32)
  1154	      let x0CondF32 = x0GuidedF32  // pure conditional x0, saved for the STG delta
  1155	
  1156	      // Negative pass: needed for classic CFG (scale>1) and for CFG++ (every
  1157	      // step, even at cfg=1 — the CFG++ update steps along the UNCONDITIONAL
  1158	      // noise direction; see ComfyUI sample_euler_ancestral_cfg_pp which sets
  1159	      // disable_cfg1_optimization=True for exactly this reason).
  1160	      var x0UncondF32: MLXArray? = nil
  1161	      if useCFG || useCfgPP, let negEmb = negativeEmbeddings {
  1162	        let velocityNeg: MLXArray
  1163	        if let av = avState, let negACtx = av.negativeAudioContext {
  1164	          // Audio joins the negative pass (task #26): the reference recipe's
  1165	          // negative ("...distorted sound, saturated sound, loud") flows
  1166	          // through BOTH streams; ours never steered audio until now. The
  1167	          // dual negative also makes the video-side negative prediction
  1168	          // reference-faithful (it carries a2v cross-modal contributions).
  1169	          let (vv, va) = transformer.callAV(
  1170	            latent: latentsFlat,
  1171	            audioLatents: av.audioLatents.asType(dtype),
  1172	            timestep: timesteps,
  1173	            videoSigmaMax: sigma,
  1174	            audioSigma: sigma,
  1175	            context: negEmb.asType(dtype),
  1176	            audioContext: negACtx.asType(dtype),
  1177	            sigma: sigmaArray,
  1178	            pe: av.pe)
  1179	          velocityNeg = vv
  1180	          avVelocityNeg = va
  1181	        } else {
  1182	          velocityNeg = transformer(
  1183	            latent: latentsFlat,
  1184	            timestep: timesteps,
  1185	            context: negEmb.asType(dtype),
  1186	            positions: positions,
  1187	            sigma: sigmaArray,
  1188	            precomputedPE: precomputedPE
  1189	          )
  1190	        }
  1191	        eval(velocityNeg)
  1192	
  1193	        let x0NegF32 = latentsFlatF32 - timestepsF32 * velocityNeg.asType(.float32)
  1194	        x0UncondF32 = x0NegF32
  1195	        if useCFG && cfgAt(i) > 1.0 {
  1196	          x0GuidedF32 = LTX2Guidance.applyCFG(
  1197	            conditioned: x0GuidedF32,
  1198	            unconditioned: x0NegF32,
  1199	            scale: cfgAt(i)
  1200	          )
  1201	          // Guidance rescale (Lin et al.; counters CFG over-saturation /
  1202	          // shadow-crush). High CFG inflates the prediction's std -> boosted
  1203	          // color/contrast (measured: cfg2 saturation +19% vs seed). Rescale
  1204	          // the guided x0 back toward the CONDITIONAL prediction's std, then
  1205	          // blend by phi — keeps the action-motion boost, restores seed color.
  1206	          // resolvedConfig.guidanceRescale (0 = off) — request/preset capable.
  1207	          let phi = resolvedConfig.guidanceRescale
  1208	          if phi > 0 {
  1209	            let stdCond = MLX.sqrt(((x0CondF32 - x0CondF32.mean()) * (x0CondF32 - x0CondF32.mean())).mean())
  1210	            let stdGuided = MLX.sqrt(((x0GuidedF32 - x0GuidedF32.mean()) * (x0GuidedF32 - x0GuidedF32.mean())).mean())
  1211	            let rescaled = x0GuidedF32 * (stdCond / (stdGuided + 1e-6))
  1212	            x0GuidedF32 = MLXArray(phi) * rescaled + MLXArray(1 - phi) * x0GuidedF32
  1213	          }
  1214	        }
  1215	      }
  1216	
  1217	      // STG: spatiotemporal guidance. A perturbed pass skips self-attention in a
  1218	      // block subset; steer away from it to restore high-frequency motion detail.
  1219	      // For distilled cfg=1 this is the primary guidance lever (anti-haze).
  1220	      let stgBase = resolvedConfig.stgScale
  1221	      if stgBase > 0 {
  1222	        let stgScale = LTX2PipelineConfig.stgScaleForStep(i, base: stgBase)
  1223	        let velocitySTG = transformer(
  1224	          latent: latentsFlat,
  1225	          timestep: timesteps,
  1226	          context: textEmbeddings.asType(dtype),
  1227	          positions: positions,
  1228	          sigma: sigmaArray,
  1229	          precomputedPE: precomputedPE,
  1230	          stgBlocks: resolvedConfig.stgBlockSet
  1231	        )
  1232	        eval(velocitySTG)
  1233	        let x0STGF32 = latentsFlatF32 - timestepsF32 * velocitySTG.asType(.float32)
  1234	        x0GuidedF32 = x0GuidedF32 + MLXArray(stgScale) * (x0CondF32 - x0STGF32)
  1235	      }
  1236	
  1237	      // Reshape x0 from token space to spatial
  1238	      var denoised = x0GuidedF32
  1239	        .transposed(0, 2, 1)
  1240	        .reshaped(b, c, f, h, w)
  1241	
  1242	      // Apply I2V denoise mask
  1243	      if let s = state {
  1244	        denoised = LTX2Conditioning.applyDenoiseMask(
  1245	          denoised: denoised,
  1246	          clean: s.cleanLatent.asType(.float32),
  1247	          denoiseMask: s.denoiseMask
  1248	        )
  1249	      }
  1250	      // Face-region identity hold: pull ONLY the masked face latents (all frames)
  1251	      // toward the source face, softly + every step. No full-frame splicing, so
  1252	      // nothing to collapse between anchors — holds a stationary partner's face.
  1253	      if let s = state, let fm = s.faceMask, let ref = s.faceRef, s.faceAnchorStrength > 0 {
  1254	        let m = fm.asType(.float32)
  1255	        let wm = m * s.faceAnchorStrength
  1256	        denoised = denoised * (MLXArray(Float(1)) - wm) + ref.asType(.float32) * wm
  1257	      }
  1258	
  1259	      // Euler step (ancestral/SDE when LTX2_SAMPLER=euler_ancestral) in float32
  1260	      if sigmaNext > 0 {
  1261	        if useCfgPP, let x0Neg = x0UncondF32 {
  1262	          // CFG++ step (ComfyUI sample_euler_ancestral_cfg_pp, flow/CONST model:
  1263	          // exp(lambda(σ)) = (1-σ)/σ so alpha_s = 1-σ, alpha_t = 1-σ_next).
  1264	          // Direction from the UNCONDITIONAL x0, target the conditional x0:
  1265	          //   d = (x - alpha_s·x0_uncond) / σ
  1266	          //   x = alpha_t·x0_cond + σ_down·d  (+ alpha_t·σ_up·noise, ancestral)
  1267	          let x0UncondSpatial = x0Neg
  1268	            .transposed(0, 2, 1)
  1269	            .reshaped(b, c, f, h, w)
  1270	          // Guard the σ=1.0 first step: alphaS→0 makes sf=σ/alphaS blow up to inf
  1271	          // and NaN the ancestral term. Clamp alphaS to a small floor.
  1272	          let alphaS = max(1.0 - sigma, 1e-4)
  1273	          let alphaT = 1.0 - sigmaNext
  1274	          let d = (currentLatents - MLXArray(alphaS) * x0UncondSpatial) / MLXArray(sigma)
  1275	          var sigmaDown = sigmaNext
  1276	          var sigmaUp: Float = 0
  1277	          if useSDE {
  1278	            // get_ancestral_step(σ/alpha_s, σ_next/alpha_t, eta=1), then
  1279	            // σ_down = alpha_t · σ_down'
  1280	            let sf = sigma / alphaS
  1281	            let st = sigmaNext / alphaT
  1282	            let inner = st * st * (sf * sf - st * st) / (sf * sf)
  1283	            let up = min(st, (inner > 0 ? inner : 0).squareRoot())
  1284	            sigmaDown = alphaT * (max(st * st - up * up, 0)).squareRoot()
  1285	            sigmaUp = up
  1286	          }
  1287	          currentLatents = MLXArray(alphaT) * denoised + MLXArray(sigmaDown) * d
  1288	          if useSDE && sigmaUp > 0 {
  1289	            // Plain Gaussian ancestral noise, matching ComfyUI's noise_sampler
  1290	            // (randn_like). getNewNoise's per-channel normalization flattens the
  1291	            // stochasticity that drives inter-frame motion — do NOT use it here.
  1292	            let noise = MLXRandom.normal(currentLatents.shape, dtype: .float32)
  1293	            currentLatents = currentLatents + MLXArray(alphaT) * noise * MLXArray(sigmaUp)
  1294	          }
  1295	        } else if useSDE {
  1296	          let sigmaF32 = MLXArray(sigma)
  1297	          let eps = (currentLatents - denoised) / sigmaF32
  1298	          let (alphaRatio, sigmaDown, sigmaUp) = getSdeCoeff(sigmaNext: sigmaNext)
  1299	          let noise = MLXRandom.normal(currentLatents.shape, dtype: .float32)
  1300	          currentLatents = MLXArray(alphaRatio) * (denoised + MLXArray(sigmaDown) * eps)
  1301	            + MLXArray(sigmaUp) * noise
  1302	        } else {
  1303	          let sigmaF32 = MLXArray(sigma)
  1304	          let sigmaNextF32 = MLXArray(sigmaNext)
  1305	          currentLatents = denoised + sigmaNextF32 * (currentLatents - denoised) / sigmaF32
  1306	        }
  1307	      } else {
  1308	        currentLatents = denoised
  1309	      }
  1310	      // Audio step (task #26): guided when the negative pass ran dual-stream,
  1311	      // plain Euler otherwise. Ancestral noise comes from the state's keyed
  1312	      // chain so the global RNG (video noise sequence) is untouched.
  1313	      if let av = avState, let velA = avVelocityPos {
  1314	        var aNoise: MLXArray? = nil
  1315	        if useSDE, avVelocityNeg != nil, let key = av.audioNoiseKey {
  1316	          let keys = MLXRandom.split(key: key)
  1317	          av.audioNoiseKey = keys.0
  1318	          aNoise = MLXRandom.normal(av.audioLatents.shape, key: keys.1).asType(.float32)
  1319	        }
  1320	        // Audio guidance is DECOUPLED from video cfg (2026-08-05): Kira's
  1321	        // video cfg 3.5 over-guided the audio stream — dragged, slow-motion
  1322	        // sound on otherwise-correct 5s clips; the author's recipes never
  1323	        // run audio above cfg 1. Negatives still steer via CFG++ stepping.
  1324	        // LTX2_AUDIO_CFG overrides (default 1.0).
  1325	        let audioCfg = Float(ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_AUDIO_CFG"] ?? "") ?? 1.0
  1326	        av.audioLatents = ltx2AudioStep(
  1327	          audio: av.audioLatents, velocityPos: velA, velocityNeg: avVelocityNeg,
  1328	          sigma: sigma, sigmaNext: sigmaNext, cfgScale: min(cfgAt(i), audioCfg),
  1329	          useCfgPP: useCfgPP, useSDE: useSDE && !forceDeterministic,
  1330	          ancestralNoise: aNoise)
  1331	        eval(av.audioLatents)
  1332	      }
  1333	      // CFG++ steps along the uncond direction, which drifts frames the denoise
  1334	      // mask pins at timestep 0 (I2V conditioning / refine re-inject); re-snap
  1335	      // them to the clean latent after every step (ComfyUI does the equivalent
  1336	      // in its outer inpaint wrapper).
  1337	      if useCfgPP, let s = state {
  1338	        currentLatents = LTX2Conditioning.applyDenoiseMask(
  1339	          denoised: currentLatents,
  1340	          clean: s.cleanLatent.asType(.float32),
  1341	          denoiseMask: s.denoiseMask
  1342	        )
  1343	      }
  1344	
  1345	      eval(currentLatents)
  1346	
  1347	      // --- Trajectory dump (after step update) ---
  1348	      if let prefix = trajPrefix, let before = trajLatentsBefore {
  1349	        // Scalar stats via small MLX reductions; only scalars are evaluated.
  1350	        let velocityF32 = velocityPos.asType(.float32)

   638	    if imageInput.ndim == 4 {
   639	      imageInput = imageInput.expandedDimensions(axis: 2)
   640	    }
   641	    let imageLatent = vae.encode(imageInput)
   642	    eval(imageLatent)
   643	
   644	    // Step 3: Compute latent dimensions
   645	    let latH = height / spatialCompression
   646	    let latW = width / spatialCompression
   647	    let latF = (numFrames - 1) / temporalCompression + 1
   648	
   649	    // Step 4: Create initial noisy state with I2V conditioning
   650	    if let seed = seed {
   651	      MLXRandom.seed(seed)
   652	    }
   653	
   654	    let sigmas = getSigmaSchedule(steps: steps, latF: latF, latH: latH, latW: latW)
   655	
   656	    // Create initial state
   657	    var state = LTX2Conditioning.createInitialState(
   658	      shape: [1, 128, latF, latH, latW],
   659	      noiseScale: sigmas[0]
   660	    )
   661	
   662	    // Apply image conditioning at frame 0
   663	    let condition = LTX2VideoCondition(
   664	      latent: imageLatent,
   665	      frameIndex: 0,
   666	      strength: strength
   667	    )
   668	    state = LTX2Conditioning.applyConditioning(state: state, conditions: [condition])
   669	    if let fm = faceAnchorMask, faceAnchorStrength > 0 {
   670	      state.faceMask = fm
   671	      state.faceRef = imageLatent  // source-encoded latent = the face identity reference
   672	      state.faceAnchorStrength = faceAnchorStrength
   673	    }
   674	
   675	    // IC-control (union-control IC-LoRA): append the source-encoded latent as
   676	    // extra reference frames at temporal frame_idx 0, FROZEN (mask=1-strength).
   677	    // The merged union-control IC-LoRA makes self-attention lock structure to
   678	    // these reference frames -> coherent motion instead of morph. Env-gated
   679	    // LTX2_IC_CONTROL; MVP uses reference_downscale_factor=1 (no dilation).
   680	    // Mirrors ComfyUI LTXVAddGuide.append_keyframe (frame axis cat) +
   681	    // add_keyframe_index (temporal position -> frame_idx 0, handled in
   682	    // createPositionGrid via refFrames). Slice back to latF before decode.
   683	    var icRefFrames = 0
   684	    // IC-control (identity anchor) defaults ON for i2v: without it the subject
   685	    // morphs into a different person over the render. LTX2_IC_CONTROL=0 disables.
   686	    if resolvedConfig.icControl {
   687	      let refStrength = resolvedConfig.icRefStrength
   688	      icRefFrames = imageLatent.dim(2)
   689	      state.latent = MLX.concatenated([state.latent, imageLatent.asType(state.latent.dtype)], axis: 2)
   690	      state.cleanLatent = MLX.concatenated([state.cleanLatent, imageLatent.asType(state.cleanLatent.dtype)], axis: 2)
   691	      let refMask = MLX.broadcast(
   692	        MLXArray(Float(1.0 - refStrength)).reshaped(1, 1, 1, 1, 1),
   693	        to: [1, 1, icRefFrames, 1, 1])
   694	      state.denoiseMask = MLX.concatenated([state.denoiseMask, refMask], axis: 2)
   695	      logger.info("IC-control: appended \(icRefFrames) reference frame(s) @ strength \(refStrength)")
   696	    }
   697	    eval(state.latent, state.cleanLatent, state.denoiseMask)
   698	
   699	    // Step 5: Build position grid (extended by icRefFrames; ref frames get
   700	    // temporal frame_idx 0 inside createPositionGrid).
   701	    let positions = createPositionGrid(
   702	      batchSize: 1, latF: latF, latH: latH, latW: latW, refFrames: icRefFrames
   703	    )
   704	
   705	    // Precompute RoPE
   706	    let precomputedPE = ltx2PrecomputeFreqsCIS(
   707	      indicesGrid: positions,
   708	      dim: innerDim,
   709	      theta: transformer.positionalEmbeddingTheta,
   710	      maxPos: transformer.positionalEmbeddingMaxPos,
   711	      useMiddleIndicesGrid: transformer.useMiddleIndicesGrid,
   712	      numAttentionHeads: transformer.numHeads,
   713	      ropeMode: transformer.ropeMode,
   714	      doublePrecision: transformer.doublePrecisionRoPE
   715	    )
   716	    eval(precomputedPE.cos, precomputedPE.sin)
   717	
   718	    // Step 5b: Audio stream (task #21 i2v). Same setup as t2v; the per-token
   719	    // av_ca video conditioning inside callAV handles the i2v denoise mask
   720	    // (conditioned tokens at ~0). Cross PEs use the FULL positions grid incl.
   721	    // IC-control ref frames (they sit at t=0s, matching their video PE).
   722	    var avState: LTX2AVDenoiseState? = nil
   723	    if let seconds = audioSeconds, wantAudio {
   724	      let ta = max(1, Int((seconds * 25).rounded(.up)))
   725	      let audioKey = seed.map { MLXRandom.key($0 &+ 0xA0D10) }
   726	      let audioNoise = MLXRandom.normal([1, 8, ta, 16], key: audioKey).asType(.float32)
   727	      let audioInit = audioNoise * MLXArray(sigmas[0])
   728	      let (_, audioCoords) = LTX2AudioPatchifier.patchify(audioInit)
   729	      let avPE = transformer.precomputeAVPositionalEmbeddings(
   730	        positions: positions, audioCoords: audioCoords)
   731	      avState = LTX2AVDenoiseState(
   732	        audioLatents: audioInit,
   733	        audioContext: textOutput.audioEmbeddings,
   734	        pe: avPE,
   735	        negativeAudioContext: negativeAudioEmbeddings,
   736	        audioNoiseKey: seed.map { MLXRandom.key($0 &+ 0xA0D12) })
   737	      logger.info("Audio stream enabled (i2v): \(ta) latent frames (\(seconds)s, negatives \(negativeAudioEmbeddings != nil ? "on" : "off")).")
   738	    }
   739	
   740	    // Step 6: Denoising loop with I2V state
