     1	// LTX2TransformerAV.swift — top-level audio conditioning + I/O for the joint
     2	// A/V forward (task #21 wire 1).
     3	//
     4	// Mirrors LTXAVModel's audio-side plumbing (av_model.py) using the hasAudio
     5	// members LTX2Transformer already binds: the audio adaln singles, the four
     6	// av_ca adaln singles (with CROSSED gate inputs and the
     7	// av_ca_timestep_scale_multiplier / timestep_scale_multiplier factor), the
     8	// audio patchify projection, and the audio output processing
     9	// (LayerNorm -> table+embedded modulation -> proj_out -> unpatchify).
    10	
    11	import Foundation
    12	import MLX
    13	import MLXFast
    14	import MLXNN
    15	
    16	/// Per-step audio-side conditioning bundle consumed by `callDualStream`
    17	/// across all blocks. Field names match the block's parameter labels.
    18	public struct LTX2AVConditioning {
    19	  /// Audio 9-coeff AdaLN timestep `(B, 1, 9*audioDim)`.
    20	  public let audioTimestep: MLXArray
    21	  /// Audio embedded timestep for output modulation `(B, 1, audioDim)`.
    22	  public let audioEmbeddedTimestep: MLXArray
    23	  /// Audio prompt AdaLN timestep `(B, 1, 2*audioDim)`.
    24	  public let audioPromptTimestep: MLXArray
    25	  /// av_ca video scale-shift `(B, 1, 4*dim)`.
    26	  public let crossScaleShiftTimestep: MLXArray
    27	  /// av_ca audio scale-shift `(B, 1, 4*audioDim)`.
    28	  public let audioCrossScaleShiftTimestep: MLXArray
    29	  /// av_ca a2v gate `(B, 1, dim)` — driven by the AUDIO sigma (crossed).
    30	  public let crossGateTimestep: MLXArray
    31	  /// av_ca v2a gate `(B, 1, audioDim)` — driven by the VIDEO sigma (crossed).
    32	  public let audioCrossGateTimestep: MLXArray
    33	}
    34	
    35	extension LTX2Transformer {
    36	  /// LTX-2.3 audio latent geometry (fixed by the audio VAE).
    37	  public static let audioLatentChannels = 8
    38	  public static let audioLatentFrequencyBins = 16
    39	  /// Reference av_ca_timestep_scale_multiplier (v16b config default).
    40	  public static let avCaTimestepScaleMultiplier: Float = 1.0
    41	
    42	  /// All audio-side timestep conditioning for one denoise step.
    43	  /// Requires `hasAudio`.
    44	  ///
    45	  /// `videoTimesteps` carries the RAW per-token video sigmas — `(B, Tv)` for
    46	  /// i2v (conditioned tokens ~0, generated tokens at sigma; the reference
    47	  /// feeds per-token timestep_flat into av_ca_video_scale_shift) or `(B, 1)`
    48	  /// / `(1, 1)` for uniform t2v, where the result broadcasts.
    49	  ///
    50	  /// Gate inputs are CROSSED per the reference: the a2v gate (applied on the
    51	  /// video stream) is driven by the max AUDIO sigma, the v2a gate by the max
    52	  /// VIDEO sigma (`videoSigmaMax`) — both times avCaTimestepScaleMultiplier,
    53	  /// NOT the 1000x multiplier (av_ca_factor = avCaMult / tsMult cancels it).
    54	  public func prepareAVConditioning(
    55	    videoTimesteps: MLXArray, videoSigmaMax: Float,
    56	    audioSigma: Float, batchSize: Int,
    57	    hiddenDtype: DType = .float32
    58	  ) -> LTX2AVConditioning {
    59	    precondition(hasAudio, "prepareAVConditioning requires hasAudio")
    60	    func rep(_ value: Float) -> MLXArray {
    61	      MLXArray(Array(repeating: value, count: batchSize))
    62	    }
    63	    let avCaMult = Self.avCaTimestepScaleMultiplier
    64	    let aScaled = rep(audioSigma * timestepScaleMultiplier)
    65	    let vScaledFlat = (videoTimesteps.asType(.float32) * timestepScaleMultiplier).reshaped([-1])
    66	
    67	    // hiddenDtype follows the joint stream (bf16 in production) — hard-coding
    68	    // float32 here promoted BOTH streams via MLX type promotion (Codex #3).
    69	    let (aTs, aEmbedded) = audioAdaLNSingle!(aScaled, hiddenDtype: hiddenDtype)
    70	    let (aPrompt, _) = audioPromptAdaLNSingle!(aScaled, hiddenDtype: hiddenDtype)
    71	    let (vSS, _) = avCaVideoScaleShiftAdaLN!(vScaledFlat, hiddenDtype: hiddenDtype)
    72	    let (aSS, _) = avCaAudioScaleShiftAdaLN!(aScaled, hiddenDtype: hiddenDtype)
    73	    let (a2vGate, _) = avCaA2vGateAdaLN!(rep(audioSigma * avCaMult), hiddenDtype: hiddenDtype)
    74	    let (v2aGate, _) = avCaV2aGateAdaLN!(rep(videoSigmaMax * avCaMult), hiddenDtype: hiddenDtype)
    75	
    76	    func shape3(_ x: MLXArray) -> MLXArray { x.reshaped([batchSize, 1, -1]) }
    77	    return LTX2AVConditioning(
    78	      audioTimestep: shape3(aTs),
    79	      audioEmbeddedTimestep: shape3(aEmbedded),
    80	      audioPromptTimestep: shape3(aPrompt),
    81	      // Per-token rows survive: (B, Tv, 4*dim); uniform inputs give (B, 1, ...).
    82	      crossScaleShiftTimestep: vSS.reshaped([batchSize, -1, vSS.dim(-1)]),
    83	      audioCrossScaleShiftTimestep: shape3(aSS),
    84	      crossGateTimestep: shape3(a2vGate),
    85	      audioCrossGateTimestep: shape3(v2aGate))
    86	  }
    87	
    88	  /// `(B, C, T, F)` audio latents -> projected tokens `(B, T, audioDim)` +
    89	  /// real-time start/end coords `(B, 1, T, 2)`. Requires `hasAudio`.
    90	  public func projectAudioTokens(_ audioLatents: MLXArray) -> (tokens: MLXArray, coords: MLXArray) {
    91	    precondition(hasAudio, "projectAudioTokens requires hasAudio")
    92	    let (tokens, coords) = LTX2AudioPatchifier.patchify(audioLatents)
    93	    return (audioPatchifyProj!(tokens), coords)
    94	  }
    95	
    96	  /// All four positional-embedding sets for the joint forward, computed once
    97	  /// per render.
    98	  ///
    99	  /// CONTRACT: `positions` is the pipeline's grid with the time row ALREADY
   100	  /// in seconds (createPositionGrid divides by condFps — same convention the
   101	  /// video self PE consumes; Codex 2026-08-04 finding #1: dividing again here
   102	  /// collapsed 4s of video into ~0.16s for cross-attention). `audioCoords`
   103	  /// are the seconds-valued start/end coords from `projectAudioTokens`, so
   104	  /// both cross PEs share one real-time axis. When LTX2_COND_FPS diverges
   105	  /// from playback fps, video "seconds" follow condFps — the established
   106	  /// motion-dial tradeoff, now shared by the audio sync axis.
   107	  public func precomputeAVPositionalEmbeddings(
   108	    positions: MLXArray,
   109	    audioCoords: MLXArray
   110	  ) -> (videoPE: (cos: MLXArray, sin: MLXArray),
   111	        audioPE: (cos: MLXArray, sin: MLXArray),
   112	        crossVideoPE: (cos: MLXArray, sin: MLXArray),
   113	        crossAudioPE: (cos: MLXArray, sin: MLXArray)) {
   114	    precondition(hasAudio, "precomputeAVPositionalEmbeddings requires hasAudio")
   115	    let videoPE = ltx2PrecomputeFreqsCIS(
   116	      indicesGrid: positions, dim: innerDim, theta: positionalEmbeddingTheta,
   117	      maxPos: positionalEmbeddingMaxPos, useMiddleIndicesGrid: useMiddleIndicesGrid,
   118	      numAttentionHeads: numHeads, ropeMode: ropeMode, doublePrecision: doublePrecisionRoPE)
   119	    // Audio self PE: 1-D temporal, audio max_pos [20].
   120	    let audioPE = ltx2PrecomputeFreqsCIS(
   121	      indicesGrid: audioCoords, dim: audioInnerDim, theta: positionalEmbeddingTheta,
   122	      maxPos: [20], useMiddleIndicesGrid: useMiddleIndicesGrid,
   123	      numAttentionHeads: audioHeads, ropeMode: ropeMode, doublePrecision: doublePrecisionRoPE)
   124	    // Cross PEs: video time row (already seconds); middle-grid ALWAYS on
   125	    // (reference passes use_middle_indices_grid=True for av cross attention).
   126	    let timeSecs = positions.asType(.float32)[0..., 0..<1]
   127	    let maxPos = max(positionalEmbeddingMaxPos[0], 20)
   128	    let crossVideoPE = ltx2PrecomputeFreqsCIS(
   129	      indicesGrid: timeSecs, dim: audioInnerDim, theta: positionalEmbeddingTheta,
   130	      maxPos: [maxPos], useMiddleIndicesGrid: true,
   131	      numAttentionHeads: audioHeads, ropeMode: ropeMode, doublePrecision: doublePrecisionRoPE)
   132	    let crossAudioPE = ltx2PrecomputeFreqsCIS(
   133	      indicesGrid: audioCoords[0..., 0..<1], dim: audioInnerDim, theta: positionalEmbeddingTheta,
   134	      maxPos: [maxPos], useMiddleIndicesGrid: true,
   135	      numAttentionHeads: audioHeads, ropeMode: ropeMode, doublePrecision: doublePrecisionRoPE)
   136	    return (videoPE, audioPE, crossVideoPE, crossAudioPE)
   137	  }
   138	
   139	  /// Joint dual-stream forward: video + audio velocities in one pass.
   140	  ///
   141	  /// Mirrors LTXAVModel.forward: per-modality timesteps, the av_ca cross
   142	  /// conditioning from `prepareAVConditioning`, all 48 blocks via
   143	  /// `callDualStream`, then both output projections.
   144	  ///
   145	  /// v1 scope: audio timestep is a SCALAR sigma (t2v audio; per-token audio
   146	  /// timesteps arrive with i2v mask integration); no NAG/STG on this path
   147	  /// yet. `videoSigmaMax` drives the av_ca video scale-shift and the v2a
   148	  /// gate — pass the max video sigma (== sigma for uniform timesteps).
   149	  public func callAV(
   150	    latent: MLXArray,
   151	    audioLatents: MLXArray,
   152	    timestep: MLXArray,
   153	    videoSigmaMax: Float,
   154	    audioSigma: Float,
   155	    context: MLXArray,
   156	    audioContext: MLXArray,
   157	    contextMask: MLXArray? = nil,
   158	    sigma: MLXArray? = nil,
   159	    pe: (videoPE: (cos: MLXArray, sin: MLXArray),
   160	         audioPE: (cos: MLXArray, sin: MLXArray),
   161	         crossVideoPE: (cos: MLXArray, sin: MLXArray),
   162	         crossAudioPE: (cos: MLXArray, sin: MLXArray))
   163	  ) -> (video: MLXArray, audio: MLXArray) {
   164	    precondition(hasAudio, "callAV requires hasAudio")
   165	    let batchSize = latent.dim(0)
   166	
   167	    // ---- Video side: identical prep to the video-only forward ----
   168	    var vx = patchifyProj(latent)
   169	    let scaledTimestep = timestep * timestepScaleMultiplier
   170	    let (timestepEmb, embeddedTimestep) = adaLNSingle(
   171	      scaledTimestep.reshaped(-1), hiddenDtype: vx.dtype)
   172	    let vTsEmb = timestepEmb.reshaped(batchSize, -1, timestepEmb.dim(-1))
   173	    let vEmbTS = embeddedTimestep.reshaped(batchSize, -1, embeddedTimestep.dim(-1))
   174	
   175	    var ctx = context
   176	    if let captionProj = captionProjection { ctx = captionProj(ctx) }
   177	    ctx = ctx.reshaped(batchSize, -1, vx.dim(-1))
   178	
   179	    var attnMask = contextMask
   180	    if let mask = attnMask {
   181	      if mask.dtype != .float32 && mask.dtype != .float16 && mask.dtype != .bfloat16 {
   182	        let floatMask = (mask.asType(latent.dtype) - 1) * 1e9
   183	        attnMask = floatMask.reshaped(mask.dim(0), 1, -1, mask.dim(-1))
   184	      }
   185	    }
   186	
   187	    var promptTS: MLXArray? = nil
   188	    if let promptAda = promptAdaLNSingle, let s = sigma {
   189	      let scaledSigma = s * timestepScaleMultiplier
   190	      let (pTS, _) = promptAda(scaledSigma.reshaped(-1), hiddenDtype: vx.dtype)
   191	      promptTS = pTS.reshaped(batchSize, -1, pTS.dim(-1))
   192	    }
   193	
   194	    // ---- Audio side ----
   195	    var (ax, _) = projectAudioTokens(audioLatents)
   196	    ax = ax.asType(vx.dtype)
   197	    let av = prepareAVConditioning(
   198	      videoTimesteps: timestep, videoSigmaMax: videoSigmaMax,
   199	      audioSigma: audioSigma, batchSize: batchSize,
   200	      hiddenDtype: vx.dtype)
   201	    let aCtx = audioContext.reshaped(batchSize, -1, audioInnerDim).asType(vx.dtype)
   202	
   203	    // ---- Joint block loop ----
   204	    for block in transformerBlocks {
   205	      (vx, ax) = block.callDualStream(
   206	        video: vx, audio: ax,
   207	        context: ctx, audioContext: aCtx,
   208	        contextMask: attnMask, audioContextMask: attnMask,
   209	        timestep: vTsEmb, audioTimestep: av.audioTimestep,
   210	        pe: pe.videoPE, audioPE: pe.audioPE,
   211	        crossPE: pe.crossVideoPE, audioCrossPE: pe.crossAudioPE,
   212	        crossScaleShiftTimestep: av.crossScaleShiftTimestep,
   213	        audioCrossScaleShiftTimestep: av.audioCrossScaleShiftTimestep,
   214	        crossGateTimestep: av.crossGateTimestep,
   215	        audioCrossGateTimestep: av.audioCrossGateTimestep,
   216	        promptTimestep: promptTS,
   217	        audioPromptTimestep: av.audioPromptTimestep)
   218	    }
   219	
   220	    // ---- Outputs ----
   221	    let videoOut = processOutput(x: vx, embeddedTimestep: vEmbTS)
   222	    let audioOut = processAudioOutput(ax, embeddedTimestep: av.audioEmbeddedTimestep)
   223	    return (videoOut, audioOut)
   224	  }
   225	
   226	  /// Audio output processing: LayerNorm (no affine, eps 1e-6) ->
   227	  /// table+embedded modulation (row 0 shift, row 1 scale) -> proj_out ->
   228	  /// unpatchify to `(B, 8, T, 16)`. Requires `hasAudio`.
   229	  public func processAudioOutput(_ ax: MLXArray, embeddedTimestep: MLXArray) -> MLXArray {
   230	    precondition(hasAudio, "processAudioOutput requires hasAudio")
   231	    let ssv = audioScaleShiftTable!.expandedDimensions(axes: [0, 1])
   232	      + embeddedTimestep.expandedDimensions(axis: 2)  // (B, 1, 2, audioDim)
   233	    let shift = ssv[0..., 0..., 0]
   234	    let scale = ssv[0..., 0..., 1]
   235	    var x = MLXFast.layerNorm(ax, weight: nil, bias: nil, eps: 1e-6)
   236	    x = x * (1 + scale) + shift
   237	    x = audioProjOut!(x)
   238	    return LTX2AudioPatchifier.unpatchify(
   239	      x, channels: Self.audioLatentChannels, freq: Self.audioLatentFrequencyBins)
   240	  }
   241	}
   165	
   166	  /// Weight-free RMSNorm over the audio hidden width.
   167	  private func audioRMSNorm(_ x: MLXArray) -> MLXArray {
   168	    MLXFast.rmsNorm(x, weight: audioRmsNormOnes, eps: normEps)
   169	  }
   170	
   171	  /// Dual-stream forward: run the video sub-steps (self-attn, text cross-attn,
   172	  /// FF) alongside the audio sub-steps and the bidirectional a2v/v2a cross-modal
   173	  /// attention, returning updated `(video, audio)`.
   174	  ///
   175	  /// Mirrors `BasicAVTransformerBlock.forward` (av_model.py):
   176	  /// - Cross-modal AdaLN uses the [5, dim] `scale_shift_table_a2v_ca_*` tables
   177	  ///   with SCALE-FIRST row order: rows 0/1 = a2v scale/shift, rows 2/3 = v2a
   178	  ///   scale/shift, row 4 = gate. (The main 9-row table is shift-first.)
   179	  /// - Both cross-modal attention inputs are norm+modulated; the norms are
   180	  ///   taken ONCE before a2v and reused for v2a (the reference computes
   181	  ///   vx_norm3/ax_norm3 before either update).
   182	  /// - a2v runs with `pe: crossPE, kPE: audioCrossPE`; v2a with the mirror.
   183	  ///
   184	  /// - Parameters:
   185	  ///   - video: Video hidden states `(B, T_v, dim)`.
   186	  ///   - audio: Audio hidden states `(B, T_a, audioDim)`.
   187	  ///   - context: Video text embeddings.
   188	  ///   - audioContext: Audio text embeddings `(B, S_a, audioDim)`.
   189	  ///   - timestep: Video timestep embedding `(B, 1, 9*dim)`.
   190	  ///   - audioTimestep: Audio timestep embedding `(B, 1, 9*audioDim)`.
   191	  ///   - pe: Video RoPE.
   192	  ///   - audioPE: Audio RoPE.
   193	  ///   - crossPE: Video-side RoPE for the cross-modal attentions.
   194	  ///   - audioCrossPE: Audio-side RoPE for the cross-modal attentions.
   195	  ///   - crossScaleShiftTimestep: Video av_ca scale-shift timestep `(B, 1, 4*dim)`.
   196	  ///   - audioCrossScaleShiftTimestep: Audio av_ca scale-shift timestep `(B, 1, 4*audioDim)`.
   197	  ///   - crossGateTimestep: Video av_ca gate timestep `(B, 1, dim)`.
   198	  ///   - audioCrossGateTimestep: Audio av_ca gate timestep `(B, 1, audioDim)`.
   199	  ///     Nil cross timesteps degrade to table-rows-only modulation.
   200	  public func callDualStream(
   201	    video: MLXArray,
   202	    audio: MLXArray,
   203	    context: MLXArray,
   204	    audioContext: MLXArray,
   205	    contextMask: MLXArray? = nil,
   206	    audioContextMask: MLXArray? = nil,
   207	    timestep: MLXArray,
   208	    audioTimestep: MLXArray,
   209	    pe: (cos: MLXArray, sin: MLXArray)? = nil,
   210	    audioPE: (cos: MLXArray, sin: MLXArray)? = nil,
   211	    crossPE: (cos: MLXArray, sin: MLXArray)? = nil,
   212	    audioCrossPE: (cos: MLXArray, sin: MLXArray)? = nil,
   213	    crossScaleShiftTimestep: MLXArray? = nil,
   214	    audioCrossScaleShiftTimestep: MLXArray? = nil,
   215	    crossGateTimestep: MLXArray? = nil,
   216	    audioCrossGateTimestep: MLXArray? = nil,
   217	    promptTimestep: MLXArray? = nil,
   218	    audioPromptTimestep: MLXArray? = nil
   219	  ) -> (video: MLXArray, audio: MLXArray) {
   220	    precondition(hasAudio, "callDualStream requires hasAudio")
   221	    let b = video.dim(0)
   222	
   223	    // ---- Video stream: self-attn, text cross-attn (reuse video sub-logic) ----
   224	    var v = video
   225	    let vMSA = getAdaValues(table: scaleShiftTable, batchSize: b, timestep: timestep, range: 0..<3)
   226	    let vNorm = weightFreeRMSNorm(v) * (1 + vMSA[1]) + vMSA[0]
   227	    v = v + attn1(vNorm, pe: pe) * vMSA[2]
   228	
   229	    let vCross = getAdaValues(table: scaleShiftTable, batchSize: b, timestep: timestep, range: 6..<9)
   230	    var vCtx = context
   231	    if let pTS = promptTimestep, let pTable = promptScaleShiftTable {
   232	      let pp = getAdaValues(table: pTable, batchSize: b, timestep: pTS, range: 0..<2)
   233	      vCtx = context * (1 + pp[1]) + pp[0]
   234	    }
   235	    v = v + attn2(weightFreeRMSNorm(v) * (1 + vCross[1]) + vCross[0],
   236	                  context: vCtx, mask: contextMask) * vCross[2]
   237	
   238	    // ---- Audio stream: self-attn, text cross-attn ----
   239	    var a = audio
   240	    let aMSA = getAdaValues(table: audioScaleShiftTable!, batchSize: b, timestep: audioTimestep, range: 0..<3)
   241	    a = a + audioAttn1!(audioRMSNorm(a) * (1 + aMSA[1]) + aMSA[0], pe: audioPE) * aMSA[2]
   242	
   243	    let aCross = getAdaValues(table: audioScaleShiftTable!, batchSize: b, timestep: audioTimestep, range: 6..<9)
   244	    var aCtx = audioContext
   245	    if let pTS = audioPromptTimestep, let pTable = audioPromptScaleShiftTable {
   246	      let pp = getAdaValues(table: pTable, batchSize: b, timestep: pTS, range: 0..<2)
   247	      aCtx = audioContext * (1 + pp[1]) + pp[0]
   248	    }
   249	    a = a + audioAttn2!(audioRMSNorm(a) * (1 + aCross[1]) + aCross[0],
   250	                        context: aCtx, mask: audioContextMask) * aCross[2]
   251	
   252	    // ---- Cross-modal: A2V (update video from audio) + V2A (update audio) ----
   253	    // Norms taken once, BEFORE either update (reference vx_norm3/ax_norm3).
   254	    let vxNorm3 = weightFreeRMSNorm(v)
   255	    let axNorm3 = audioRMSNorm(a)
   256	    let (vCA, vGateCA) = avCrossAdaValues(
   257	      table: scaleShiftTableA2VCaVideo!, batchSize: b,
   258	      scaleShiftTimestep: crossScaleShiftTimestep, gateTimestep: crossGateTimestep)
   259	    let (aCA, aGateCA) = avCrossAdaValues(
   260	      table: scaleShiftTableA2VCaAudio!, batchSize: b,
   261	      scaleShiftTimestep: audioCrossScaleShiftTimestep, gateTimestep: audioCrossGateTimestep)
   262	
   263	    // a2v: Q = modulated video, KV = modulated audio (scale row 0, shift row 1).
   264	    let vxScaledA2V = vxNorm3 * (1 + vCA[0]) + vCA[1]
   265	    let axScaledA2V = axNorm3 * (1 + aCA[0]) + aCA[1]
   266	    v = v + audioToVideoAttn!(vxScaledA2V, context: axScaledA2V,
   267	                              pe: crossPE, kPE: audioCrossPE) * vGateCA
   268	
   269	    // v2a: Q = modulated audio, KV = modulated video (scale row 2, shift row 3).
   270	    let axScaledV2A = axNorm3 * (1 + aCA[2]) + aCA[3]
   271	    let vxScaledV2A = vxNorm3 * (1 + vCA[2]) + vCA[3]
   272	    a = a + videoToAudioAttn!(axScaledV2A, context: vxScaledV2A,
   273	                              pe: audioCrossPE, kPE: crossPE) * aGateCA
   274	
   275	    // ---- Feed-forward on both streams ----
   276	    let vMLP = getAdaValues(table: scaleShiftTable, batchSize: b, timestep: timestep, range: 3..<6)
   277	    v = v + ff(weightFreeRMSNorm(v) * (1 + vMLP[1]) + vMLP[0]) * vMLP[2]
   278	    let aMLP = getAdaValues(table: audioScaleShiftTable!, batchSize: b, timestep: audioTimestep, range: 3..<6)
   279	    a = a + audioFF!(audioRMSNorm(a) * (1 + aMLP[1]) + aMLP[0]) * aMLP[2]
   280	
   281	    return (v, a)
   282	  }
   283	
   284	  /// AdaLN values for a cross-modal [5, dim] table: rows 0..3 combined with
   285	  /// the 4-row scale-shift timestep, row 4 with the 1-row gate timestep
   286	  /// (reference `get_av_ca_ada_values`). Nil timesteps contribute zero.
   287	  private func avCrossAdaValues(
   288	    table: MLXArray,
   289	    batchSize: Int,
   290	    scaleShiftTimestep: MLXArray?,
   291	    gateTimestep: MLXArray?
   292	  ) -> (values: [MLXArray], gate: MLXArray) {
   293	    let dim = table.dim(1)
   294	    var ss = table[0..<4].expandedDimensions(axes: [0, 1])  // (1, 1, 4, dim)
   295	    if let ts = scaleShiftTimestep {
   296	      ss = ss + ts.reshaped(batchSize, ts.dim(1), 4, dim)
   297	    }
   298	    var values: [MLXArray] = []
   299	    for i in 0..<4 { values.append(ss[0..., 0..., i]) }
   300	    var g = table[4..<5].expandedDimensions(axes: [0, 1])  // (1, 1, 1, dim)
     1	// LTX2CrossModalAttention.swift — Bidirectional cross-modal attention (A2V/V2A) stub
     2	// Phase 3 of the LTX-2 Swift/MLX port
     3	//
     4	// Placeholder for audio-video cross-modal attention. The structural scaffolding
     5	// is here so transformer blocks can reference it, but the actual cross-modal
     6	// computation is deferred to Phase 5 (audio support).
     7	//
     8	// When audio is disabled (nil), this module is a pure pass-through.
     9	//
    10	// Reference: transformer.py — audio_to_video_attn, video_to_audio_attn sections
    11	
    12	import MLX
    13	import MLXNN
    14	
    15	/// Bidirectional cross-modal attention for audio-video interaction.
    16	///
    17	/// Phase 3 stub: returns inputs unchanged when audio is nil.
    18	/// Full implementation deferred to Phase 5.
    19	///
    20	/// Architecture (when enabled):
    21	///   A2V: Q from video, K/V from audio -> updates video
    22	///   V2A: Q from audio, K/V from video -> updates audio
    23	///   Each direction has its own AdaLN gating (5 params)
    24	public final class LTX2CrossModalAttention: Module {
    25	
    26	  /// Whether cross-modal attention is structurally enabled.
    27	  /// Even when true, actual computation only happens if audio is non-nil.
    28	  let enabled: Bool
    29	
    30	  public init(enabled: Bool = false) {
    31	    self.enabled = enabled
    32	  }
    33	
    34	  /// Forward pass for cross-modal attention.
    35	  ///
    36	  /// - Parameters:
    37	  ///   - videoHidden: Video hidden states `(B, T_v, dim)`.
    38	  ///   - audioHidden: Audio hidden states `(B, T_a, dim)`, or nil if audio disabled.
    39	  /// - Returns: Updated `(videoHidden, audioHidden)` tuple.
    40	  public func callAsFunction(
    41	    videoHidden: MLXArray,
    42	    audioHidden: MLXArray?
    43	  ) -> (MLXArray, MLXArray?) {
    44	    guard let audio = audioHidden, enabled else {
    45	      return (videoHidden, nil)
    46	    }
    47	    // Full implementation deferred to Phase 5
    48	    return (videoHidden, audio)
    49	  }
    50	}

    95	/**
    96	 * The stored default fruit mode — the same store the Kira-tab picker and
    97	 * /apple //banana //avocado write. Falls back to 'avocado' (the historical
    98	 * hardcode) only if the store itself is unreadable, never on a mere
    99	 * unfamiliar value (that normalizes to null → avocado too).
   100	 */
   101	export function resolveScheduledContentMode(): SchedulerContentMode {
   102	    try {
   103	        return normalizeSchedulerContentMode(kiraContentModeStore.getDefault()) ?? 'avocado';
   104	    } catch {
   105	        return 'avocado';
   106	    }
   107	}

   109	/** Which seed tiers a mode may draw from (cumulative; neutral never reaches
   110	 *  the Kira pools — it has its own still-life stream — but degrades to apple
   111	 *  defensively). */
   112	function tiersFor(mode: SchedulerContentMode): ReadonlyArray<'apple' | 'banana' | 'avocado'> {

  1241	
  1242	/**
  1243	 * VLM seed-adequacy question — a strict one-word verdict + reason. The
  1244	 * uncensored qwen VLM (LM Studio :1234) reasons freely; we key on the verdict.
  1245	 */
  1246	export function seedQaQuestion(action: string): string {
  1247	    return `You are QA for an image-to-video SEED frame. The intended action to animate is: "${action}". `
  1248	        + `Judge ONLY whether this still is a usable i2v seed: is the subject a coherent, on-model young woman; `
  1249	        + `is the pose correct and clearly set up for that action; and is the anatomy intact `
  1250	        + `(no melted/extra/missing limbs, no distortion)? `
  1251	        + `Reply with exactly one word on the first line — ADEQUATE or INADEQUATE — then a short reason.`;
  1252	}
  1253	
  1254	/**
  1255	 * Parse a VLM adequacy reply into a boolean. Fail-open: only an explicit
  1256	 * INADEQUATE rejects; empty / ambiguous / null accept (a missing VLM signal
  1257	 * must never block the cycle).
  1258	 */
  1259	export function parseVlmVerdict(text: string | null | undefined): boolean {
  1260	    if (!text || !text.trim()) return true;
  1261	    if (/\bINADEQUATE\b/i.test(text)) return false;
  1262	    return true;
  1263	}
  1264	
  1265	export interface SeedQADeps {
  1266	    /** Render a fresh seed (a new random seed each call), or null on failure. */
  1267	    generateSeed: (attempt: number) => Promise<string | null>;
  1268	    /**
  1269	     * VLM analyzer: (imagePath, question) → free-form reply (or null when the
  1270	     * VLM is unavailable). null/undefined disables the check (fail-open accept).
  1271	     */
  1272	    analyze?: ((imagePath: string, question: string) => Promise<string | null>) | null;
  1273	    /** The bare action the seed must set up (for the QA question). */
  1274	    action: string;
  1275	    maxAttempts?: number;
  1276	    log?: (msg: string) => void;
  1277	}
  1278	
  1279	/**
  1280	 * Generate a seed frame and VLM-check it's a usable i2v seed, regenerating up
  1281	 * to `maxAttempts` times and returning the first ADEQUATE render. Fail-open at
  1282	 * every edge: no VLM → accept the first render; a VLM error → accept that seed;
  1283	 * all attempts INADEQUATE → ship the first usable render (a mediocre seed beats
  1284	 * no video). Pure control flow (renders + VLM injected) — exported for tests.
  1285	 */
  1286	export async function generateSeedWithQA(deps: SeedQADeps): Promise<string | null> {
  1287	    const max = Math.max(1, deps.maxAttempts ?? SEED_QA_MAX_ATTEMPTS);
  1288	    const log = deps.log ?? ((m: string) => console.log(m));
  1289	    let firstUsable: string | null = null;
  1290	    for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= max; attempt++) {
  1291	        let path: string | null = null;
  1292	        try {
  1293	            path = await deps.generateSeed(attempt);
  1294	        } catch (err) {
  1295	            log(`[kira-content-scheduler] seed attempt ${attempt} threw: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);
  1296	            continue;
  1297	        }
  1298	        if (!path) {
  1299	            log(`[kira-content-scheduler] seed attempt ${attempt}: no image`);
  1300	            continue;
  1301	        }
  1302	        if (!firstUsable) firstUsable = path;
  1303	        if (!deps.analyze) return path; // no VLM → accept first render (fail-open)
  1304	        let verdict: string | null = null;
  1305	        try {
  1306	            verdict = await deps.analyze(path, seedQaQuestion(deps.action));
  1307	        } catch {
  1308	            log(`[kira-content-scheduler] seed QA attempt ${attempt}: VLM error — accepting seed (fail-open)`);
  1309	            return path;
  1310	        }
  1311	        const ok = parseVlmVerdict(verdict);
  1312	        log(`[kira-content-scheduler] seed QA attempt ${attempt}: ${ok ? 'ADEQUATE' : 'INADEQUATE'}`);
  1313	        if (ok) return path;
  1314	    }
  1315	    if (firstUsable) {
  1316	        log(`[kira-content-scheduler] seed QA: ${max} attempts all INADEQUATE — using best-effort first render`);
  1317	    }
  1318	    return firstUsable;
  1319	}

   875	// -- I2V input transfer (server → Mac) --
   876	
   877	/** Stable Mac-side staging dir for server-local i2v source images. */
   878	const I2V_REMOTE_STAGING_DIR = '/tmp/comfybox-i2v';
   879	
   880	/**
   881	 * Stage a server-local i2v source image on the Mac. ComfyBox reads
   882	 * image_path on the MAC (live 2026-07-07 catalog: a server-only path gets a
   883	 * 400 'image_path not found') — mirror of the output direction's
   884	 * fetchComfyBoxRemote. Uses the injected pusher (tests) or ssh mkdir -p +
   885	 * scp via config.comfyboxSsh.
   886	 *
   887	 * Returns the path to submit as image_path:
   888	 *   - '/Users/' paths are already Mac-side → returned verbatim, no push
   889	 *   - no push route configured → returned verbatim (today's pass-through)
   890	 *   - pushed OK → the /tmp/comfybox-i2v/<basename> staging path
   891	 *   - push failed → null (caller must error out, never submit)
   892	 */
   893	async function pushComfyBoxSource(config: VideoConfig, localPath: string): Promise<string | null> {
   894	    if (localPath.startsWith('/Users/')) return localPath;
   895	    if (!config.pushRemoteFile && !config.comfyboxSsh) return localPath;
   896	    const remotePath = `${I2V_REMOTE_STAGING_DIR}/${path.basename(localPath)}`;
   897	    if (config.pushRemoteFile) {
   898	        try { return (await config.pushRemoteFile(localPath, remotePath)) ? remotePath : null; }
   899	        catch { return null; }
   900	    }
   901	    try {
   902	        // scp cannot create the target dir — ensure it first via ssh.
   903	        await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
   904	            execFile('ssh', ['-o', 'StrictHostKeyChecking=no', '-o', 'ConnectTimeout=10',
   905	                config.comfyboxSsh!, 'mkdir', '-p', I2V_REMOTE_STAGING_DIR],
   906	                { timeout: 30_000 }, (err) => err ? reject(err) : resolve());
   907	        });
   908	        await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
   909	            execFile('scp', ['-o', 'StrictHostKeyChecking=no', '-o', 'ConnectTimeout=10',
   910	                localPath, `${config.comfyboxSsh}:${remotePath}`],
   911	                { timeout: 120_000 }, (err) => err ? reject(err) : resolve());
   912	        });
   913	        return remotePath;
   914	    } catch (err) {
   915	        console.warn('[video] i2v source push failed:', err instanceof Error ? err.message : err);
   916	        return null;
   917	    }
   918	}
   919	
   920	// -- I2V via ComfyBox MCP --
   921	
   922	async function generateI2V(
   923	    config: VideoConfig,
   924	    promptRaw: string,
   925	    imagePath: string,
   926	    character: string,
   927	    aspectRatio: string,
   928	    params: Record<string, any>,
   929	    internal: VideoInternalOptions,
   930	) {
   931	    const resolution = String(params.resolution || '480p');
   932	    let prompt = promptRaw;
   933	    // LTX carries a fixed negative string (FDD 2026-07-12); Wan does not. Only
   934	    // set on the non-sealed LTX path — undefined leaves the submit unchanged.
   935	    let i2vNegative: string | undefined;
   936	
   937	    // Skip local fs check for Mac-hosted paths
   938	    const isRemoteMacPath = imagePath.startsWith('/Users/');
   939	    if (!isRemoteMacPath && !fs.existsSync(imagePath)) {
   940	        return { error: `Source image not found: ${imagePath}` };
   941	    }
   942	
   943	    // Rule: i2v aspect matches the source image (Todd 2026-07-14). Derive it
   944	    // from the source frame's real dimensions; the source image wins over a
   945	    // caller-supplied aspect so a mismatch can't crop/pad the framing. No-ops
   946	    // safely (keeps `aspectRatio`) for remote/non-PNG/unreadable sources.
   947	    const sourceAspect = readImageAspectRatio(imagePath, aspectRatio);
   948	    if (sourceAspect !== aspectRatio) {
   949	        console.log(`[video][i2v] aspect matched to source image: ${aspectRatio} → ${sourceAspect}`);
   950	        aspectRatio = sourceAspect;
   951	    }
   952	
   953	    // Optimize motion prompt via LLM.
   954	    // Sealed path (D3): author LOCALLY, seal, resolve just for the ComfyBox call.
   955	    // `internal.skipOptimize` lets an upstream caller (photoshoot_render)
   956	    // that has already run the sealed authoring bypass this step. It's a
   957	    // separate function argument (not a tool param) so an external caller
   958	    // cannot smuggle `skipOptimize: true` through the tool schema.
   959	    const sealing = shouldSeal(config.sealedOrchestrator);
   960	    const skipOptimize = internal.skipOptimize;
   961	    // Belt-and-braces coupling assertion: skipOptimize is only ever set by
   962	    // photoshoot_render AFTER its own sealed authoring. If a future refactor
   963	    // splits video sealing from photoshoot sealing, this catches the
   964	    // silent-drift case — skipping I2V's optimizer with no outer seal store
   965	    // to guard the return would be a fail-open regression.
   966	    if (skipOptimize && !sealing) {
   967	        throw new Error('[video][i2v] internal.skipOptimize=true requires sealing to be active on this VideoConfig — fail-closed');
   968	    }
   969	
   970	    // Scene-anchored motion (Todd 2026-07-14): a generic motion prompt makes LTX
   971	    // re-imagine a stock bedroom mid-clip. Prepend the source frame's OWN scene
   972	    // (its embedded generation prompt, read from the PNG's EXIF ImageDescription)
   973	    // so the video animates the ACTUAL scene. The scene is already a rich local
   974	    // prompt, so we use it verbatim + the motion and SKIP the motion re-optimizer.
   975	    // Non-sealed only (Kira's path); the sealed authoring flow is left untouched.
   976	    let sceneAnchored = false;
   977	    if (!sealing) {
   978	        const scene = readImageScenePrompt(imagePath);
   979	        if (scene) {
   980	            prompt = `${scene} ${promptRaw}`.trim();
   981	            sceneAnchored = true;
   982	            console.log(`[video][i2v] scene-anchored from source PNG (${scene.length} chars) + motion: "${promptRaw.slice(0, 60)}"`);
   983	        }
   984	    }
   985	
   986	    let sealStore: SealedPayloadStore | null = null;
   987	    if (sealing && !skipOptimize) {
   988	        const localBase = config.sealedOrchestrator!.localContentBaseUrl;
   989	        if (!localBase) {
   990	            throw new Error('[video][i2v] sealed mode enabled but no localContentBaseUrl configured — fail-closed');
   991	        }
   992	        // Local-author config: spread promptOptimizer for model/timeout/etc,
   993	        // then FORCE baseUrl to localBase and CLEAR apiKey. A cloud bearer
   994	        // token (OpenRouter/Anthropic) must never leave the box addressed to
   995	        // a local endpoint — even a local endpoint could log/persist headers.
   996	        // Order matters: apiKey/baseUrl must come AFTER the spread.
   997	        const localOptimizer: PromptOptimizerConfig = {
   998	            model: config.promptOptimizer?.model || 'openai/gpt-4.1-mini',
   999	            ...config.promptOptimizer,
  1000	            baseUrl: localBase,
  1001	            enabled: true,
  1002	            apiKey: '',
  1003	        };
  1004	        const prepared = await runWithSealedStore(async (store) => {
  1005	            sealStore = store;
  1006	            return prepareSealedImagePrompt(
  1007	                store,
  1008	                async (intent) => {
  1009	                    const o = await optimizeI2VPrompt(intent, localOptimizer, { model: String(params.model || 'ltx') });
  1010	                    // `optimizeI2VPrompt` catches its own errors and returns
  1011	                    // `{ prompt: rawPrompt, enhanced: false }` on any failure.
  1012	                    // Under seal we MUST NOT fall back to the caller's raw
  1013	                    // (cloud-authored) intent as the "authored" prompt — that
  1014	                    // would defeat the whole point. Require enhancement.
  1015	                    if (!o.enhanced || !o.prompt || !o.prompt.trim()) {
  1015	                    if (!o.enhanced || !o.prompt || !o.prompt.trim()) {
  1016	                        throw new Error('[video][i2v] local motion optimizer did not enhance the prompt — fail-closed (no raw fallback under seal)');
  1017	                    }
  1018	                    return o.prompt;
  1019	                },
  1020	                prompt,
  1021	                { contentMode: internal.contentMode },
  1022	            );
  1023	        });
  1024	        prompt = prepared.resolvedPrompt;
  1025	    } else if (config.promptOptimizer?.enabled && !skipOptimize && !sceneAnchored) {
  1026	        const optimized = await optimizeI2VPrompt(prompt, config.promptOptimizer, { model: String(params.model || 'ltx') });
  1027	        if (optimized.enhanced) {
  1028	            console.log(`[video] I2V prompt optimized`);
  1029	            prompt = optimized.prompt;
  1030	            if (optimized.negativePrompt) i2vNegative = optimized.negativePrompt;
  1031	        }
  1032	    }
  1033	
  1034	    if (sealing) {
  1035	        console.log(`[video] I2V ${resolution} via ComfyBox MCP (sealed — prompt withheld from logs)`);
  1036	    } else {
  1037	        console.log(`[video] I2V ${resolution} via ComfyBox MCP: "${prompt.slice(0, 80)}..."`);
  1038	    }
  1039	
  1040	    if (!config.executor) {
  1041	        return { error: 'ToolExecutor not available — required for ComfyBox MCP routing' };
  1042	    }
  1043	
  1044	    // Stage a server-local source on the Mac before submit — ComfyBox reads
  1045	    // image_path Mac-side. Pass-through when no push route is configured.
  1046	    // (Echoing imagePath in the error is seal-safe: it's caller-supplied.)
  1047	    const submitImagePath = await pushComfyBoxSource(config, imagePath);
  1048	    if (!submitImagePath) {
  1049	        return { error: `Failed to push source image to ComfyBox host: ${imagePath}` };
  1050	    }
  1051	
  1052	    // Character/content-mode preset (server resolves LoRAs + prompt shaping).
  1053	    // Absent key = byte-identical submit for unmapped characters.
  1054	    const i2vPreset = resolveVideoPreset(config, character, internal.contentMode, params);
  1055	
  1056	    // i2v motion recipe (Todd-approved 2026-07-28, memory reference_kira_8s_video_recipe;
  1057	    // extended to solo 2026-07-29): ALL clips get cfg 3.0 / strength 0.5, rendered
  1058	    // SINGLE-PASS for ≤12s (no chunking → kills the last-third color + motion drift
  1059	    // AND the continuation-chunk guidance bug). Motion energy comes from the server's
  1060	    // LTX2_COND_FPS env (temporal-RoPE dial); img_compression is left at the engine
  1061	    // default (the old comp30 lever plasticized dark-morena skin). Solo previously ran
  1062	    // on ComfyBox's schema default strength 1.0 — the hardest frame-0 hold, implicated
  1063	    // in solo close-frame face ghosting. Logic is in computeI2VMotionRecipe.
  1064	    const motionText = `${promptRaw} ${prompt}`;
  1065	    const { isAction, recipe: motionRecipe, singlePassFrames } =
  1066	        computeI2VMotionRecipe(motionText, Number(params.duration) || 0, internal.contentMode);
  1067	    console.log(`[video][i2v] ${isAction ? 'action' : 'solo'} recipe: str ${motionRecipe.strength} / cfg ${motionRecipe.guidance} (motion via server cond-fps env)${singlePassFrames ? ` / single-pass ${singlePassFrames}f` : ''}`);
  1068	
  1069	    // Provenance (2026-07-29): every render gets a concrete diffusion seed —
  1070	    // caller-supplied or minted — sent to ComfyBox and persisted in the
  1071	    // sidecar together with the render params, so any clip is exactly
  1072	    // reproducible (the 480p/720p ghosting A/B hinges on matched seeds).
  1073	    const diffusionSeed = resolveDiffusionSeed(params.seed);
  1074	
  1075	    // SexGod LoRA activation (Todd 2026-07-29): its model card names
  1076	    // `LTXNUDES` as the trigger; without it the nudity LoRA runs unactivated.
  1077	    // NSFW registers only — see src/video/sexgod-trigger.ts.
  1078	    prompt = applySexgodTrigger(prompt, internal.contentMode);
  1079	
  1080	
  1081	    // Submit via ComfyBox MCP generate_video tool
  1082	    const submitResult = await config.executor.execute('mcp_comfybox__generate_video', {
  1083	        prompt,
  1084	        image_path: submitImagePath,
  1085	        mode: 'i2v',
  1086	        // The daemon has ALREADY optimized this prompt above. Without this flag
  1087	        // ComfyBox re-enhances it server-side — two rewrites drift the prompt off
  1088	        // concrete staging (limb placement, figure count) and double-inject the
  1089	        // character description. Observed 2026-08-02 on the t2v path: a
  1090	        // daemon-optimized scene was re-optimized to 952 chars with a second
  1091	        // character injection.
  1092	        enhance: false,
  1093	        resolution,
  1094	        aspect_ratio: aspectRatio,
  1095	        ...motionRecipe,
  1096	        seed: diffusionSeed,
  1097	        // LTX frame-rate conditioning (#1439): explicit on every submit rather
  1098	        // than relying on the render host's LTX2_COND_FPS env, which drifted
  1099	        // to 24 vs the trained recipe's 25 (docs/methods/nsfw-ltx-t2v-recipe.md).
  1100	        frame_rate: LTX_CONDITIONING_FPS,
  1101	        ...(singlePassFrames ? { frames: singlePassFrames } : {}),
  1102	        model: params.model || 'ltx',   // LTX = fast local segments (Todd 2026-07-05); 'wan' for longer/quality
  1103	        // Long clips: the server maps duration beyond one ~4s chunk onto
  1104	        // chunked continuation (each chunk re-anchors on the previous
  1105	        // chunk's last frame). Only sent when the caller asked for one.
  1106	        ...(!singlePassFrames && Number(params.duration) > 0 ? { duration: Number(params.duration) } : {}),
  1107	        // Gated: only send negative_prompt when explicitly enabled AND set (LTX
  1108	        // path). Default off → key absent → submit byte-identical to today.
  1109	        ...(config.ltxNegativePrompt === true && i2vNegative ? { negative_prompt: i2vNegative } : {}),
  1110	        ...(i2vPreset ? { preset: i2vPreset } : {}),
  1111	        // Sprint 2 wiring (#1443/#1444): only send when explicitly requested —
  1112	        // absent keys keep the submit byte-identical for older ComfyBox builds.
  1113	        ...(params.quality === 'hq' ? { quality: 'hq' } : {}),
  1114	        ...(params.audio !== false ? { audio: true } : {})  // default ON (2026-08-05),
  1115	    });
  1116	
  1117	    const submitData = mcpData(submitResult);
  1118	
  1119	    // ComfyBox generate_video may run SYNCHRONOUSLY — it blocks for the full
  1120	    // render and returns the finished output inline with NO job_id (#1281). The
  1121	    // async job_id+poll branch below then mis-read that as "submit failed" and
  1122	    // discarded a perfectly-rendered mp4. Handle the sync shape first: if the
  1123	    // response already carries an output path, save it directly.
  1124	    const syncOutput = (typeof submitData?.output_path === 'string' && submitData.output_path)
  1125	        || (typeof submitData?.local_path === 'string' && submitData.local_path)
  1126	        || (typeof submitData?.video_path === 'string' && submitData.video_path)
  1127	        || '';
  1128	    if (syncOutput) {
  1129	        const saved = await saveComfyBoxVideo(config, syncOutput, prompt, character, 'i2v', {
  1130	            sourcePath: imagePath,
  1131	            sealing,
  1132	            promptRaw,
  1133	            resolution,
  1134	            aspectRatio,
  1135	            model: String(params.model || 'ltx'),
  1136	            contentMode: internal.contentMode,
  1137	            seed: diffusionSeed,
  1138	            strength: motionRecipe.strength,
  1139	            guidance: motionRecipe.guidance,
  1140	            frames: singlePassFrames || undefined,
  1141	            quality: params.quality === 'hq' ? 'hq' : undefined,
  1142	            audio: params.audio !== false || undefined  // default ON for local A/V (2026-08-05); pass audio:false to opt out,
  1143	        });
  1144	        if (sealing && sealStore) {
  1145	            guardSealedResult(sealStore, JSON.stringify(saved));
  1146	        }
  1147	        return saved;
  1148	    }
  1149	
  1150	    if (!submitData?.job_id) {
  1151	        const detail = submitData?.error || submitResult.error || 'no job_id';
  1152	        if (sealing) {
  1153	            // Sealed turn: the submitted prompt is local-only; never echo a
  1154	            // provider error verbatim (may contain the resolved prompt).
  1155	            console.error(`[video][i2v][sealed] ComfyBox submit failed: ${detail}`);
  1156	            return { error: 'ComfyBox video submit failed (sealed turn — provider details withheld locally)' };
  1157	        }
  1158	        // Non-sealed: log the raw shape so any further response-shape drift is
  1159	        // captured (this path has burned several key/nesting assumptions).
  1160	        console.warn(`[video][i2v] no job_id and no sync output; submitData=${JSON.stringify(submitData)?.slice(0, 400)}`);
  1161	        return { error: `ComfyBox video submit failed: ${detail}` };
  1162	    }
  1163	
  1164	    // Poll via ComfyBox MCP video_status tool
  1165	    const jobId = submitData.job_id;
  1166	    const deadlineMs = computeVideoDeadlineMs(params.duration, params.quality);
  1167	    const deadline = Date.now() + deadlineMs;
  1168	    // Lost-job fail-fast (2026-07-25): a Mac server crash/restart mid-render
  1169	    // wipes its in-memory job tracker, so every later poll errors "Video job
  1170	    // not found" — waiting out the full deadline burns 20+ min polling a
  1171	    // ghost (10:48 incident: chunk-boundary Metal abort, 6 min of 404s, then
  1172	    // a spurious "timed out"). Three consecutive not-found polls = job lost.
  1173	    let notFoundStreak = 0;
  1174	    let errorStreak = 0;
  1175	    while (Date.now() < deadline) {
  1176	        await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, POLL_INTERVAL_MS));
  1177	        const statusResult = await config.executor.execute('mcp_comfybox__video_status', { job_id: jobId });
  1178	        const status = mcpData(statusResult);
  1179	        if (!status) {
  1180	            // Sustained poll failure is terminal EITHER way. Counting only
  1181	            // literal "not found" text doesn't work in practice: the executor's
  1182	            // circuit breaker opens after a couple of 404s and replaces the
  1183	            // error text with "circuit breaker open", resetting a text-matched
  1184	            // streak forever — observed 2026-07-26 02:22: a ghost job polled
  1185	            // for the full 3900s deadline. A healthy render polls clean
  1186	            // continuously, so ANY long error streak means the job is gone or
  1187	            // the integration is down.
  1188	            const errText = String(statusResult?.error ?? '');
  1189	            if (/not found/i.test(errText)) notFoundStreak++;
  1190	            errorStreak++;
  1191	            if (notFoundStreak >= 3 || errorStreak >= 12) {
  1192	                const why = notFoundStreak >= 3
  1193	                    ? 'lost by ComfyBox (server restarted mid-render?)'
  1194	                    : `status polling failed ${errorStreak}x consecutively`;
  1195	                return { error: `video job ${jobId} ${why} — failing fast: ${errText.slice(0, 200)}` };
  1196	            }
  1197	            continue;
  1198	        }
  1199	        notFoundStreak = 0;
  1200	        errorStreak = 0;
  1201	
  1202	        if ((status?.status === 'completed' || status?.status === 'succeeded') && status?.output_path) {
  1203	            const saved = await saveComfyBoxVideo(config, status.output_path, prompt, character, 'i2v', {
  1204	                sourcePath: imagePath,
  1205	                sealing,
  1206	                promptRaw,
  1207	                resolution,
  1208	                aspectRatio,
  1209	                model: String(params.model || 'ltx'),
  1210	                contentMode: internal.contentMode,
  1211	                seed: diffusionSeed,
  1212	                strength: motionRecipe.strength,
  1213	                guidance: motionRecipe.guidance,
  1214	                frames: singlePassFrames || undefined,
  1215	                quality: params.quality === 'hq' ? 'hq' : undefined,
  1216	                audio: params.audio !== false || undefined  // default ON for local A/V (2026-08-05); pass audio:false to opt out,
  1217	            });
  1218	            if (sealing && sealStore) {
  1219	                guardSealedResult(sealStore, JSON.stringify(saved));
  1220	            }
  1221	            return saved;
  1222	        }
  1223	        if (status?.status === 'failed') {
  1224	            const detail = status.error || 'unknown';
  1225	            if (sealing) {
  1226	                console.error(`[video][i2v][sealed] ComfyBox generation failed: ${detail}`);
  1227	                return { error: 'I2V generation failed (sealed turn — provider details withheld locally)' };
  1228	            }
  1229	            return { error: `I2V generation failed: ${detail}` };
  1230	        }
  1250	    } catch (err) {
  1251	        console.warn(`[video] Failed to cancel ComfyBox job ${jobId}: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);
  1252	    }
  1253	}
  1254	
  1255	// -- T2V via ComfyBox MCP --
  1256	
  1257	async function generateT2V(
  1258	    config: VideoConfig,
  1259	    promptRaw: string,
  1260	    character: string,
  1261	    aspectRatio: string,
  1262	    params: Record<string, any>,
  1263	    internal: VideoInternalOptions,
  1264	) {
  1265	    const isNsfw = internal.contentMode === 'banana' || internal.contentMode === 'avocado';
  1266	    const duration = Number(params.duration) || (isNsfw ? T2V_NSFW_DEFAULT_DURATION_SEC : T2V_DEFAULT_DURATION_SEC);
  1267	    const resolution = String(params.resolution || '720p');
  1268	    let prompt = promptRaw;
  1269	    // LTX carries a fixed negative string (FDD 2026-07-12); Wan does not. Only
  1270	    // set on the non-sealed LTX path — undefined leaves the submit unchanged.
  1271	    let t2vNegative: string | undefined;
  1272	
  1273	    // Optimize cinematic prompt via LLM.
  1274	    // Sealed path (D3): author LOCALLY, seal, resolve just for the ComfyBox call.
  1275	    const sealing = shouldSeal(config.sealedOrchestrator);
  1276	    const skipOptimize = internal.skipOptimize;
  1277	    // Same coupling assertion as generateI2V.
  1278	    if (skipOptimize && !sealing) {
  1279	        throw new Error('[video][t2v] internal.skipOptimize=true requires sealing to be active on this VideoConfig — fail-closed');
  1280	    }
  1281	    let sealStore: SealedPayloadStore | null = null;
  1282	    if (sealing && !skipOptimize) {
  1283	        const localBase = config.sealedOrchestrator!.localContentBaseUrl;
  1284	        if (!localBase) {
  1285	            throw new Error('[video][t2v] sealed mode enabled but no localContentBaseUrl configured — fail-closed');
  1286	        }
  1287	        // Local-author config: spread promptOptimizer for model/timeout/etc,
  1288	        // then FORCE baseUrl to localBase and CLEAR apiKey. A cloud bearer
  1289	        // token (OpenRouter/Anthropic) must never leave the box addressed to
  1290	        // a local endpoint — even a local endpoint could log/persist headers.
  1291	        // Order matters: apiKey/baseUrl must come AFTER the spread.
  1292	        const localOptimizer: PromptOptimizerConfig = {
  1293	            model: config.promptOptimizer?.model || 'openai/gpt-4.1-mini',
  1294	            ...config.promptOptimizer,
  1295	            baseUrl: localBase,
  1296	            enabled: true,
  1297	            apiKey: '',
  1298	        };
  1299	        const prepared = await runWithSealedStore(async (store) => {
  1300	            sealStore = store;
  1301	            return prepareSealedImagePrompt(
  1302	                store,
  1303	                async (intent) => {
  1304	                    const o = await optimizeT2VPrompt(intent, localOptimizer, { character, model: String(params.model || 'ltx') });
  1305	                    // Same fail-closed contract as I2V: on optimizer fallback
  1306	                    // (endpoint down, refused, empty) we must not seal the
  1307	                    // caller's raw cloud-authored intent as the "authored"
  1308	                    // prompt. Require enhancement.
  1309	                    if (!o.enhanced || !o.prompt || !o.prompt.trim()) {
  1310	                        throw new Error('[video][t2v] local cinematic optimizer did not enhance the prompt — fail-closed (no raw fallback under seal)');
  1311	                    }
  1312	                    return o.prompt;
  1313	                },
  1314	                prompt,
  1315	                { contentMode: internal.contentMode, character },
  1316	            );
  1317	        });
  1318	        prompt = prepared.resolvedPrompt;
  1319	    } else if (config.promptOptimizer?.enabled && !skipOptimize) {
  1320	        const optimized = await optimizeT2VPrompt(prompt, config.promptOptimizer, { character, model: String(params.model || 'ltx') });
  1321	        if (optimized.enhanced) {
  1322	            console.log(`[video] T2V prompt optimized`);
  1323	            prompt = optimized.prompt;
  1324	            if (optimized.negativePrompt) t2vNegative = optimized.negativePrompt;
  1325	        }
  1326	    }
  1327	
  1328	    console.log(`[video] T2V ${duration}s ${resolution} via ComfyBox MCP`);
  1329	
  1330	    if (!config.executor) {
  1331	        return { error: 'ToolExecutor not available — required for ComfyBox MCP routing' };
  1332	    }
  1333	
  1334	    // Character/content-mode preset — same mapping as I2V.
  1335	    const t2vPreset = resolveVideoPreset(config, character, internal.contentMode, params);
  1336	    // Act-LoRA selection (Todd 2026-07-30 item 3): the generic banana/avocado
  1337	    // presets carry only the condsafe distill LoRA, so partnered clips got no
  1338	    // act guidance. Match the position in the prompt to its trained preset;
  1339	    // non-matching scenes keep the generic preset unchanged.
  1340	    const t2vActPreset = selectVideoPreset({
  1341	        prompt, contentMode: internal.contentMode, generic: t2vPreset, explicit: params?.preset,
  1342	    });
  1343	
  1344	    // SexGod LoRA activation (Todd 2026-07-29) — NSFW registers only.
  1345	    prompt = applySexgodTrigger(prompt, internal.contentMode);
  1346	
  1347	
  1348	    // Provenance (2026-07-29): same seed contract as I2V — see resolveDiffusionSeed.
  1349	    const diffusionSeed = resolveDiffusionSeed(params.seed);
  1350	
  1351	    const submitResult = await config.executor.execute('mcp_comfybox__generate_video', {
  1352	        prompt,
  1353	        model: params.model || 'ltx',
  1354	        mode: 't2v',
  1355	        // The daemon has ALREADY optimized this prompt above. Without this flag
  1356	        // ComfyBox re-enhances it server-side — two rewrites drift the prompt off
  1357	        // concrete staging (limb placement, figure count) and double-inject the
  1358	        // character description. Observed 2026-08-02: a daemon-optimized scene was
  1359	        // re-optimized to 952 chars with a second character injection.
  1360	        enhance: false,
  1361	
  1362	        duration,
  1363	        resolution,
  1364	        aspect_ratio: aspectRatio,
  1365	        seed: diffusionSeed,
  1366	        // LTX frame-rate conditioning (#1439) — same rationale as the i2v submit above.
  1367	        frame_rate: LTX_CONDITIONING_FPS,
  1368	        // T2V guidance (Todd 2026-07-30 item 1). The distilled pipeline's
  1369	        // config default is 1.0 = CFG OFF, and this path previously sent no
  1370	        // guidance at all, so t2v clips ignored the described action/camera.

 succeeded in 0ms:
   260	 * i2v motion recipe (Todd-approved 2026-07-28; memory reference_kira_8s_video_recipe;
   261	 * extended to solo 2026-07-29). ALL i2v clips get cfg 3.0 / strength 0.5 and render
   262	 * SINGLE-PASS for durations ≤12s — one pass eliminates the last-third color/motion
   263	 * drift and the continuation-chunk guidance bug that plagued chunked long clips.
   264	 * Motion energy comes from the server's LTX2_COND_FPS env, not compression (the old
   265	 * comp30 lever plasticized dark-morena skin), so img_compression is left at the
   266	 * engine default.
   267	 *
   268	 * 2026-07-29: solo clips no longer return an empty recipe. "Server fidelity
   269	 * defaults" turned out to be ComfyBox's schema default strength 1.0 — the hardest
   270	 * frame-0 conditioning hold — implicated in solo close-frame face ghosting (the
   271	 * seed frame's facial features persist as a static overlay while the head moves;
   272	 * controlled arms at str 0.5/0.35 both killed the ghost, action clips at 0.5
   273	 * never showed it). isAction remains for logging/telemetry. Pure + testable.
   274	 *
   275	 * 2026-07-31 (#1440, re-affirmed by SG alignment): banana/avocado clips get
   276	 * a raised single-pass frame FLOOR — 241f instead of the SFW 97f. The
   277	 * author's published workflow renders 14s x 24fps = 337f (the latent's "121"
   278	 * widget is stale — length is link-fed), so 241f sits comfortably inside his
   279	 * demonstrated range. NOTE: 241f clips looked smeared on the OLD single-pass
   280	 * plain-euler stack (2026-07-31 evening regression) — long clips need the
   281	 * author's cfg_pp + two-stage + sigma stack (LTX2_* env on the render host)
   282	 * that shipped with this alignment. 241f = 1+8*30. It's a MINIMUM, not a
   283	 * fixed value: Math.max means an explicit longer duration still produces
   284	 * more frames (capped at 289f) — the floor only lifts clips that would
   285	 * otherwise render shorter.
   286	 */
   287	export function computeI2VMotionRecipe(
   288	    promptText: string,
   289	    durationSec: number,
   290	    contentMode?: VideoContentMode,
   291	): { isAction: boolean; recipe: { strength?: number; guidance?: number }; singlePassFrames: number } {
   292	    const t = String(promptText).toLowerCase();
   293	    const hasPartner = /\b(older man|man |partner|behind her|him|his |thrust|penetrat|doggy|cowgirl|riding|spooning|missionary|blowjob|standing sex)\b/.test(t);
   294	    const hasAction = /\b(thrust|penetrat|rock|bounce|riding|driving|grind|pump|slam)\b/.test(t);
   295	    const isAction = hasPartner && hasAction;
   296	    const d = Number(durationSec) || 0;
   297	    const isNsfw = contentMode === 'banana' || contentMode === 'avocado';
   298	    // NSFW_MIN_FRAMES 241 vs the SFW/neutral 97f floor — see the doc note above.
   299	    const minFrames = isNsfw ? 241 : 97;
   300	    // 289f = 1+8*36 (LTX trained window). frames overrides the chunked `duration` path.
   301	    // ALL clips ≤12s render single-pass, not just action clips (2026-07-28 fix):
   302	    // the chunked continuation path degenerates in the second chunk — verified
   303	    // live when the first non-avocado rotation cycles rendered solo concepts.
   304	    const singlePassFrames = d > 0 && d <= 12
   305	        ? Math.min(289, Math.max(minFrames, Math.round((d * LTX_CONDITIONING_FPS - 1) / 8) * 8 + 1))
   306	        : 0;
   307	    // One recipe for every i2v clip (solo included, 2026-07-29 — see doc above).
   308	    // strength 0.5 (REVERTED 2026-08-03 after ~3h at 0.75): at Kira's real
   309	    // config (832x448, 241f single-pass) 0.75 collapses mid-clip — subject
   310	    // balloons toward camera, faces smear, scene washes out. NAG carries
   311	    // prompt adherence but does NOT substitute for the seed hold on long
   312	    // single passes. 0.5 is the validated value; a proper 0.5/0.6/0.75 ladder
   313	    // at production config is the way back up, not another point change.
   314	    // guidance 1.0 as of 2026-08-02 (was 3.0). ComfyBox now implements NAG
   315	    // (Normalized Attention Guidance), which is how the reference PinkCherry
   316	    // author gets prompt adherence — he runs cfg 1.0 on BOTH passes with
   317	    // NAG 11.0/0.25/2.5, verified from the API graphs embedded in his published
   318	    // mp4s across v1.6/v1.7/v1.8. NAG replaces high CFG rather than supplementing
   319	    // it: CFG extrapolates the final prediction with no magnitude bound, while
   320	    // NAG extrapolates inside cross-attention and renormalises, so adherence
   321	    // rises without driving channels into saturation.
   322	    //
   323	    // Leaving i2v at 3.0 after NAG shipped stacked BOTH mechanisms, which is the
   324	    // double-guidance NAG exists to avoid. Validated at Kira's own i2v config
   325	    // (1280x704, strength 0.5, 97f, matched seed): sharpness 7.91 -> 7.96,
   326	    // brightness held, cyan-blown pixels 0.58% of frame — below the t2v render
   327	    // Todd approved (0.78%) and an order of magnitude under the cfg 3.5 failure
   328	    // (~8.7%).
   329	    return { isAction, recipe: { strength: 0.5, guidance: 1.0 }, singlePassFrames };
   330	}
   331

   334	 * remediation — provenance). Mirrors the image path's pattern: an explicit
   335	 * caller seed passes through; anything else mints a random one. The resolved
   336	 * seed is ALWAYS sent to ComfyBox and recorded in the sidecar, so any clip can
   337	 * be re-rendered exactly (matched-seed A/B experiments need this). Range is
   338	 * int32-positive to stay safely inside ComfyBox's integer parsing.
   339	 */
   340	export function resolveDiffusionSeed(raw: unknown): number {

    97	})();
    98	
    99	(function testNsfwFloorIsAMinimumNotAFixedValue(): void {
   100	    // An explicit duration that already clears 241f is NOT clamped down to
   101	    // it — the floor only lifts clips that would otherwise render shorter.
   102	    //
   103	    // Derived from LTX_CONDITIONING_FPS rather than hardcoded: #1440 was written
   104	    // against 24fps, where 10s landed on exactly 241f and the assertion read as
   105	    // "the floor is not applied". #1439 then moved the conditioning dial to
   106	    // 25fps, so 10s became 249f and this broke — the floor was fine, the
   107	    // arithmetic underneath it had moved. Computing the expectation keeps the
   108	    // PROPERTY under test (a duration above the floor is left alone) and lets
   109	    // the fps dial move again without a false failure.
   110	    const twelveSecondFrames = Math.round((12 * LTX_CONDITIONING_FPS - 1) / 8) * 8 + 1;
   111	    assert.ok(twelveSecondFrames > 241,
   112	        `12s must already clear the 241f floor at ${LTX_CONDITIONING_FPS}fps, else this test proves nothing`);
   113	    assert.strictEqual(computeI2VMotionRecipe(ACTION, 12, 'avocado').singlePassFrames, Math.min(289, twelveSecondFrames),
   114	        `12s avocado computes above the floor on its own (no floor applied, 289f cap)`);
   115	    assert.strictEqual(computeI2VMotionRecipe(ACTION, 12, 'avocado').singlePassFrames, 289,
   116	        '12s avocado still caps at 289f — an explicit long duration overrides the floor upward');
   117	    console.log('✓ NSFW floor is a minimum — explicit longer durations still win');
   118	})();
   119

   122	    assert.strictEqual(computeI2VMotionRecipe(ACTION, 16, 'avocado').singlePassFrames, 0,

   124	    console.log('✓ NSFW >12s clips stay on the chunked path too');

codex
A second high-confidence miss is in the daemon, not the engine: the “4s → 241f” behavior is deliberate. For banana/avocado, `computeI2VMotionRecipe` floors every ≤12s I2V request to 241 frames, and the submit sends `frames` instead of `duration`. So P2’s suspected duration-loss bug is misclassified; the code explicitly overrides 4 seconds to roughly 10 seconds. I’ll verify the final MCP-to-HTTP mapping before ranking it.

   457	  /// load_model -> POST /v1/model/load
   458	  private func executeLoadModel(_ params: MCPParams?) async throws -> MCPToolResult {
   459	    guard let model = params?.string("model"), !model.isEmpty else {
   460	      return MCPToolResult(error: "Error: 'model' is required")
   461	    }
   462	    var body: [String: Any] = ["model": model]
   463	    if let quantization = params?.string("quantization") {
   464	      body["quantization"] = quantization
   465	    }
   466	    if let activate = params?.bool("activate") {
   467	      body["activate"] = activate
   468	    }
   469	    if let wait = params?.bool("wait") {
   470	      body["wait"] = wait
   471	    }
   472	    let jsonData = try JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: body)
   473	    let (status, data) = try await client.post("/v1/model/load", body: jsonData)
   474	    // 202 is valid (async load in progress)
   475	    if status == 200 || status == 202 {
   476	      let text = String(data: data, encoding: .utf8) ?? "{}"
   477	      return MCPToolResult(text: text)
   478	    }
   479	    return mapHTTPResponse(status: status, data: data)
   480	  }
   481

   506	  /// generate_video -> POST /v1/video/generate/async (submit; poll video_status)
   507	  private func executeGenerateVideo(_ params: MCPParams?) async throws -> MCPToolResult {
   508	    guard let prompt = params?.string("prompt"), !prompt.isEmpty else {
   509	      return MCPToolResult(error: "Error: 'prompt' is required")
   510	    }
   511	
   512	    var body: [String: Any] = ["prompt": prompt]
   513	
   514	    if let imagePath = params?.string("image_path") {
   515	      body["image_path"] = imagePath
   516	    }
   517	    if let audio = params?.bool("audio") {
   518	      body["audio"] = audio
   519	    }
   520	    if let duration = params?.integer("duration") {
   521	      body["duration"] = duration
   522	    }
   523	    if let resolution = params?.string("resolution") {
   524	      body["resolution"] = resolution
   525	    }
   526	    if let aspectRatio = params?.string("aspect_ratio") {
   527	      body["aspect_ratio"] = aspectRatio
   528	    }
   529	    // T2V has no source frame, so translate aspect_ratio into explicit
   530	    // width/height here: WarmServer's video request decodes width/height, NOT
   531	    // aspect_ratio, so otherwise every t2v render fell back to the 704x448
   532	    // landscape default regardless of the requested orientation. Portrait
   533	    // ("9:16") swaps to 448x704 (both /64). I2V leaves dims unset so WarmServer
   534	    // keeps matching the source image aspect.
   535	    if params?.string("image_path") == nil {
   536	      // Respect caller-supplied dims; otherwise orient the standard t2v dims by
   537	      // aspect_ratio. WarmServer reads width/height (not aspect_ratio/resolution),
   538	      // so without this every t2v fell back to the 704x448 landscape default.
   539	      if let w = params?.integer("width"), let h = params?.integer("height") {
   540	        body["width"] = w
   541	        body["height"] = h
   542	      } else {
   543	        let portrait = (params?.string("aspect_ratio") ?? "16:9") == "9:16"
   544	        body["width"] = portrait ? 448 : 704
   545	        body["height"] = portrait ? 704 : 448

   506	  /// generate_video -> POST /v1/video/generate/async (submit; poll video_status)
   507	  private func executeGenerateVideo(_ params: MCPParams?) async throws -> MCPToolResult {
   508	    guard let prompt = params?.string("prompt"), !prompt.isEmpty else {
   509	      return MCPToolResult(error: "Error: 'prompt' is required")
   510	    }
   511	
   512	    var body: [String: Any] = ["prompt": prompt]
   513	
   514	    if let imagePath = params?.string("image_path") {
   515	      body["image_path"] = imagePath
   516	    }
   517	    if let audio = params?.bool("audio") {
   518	      body["audio"] = audio
   519	    }
   520	    if let duration = params?.integer("duration") {
   521	      body["duration"] = duration
   522	    }
   523	    if let resolution = params?.string("resolution") {
   524	      body["resolution"] = resolution
   525	    }
   526	    if let aspectRatio = params?.string("aspect_ratio") {
   527	      body["aspect_ratio"] = aspectRatio
   528	    }
   529	    // T2V has no source frame, so translate aspect_ratio into explicit
   530	    // width/height here: WarmServer's video request decodes width/height, NOT
   531	    // aspect_ratio, so otherwise every t2v render fell back to the 704x448
   532	    // landscape default regardless of the requested orientation. Portrait
   533	    // ("9:16") swaps to 448x704 (both /64). I2V leaves dims unset so WarmServer
   534	    // keeps matching the source image aspect.
   535	    if params?.string("image_path") == nil {
   536	      // Respect caller-supplied dims; otherwise orient the standard t2v dims by
   537	      // aspect_ratio. WarmServer reads width/height (not aspect_ratio/resolution),
   538	      // so without this every t2v fell back to the 704x448 landscape default.
   539	      if let w = params?.integer("width"), let h = params?.integer("height") {
   540	        body["width"] = w
   541	        body["height"] = h
   542	      } else {
   543	        let portrait = (params?.string("aspect_ratio") ?? "16:9") == "9:16"
   544	        body["width"] = portrait ? 448 : 704
   545	        body["height"] = portrait ? 704 : 448
   546	      }
   547	    }
   548	    if let seed = params?.integer("seed") {
   549	      body["seed"] = seed
   550	    }
   551	    if let outputPath = params?.string("output_path") {
   552	      body["output_path"] = outputPath
   553	    }
   554	    if let preset = params?.string("preset") {
   555	      body["preset"] = preset
   556	    }
   557	    // Motion vs fidelity per content type (#40): the daemon sends a high
   558	    // img_compression + lower strength for partnered-action prompts (motion)
   559	    // and low compression + strength 1.0 for solo/portrait (fidelity).
   560	    if let strength = params?.number("strength") {
   561	      body["strength"] = strength
   562	    }
   563	    if let comp = params?.integer("img_compression") {
   564	      body["img_compression"] = comp
   565	    }
   566	    if let cfg = params?.number("guidance") {
   567	      body["guidance"] = cfg
   568	    }
   569	    if let tuning = params?.dict("tuning") {
   570	      body["tuning"] = tuning.mapValues { $0.value }
   571	    }
   572	    if let attemptId = params?.string("optimization_attempt_id") {
   573	      body["optimization_attempt_id"] = attemptId
   574	    }
   575	    if let frames = params?.integer("frames") {
   576	      body["frames"] = frames
   577	    }
   578	    if let neg = params?.string("negative_prompt") {
   579	      body["negative_prompt"] = neg
   580	    }
   581	    // Callers that ran their OWN optimizer send enhance:false — a second
   582	    // server-side rewrite drifts the prompt off concrete staging (limb
   583	    // placement, figure count) and double-injects the character description.
   584	    if let enhance = params?.bool("enhance") {
   585	      body["enhance"] = enhance
   586	    }
   587	
   588	    let jsonData = try JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: body)
   589	    // Async route for BOTH backends: local renders return 202 + job_id
   590	    // immediately (poll video_status), instead of blocking the MCP call for
   591	    // the entire multi-minute render — which overran the daemon-side 300s
   592	    // MCP tool timeout on every cold-start render (#219).
   593	    let (status, data) = try await client.post("/v1/video/generate/async", body: jsonData)
   594	    if status == 200 || status == 202 {
   595	      let text = String(data: data, encoding: .utf8) ?? "{}"
   596	      return MCPToolResult(text: text)
   597	    }
   598	    return mapHTTPResponse(status: status, data: data)
   599	  }
   600	
   601	  /// compose_montage -> POST /v1/montage/compose (sync — compositing is cheap).
   602	  /// The params object IS the wire payload (segments/transitions/output/
   603	  /// aspect_policy, already snake_case), so it forwards verbatim.
   604	  private func executeComposeMontage(_ params: MCPParams?) async throws -> MCPToolResult {
   605	    guard let params, params.raw["segments"] != nil else {
   606	      return MCPToolResult(error: "Error: 'segments' is required")
   607	    }
   608	    let jsonData = try JSONEncoder().encode(params.raw)
   609	    let (status, data) = try await client.post("/v1/montage/compose", body: jsonData)
   610	    if status == 200 {
   611	      return MCPToolResult(text: String(data: data, encoding: .utf8) ?? "{}")
   612	    }
   613	    return mapHTTPResponse(status: status, data: data)
   614	  }

   351	function mcpData(result: { data?: unknown }): Record<string, any> | undefined {
   352	    const d = result?.data;
   353	    if (d && typeof d === 'object') return d as Record<string, any>;
   354	    if (typeof d === 'string') {
   355	        try { return JSON.parse(d) as Record<string, any>; } catch { return undefined; }
   356	    }
   357	    return undefined;
   358	}
   359	
   360	/**
   361	 * Rule (Todd 2026-07-14): i2v video aspect MUST match the source image's aspect.
   362	 * Reads the source PNG's real dimensions (zero-dep: PNG IHDR) and returns the
   363	 * matching aspect_ratio bucket. Falls back to `fallback` for non-PNG, remote
   364	 * Mac paths, or any read error — never throws. Renders are always PNG, so this
   365	 * covers the real case (scheduler/media pool) while staying safe elsewhere.
   366	 */
   367	export function readImageAspectRatio(imagePath: string, fallback: string): string {
   368	    try {
   369	        if (!imagePath || imagePath.startsWith('/Users/') || !fs.existsSync(imagePath)) return fallback;
   370	        const fd = fs.openSync(imagePath, 'r');
   371	        try {
   372	            const buf = Buffer.alloc(24);
   373	            const read = fs.readSync(fd, buf, 0, 24, 0);
   374	            if (read < 24) return fallback;
   375	            // PNG signature (0x89 'PNG'...) + IHDR width@16 / height@20 (BE uint32)
   376	            if (buf.readUInt32BE(0) !== 0x89504e47) return fallback;
   377	            const w = buf.readUInt32BE(16);
   378	            const h = buf.readUInt32BE(20);
   379	            if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return fallback;
   380	            if (w >= h * 1.2) return '16:9';
   381	            if (h >= w * 1.2) return '9:16';
   382	            return '1:1';
   383	        } finally {
   384	            fs.closeSync(fd);
   385	        }
   386	    } catch {
   387	        return fallback;
   388	    }
   389	}
   390	
   391	/**
   392	 * Read the SCENE description embedded in a rendered PNG — the image's own
   393	 * generation prompt, stored in the EXIF ImageDescription tag (0x010e) that
   394	 * ComfyBox writes (also visible in Finder as the image Description).
   395	 *
   396	 * Todd 2026-07-14: i2v starts on the real frame but a GENERIC motion prompt
   397	 * makes LTX re-imagine a stock bedroom mid-clip. Feeding the source frame's
   398	 * actual scene back into the motion prompt keeps the video in the real scene.
   399	 *
   400	 * Zero-dep: walks PNG chunks to the `eXIf` chunk, parses the TIFF/IFD, returns
   401	 * the ImageDescription string. Returns undefined for remote/non-PNG/unreadable
   402	 * sources or if the tag is absent — callers fall back to the raw motion prompt.
   403	 */
   404	export function readImageScenePrompt(imagePath: string): string | undefined {
   405	    try {
   406	        if (!imagePath || imagePath.startsWith('/Users/') || !fs.existsSync(imagePath)) return undefined;
   407	        const buf = fs.readFileSync(imagePath);
   408	        if (buf.readUInt32BE(0) !== 0x89504e47) return undefined; // not a PNG
   409	        // Walk chunks to eXIf.
   410	        let i = 8;
   411	        let exif: Buffer | null = null;
   412	        while (i + 12 <= buf.length) {
   413	            const len = buf.readUInt32BE(i);
   414	            const type = buf.toString('latin1', i + 4, i + 8);
   415	            if (type === 'eXIf') { exif = buf.subarray(i + 8, i + 8 + len); break; }
   416	            if (type === 'IEND') break;
   417	            i += 12 + len;
   418	        }
   419	        if (!exif || exif.length < 8) return undefined;
   420	        // TIFF header: 'MM' = big-endian, 'II' = little-endian.
   421	        const be = exif.toString('latin1', 0, 2) === 'MM';
   422	        const u16 = (o: number) => (be ? exif!.readUInt16BE(o) : exif!.readUInt16LE(o));
   423	        const u32 = (o: number) => (be ? exif!.readUInt32BE(o) : exif!.readUInt32LE(o));
   424	        const ifd0 = u32(4);
   425	        if (ifd0 + 2 > exif.length) return undefined;
   426	        const n = u16(ifd0);
   427	        for (let e = 0; e < n; e++) {
   428	            const off = ifd0 + 2 + e * 12;
   429	            if (off + 12 > exif.length) break;
   430	            if (u16(off) === 0x010e) { // ImageDescription, ASCII
   431	                const count = u32(off + 4);
   432	                const voff = count <= 4 ? off + 8 : u32(off + 8);
   433	                if (voff + count > exif.length) return undefined;
   434	                const s = exif.toString('utf8', voff, voff + count).replace(/\0+$/, '').trim();
   435	                return s.length > 0 ? s : undefined;
   436	            }
   437	        }
   438	        return undefined;
   439	    } catch {
   440	        return undefined;
   441	    }
   442	}
