   506	  /// generate_video -> POST /v1/video/generate/async (submit; poll video_status)
   507	  private func executeGenerateVideo(_ params: MCPParams?) async throws -> MCPToolResult {
   508	    guard let prompt = params?.string("prompt"), !prompt.isEmpty else {
   509	      return MCPToolResult(error: "Error: 'prompt' is required")
   510	    }
   511	
   512	    var body: [String: Any] = ["prompt": prompt]
   513	
   514	    if let imagePath = params?.string("image_path") {
   515	      body["image_path"] = imagePath
   516	    }
   517	    if let audio = params?.bool("audio") {
   518	      body["audio"] = audio
   519	    }
   520	    if let duration = params?.integer("duration") {
   521	      body["duration"] = duration
   522	    }
   523	    if let resolution = params?.string("resolution") {
   524	      body["resolution"] = resolution
   525	    }
   526	    if let aspectRatio = params?.string("aspect_ratio") {
   527	      body["aspect_ratio"] = aspectRatio
   528	    }
   529	    // T2V has no source frame, so translate aspect_ratio into explicit
   530	    // width/height here: WarmServer's video request decodes width/height, NOT
   531	    // aspect_ratio, so otherwise every t2v render fell back to the 704x448
   532	    // landscape default regardless of the requested orientation. Portrait
   533	    // ("9:16") swaps to 448x704 (both /64). I2V leaves dims unset so WarmServer
   534	    // keeps matching the source image aspect.
   535	    if params?.string("image_path") == nil {
   536	      // Respect caller-supplied dims; otherwise orient the standard t2v dims by
   537	      // aspect_ratio. WarmServer reads width/height (not aspect_ratio/resolution),
   538	      // so without this every t2v fell back to the 704x448 landscape default.
   539	      if let w = params?.integer("width"), let h = params?.integer("height") {
   540	        body["width"] = w
   541	        body["height"] = h
   542	      } else {
   543	        let portrait = (params?.string("aspect_ratio") ?? "16:9") == "9:16"
   544	        body["width"] = portrait ? 448 : 704
   545	        body["height"] = portrait ? 704 : 448
   546	      }
   547	    }
   548	    if let seed = params?.integer("seed") {
   549	      body["seed"] = seed
   550	    }
   551	    if let outputPath = params?.string("output_path") {
   552	      body["output_path"] = outputPath
   553	    }
   554	    if let preset = params?.string("preset") {
   555	      body["preset"] = preset
   556	    }
   557	    // Motion vs fidelity per content type (#40): the daemon sends a high
   558	    // img_compression + lower strength for partnered-action prompts (motion)
   559	    // and low compression + strength 1.0 for solo/portrait (fidelity).
   560	    if let strength = params?.number("strength") {
   561	      body["strength"] = strength
   562	    }
   563	    if let comp = params?.integer("img_compression") {
   564	      body["img_compression"] = comp
   565	    }
   566	    if let cfg = params?.number("guidance") {
   567	      body["guidance"] = cfg
   568	    }
   569	    if let tuning = params?.dict("tuning") {
   570	      body["tuning"] = tuning.mapValues { $0.value }
   571	    }
   572	    if let attemptId = params?.string("optimization_attempt_id") {
   573	      body["optimization_attempt_id"] = attemptId
   574	    }
   575	    if let frames = params?.integer("frames") {
   576	      body["frames"] = frames
   577	    }
   578	    if let neg = params?.string("negative_prompt") {
   579	      body["negative_prompt"] = neg
   580	    }
   581	    // Callers that ran their OWN optimizer send enhance:false — a second
   582	    // server-side rewrite drifts the prompt off concrete staging (limb
   583	    // placement, figure count) and double-injects the character description.
   584	    if let enhance = params?.bool("enhance") {
   585	      body["enhance"] = enhance
   586	    }
   587	
   588	    let jsonData = try JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: body)
   589	    // Async route for BOTH backends: local renders return 202 + job_id
   590	    // immediately (poll video_status), instead of blocking the MCP call for
   591	    // the entire multi-minute render — which overran the daemon-side 300s
   592	    // MCP tool timeout on every cold-start render (#219).
   593	    let (status, data) = try await client.post("/v1/video/generate/async", body: jsonData)
   594	    if status == 200 || status == 202 {
   595	      let text = String(data: data, encoding: .utf8) ?? "{}"
   596	      return MCPToolResult(text: text)
   597	    }
   598	    return mapHTTPResponse(status: status, data: data)
   599	  }
   600	
   601	  /// compose_montage -> POST /v1/montage/compose (sync — compositing is cheap).
   602	  /// The params object IS the wire payload (segments/transitions/output/
   603	  /// aspect_policy, already snake_case), so it forwards verbatim.
   604	  private func executeComposeMontage(_ params: MCPParams?) async throws -> MCPToolResult {
   605	    guard let params, params.raw["segments"] != nil else {
   606	      return MCPToolResult(error: "Error: 'segments' is required")
   607	    }
   608	    let jsonData = try JSONEncoder().encode(params.raw)
   609	    let (status, data) = try await client.post("/v1/montage/compose", body: jsonData)
   610	    if status == 200 {
    15	import Foundation
    16	import MLX
    17	import MLXFast
    18	import MLXNN
    19	
    20	// MARK: - Text Encoder Output
    21	
    22	/// Output from the LTX-2 text encoder.
    23	public struct LTX2TextEncoderOutput {
    24	  /// Video embeddings for cross-attention `[B, S, videoDim]`.
    25	  public let videoEmbeddings: MLXArray
    26	
    27	  /// Audio embeddings for cross-attention `[B, S, audioDim]`.
    28	  public let audioEmbeddings: MLXArray
    29	
    30	  /// Attention mask `[B, S]` (1 = valid, 0 = pad).
    31	  public let attentionMask: MLXArray
    32	}
    33	
    34	// MARK: - LTX-2 Text Encoder
    35	
    36	/// Top-level LTX-2 text encoder.
    37	///
    38	/// Orchestrates the complete text encoding pipeline:
    39	/// 1. Tokenize input prompt
    40	/// 2. Run Gemma 3 forward pass extracting all 49 hidden states
    41	/// 3. Feature extraction (V1 or V2 normalization + projection)
    42	/// 4. 1D connector (transformer blocks + register tokens + RoPE)
    43	/// 5. Output dual video/audio embeddings
    44	///
    45	/// The text encoder is a heavyweight module (~24 GB at fp16, ~6 GB at Q4).
    85	    self._audioConnector.wrappedValue = LTX2Connector1D(config: config.audioConnector)
    86	  }
    87	
    88	  /// Encode a tokenized input to video and audio embeddings.
    89	  ///
    90	  /// This is the main encoding function that takes pre-tokenized input
    91	  /// (input IDs and attention mask) and runs the full pipeline.
    92	  ///
    93	  /// - Parameters:
    94	  ///   - inputIds: Token IDs `[B, S]`.
    95	  ///   - attentionMask: Padding mask `[B, S]` (1 = valid, 0 = pad).
    96	  ///   - returnAudioEmbeddings: If true, computes audio embeddings too.
    97	  /// - Returns: `LTX2TextEncoderOutput` with video and audio embeddings.
    98	  public func encode(
    99	    inputIds: MLXArray,
   100	    attentionMask: MLXArray,
   101	    returnAudioEmbeddings: Bool = true
   102	  ) -> LTX2TextEncoderOutput {
   103	    // Step 1: Gemma 3 forward pass — extract ALL hidden states
   104	    let allHiddenStates = languageModel(
   105	      inputIds: inputIds,
   106	      attentionMask: attentionMask,
   107	      outputHiddenStates: true
   108	    )
   109	
   110	    let videoEmbeddings: MLXArray
   111	    let audioEmbeddings: MLXArray
   112	
   113	    if config.hasPromptAdaLN {
   114	      // LTX-2.3: V2 feature extraction with separate video/audio paths
   115	      guard let extractor = featureExtractorV2 else {
   116	        fatalError("V2 feature extractor not initialized for prompt adaln mode")
   117	      }
   118	
   119	      // Video features
   120	      let videoFeatures = extractor.videoFeatures(
   121	        hiddenStates: allHiddenStates,
   122	        attentionMask: attentionMask
   123	      )
   124	
   125	      // Build additive mask for connector
   126	      let additiveMask = buildAdditiveMask(
   127	        attentionMask: attentionMask, dtype: videoFeatures.dtype)
   128	
   129	      let (videoOut, _) = videoConnector(
   130	        hiddenStates: videoFeatures, attentionMask: additiveMask)
   131	      videoEmbeddings = videoOut
   132	
   133	      if returnAudioEmbeddings {
   134	        let audioFeatures = extractor.audioFeatures(
   135	          hiddenStates: allHiddenStates,
   136	          attentionMask: attentionMask
   137	        )
   138	        let audioMask = buildAdditiveMask(
   139	          attentionMask: attentionMask, dtype: audioFeatures.dtype)
   140	        let (audioOut, _) = audioConnector(
   141	          hiddenStates: audioFeatures, attentionMask: audioMask)
   142	        audioEmbeddings = audioOut
   143	      } else {
   144	        audioEmbeddings = MLX.zeros([inputIds.dim(0), inputIds.dim(1), config.audioDim])
   145	      }
   146	
   147	    } else {
   148	      // LTX-2 original: V1 feature extraction with shared features
   149	      guard let extractor = featureExtractorV1 else {
   150	        fatalError("V1 feature extractor not initialized for non-prompt-adaln mode")
   151	      }
   152	
   153	      let features = extractor(
   154	        hiddenStates: allHiddenStates,
   155	        attentionMask: attentionMask
   300	      self.learnableRegisters = MLX.zeros([0, config.dim])
   301	    }
   302	  }
   303	
   304	  /// Forward pass through the 1D connector.
   305	  ///
   306	  /// - Parameters:
   307	  ///   - hiddenStates: Text features `[B, S, dim]`.
   308	  ///   - attentionMask: Optional additive mask `[B, 1, 1, S]` (0 = attend, -1e9 = pad).
   309	  /// - Returns: `(processedHiddenStates, processedMask)` with same shapes.
   310	  public func callAsFunction(
   311	    hiddenStates: MLXArray,
   312	    attentionMask: MLXArray? = nil
   313	  ) -> (MLXArray, MLXArray?) {
   314	    var h = hiddenStates
   315	    var mask = attentionMask
   316	
   317	    // Replace padded tokens with learnable registers
   318	    if config.numLearnableRegisters > 0, let attMask = mask {
   319	      (h, mask) = replacePaddedWithRegisters(hiddenStates: h, attentionMask: attMask)
   320	    }
   321	
   322	    // Compute SPLIT RoPE frequencies
   323	    let seqLen = h.dim(1)
   324	    let pe = precomputeFreqsCIS(seqLen: seqLen, dtype: h.dtype)
   325	
   326	    // Process through transformer blocks
   327	    for block in blocks {
   328	      h = block(h, pe: pe)
   329	    }
   330	
   331	    // Final weight-free RMSNorm
   332	    let dim = h.dim(-1)
   333	    let ones = MLXArray.ones([dim])
   334	    h = MLXFast.rmsNorm(h, weight: ones, eps: 1e-6)
   335	
   336	    return (h, mask)
   337	  }
   338	
   339	  // MARK: - RoPE Frequency Computation
   340	
   410	
   411	    // Transpose: (S, H, D/2) -> (H, S, D/2) -> (1, H, S, D/2)
   412	    let cosFinal = cosArr.transposed(1, 0, 2).expandedDimensions(axis: 0).asType(dtype)
   413	    let sinFinal = sinArr.transposed(1, 0, 2).expandedDimensions(axis: 0).asType(dtype)
   414	
   415	    return (cosFinal, sinFinal)
   416	  }
   417	
   418	  // MARK: - Register Replacement
   419	
   420	  /// Replace padded positions with learnable register tokens.
   421	  ///
   422	  /// For left-padded input, valid tokens are at the end. This function:
   423	  /// 1. Moves valid tokens to the front
   424	  /// 2. Fills remaining positions with tiled register tokens
   425	  /// 3. Resets the attention mask to all-zeros (no masking needed)
   426	  ///
   427	  /// Matches Python `_replace_padded_with_registers`.
   428	  private func replacePaddedWithRegisters(
   429	    hiddenStates: MLXArray,
   430	    attentionMask: MLXArray
   431	  ) -> (MLXArray, MLXArray?) {
   432	    let batchSize = hiddenStates.dim(0)
   433	    let seqLen = hiddenStates.dim(1)
   434	    let dim = hiddenStates.dim(2)
   435	    let dtype = hiddenStates.dtype
   436	
   437	    // Binary mask: 1 for valid, 0 for padded
   438	    // attentionMask is additive: 0 for valid, large negative for padded
   439	    let maskSquashed = attentionMask.squeezed(axis: 1).squeezed(axis: 1)  // (B, S)
   440	    let maskBinary = (maskSquashed .>= MLXArray(Float(-9000.0))).asType(.int32)
   441	
   442	    // Tile registers to fill sequence length
   443	    let numTiles = seqLen / config.numLearnableRegisters
   444	    let registers = MLX.tiled(learnableRegisters, repetitions: [numTiles, 1])
   445	      .asType(dtype)  // (seqLen, dim)
   446	
   447	    // Process each batch item
   448	    var resultList: [MLXArray] = []
   449	    for b in 0..<batchSize {
   450	      let maskB = maskBinary[b]  // (S,)
   451	      let hsB = hiddenStates[b]  // (S, dim)
   452	
   453	      let numValid = Int(MLX.sum(maskB).item(Int.self))
   454	      let padLength = seqLen - numValid
   455	
   456	      // Valid tokens are at the end (left-padded)
   457	      let validTokens = hsB[(seqLen - numValid)...]  // (numValid, dim)
   458	
   459	      // Build: valid tokens at front, registers at back
   460	      let adjusted: MLXArray
   461	      if padLength > 0 {
   462	        let padding = MLX.zeros([padLength, dim], dtype: dtype)
   463	        adjusted = MLX.concatenated([validTokens, padding], axis: 0)
   464	      } else {
   465	        adjusted = validTokens
   466	      }
   467	
   468	      // Flipped mask: 1s at front (valid), 0s at back (register positions)
   469	      let flippedMask: MLXArray
   470	      if padLength > 0 {
   471	        flippedMask = MLX.concatenated([
   472	          MLX.ones([numValid], dtype: .int32),
   473	          MLX.zeros([padLength], dtype: .int32),
   474	        ], axis: 0)
   475	      } else {
   476	        flippedMask = MLX.ones([seqLen], dtype: .int32)
   477	      }
   478	
   479	      // Combine: valid tokens where mask=1, registers where mask=0
   480	      let flippedMaskExpanded = flippedMask.expandedDimensions(axis: 1).asType(dtype)
   481	      let combined = flippedMaskExpanded * adjusted + (1 - flippedMaskExpanded) * registers
   482	      resultList.append(combined)
   483	    }
   484	
   485	    let result = MLX.stacked(resultList, axis: 0)
   486	
   487	    // Reset attention mask to all zeros (no masking after register replacement)
   488	    let newMask = MLX.zeros(like: attentionMask)
   489	
   490	    return (result, newMask)
   491	  }
   492	}

   737	      logger.info("Audio stream enabled (i2v): \(ta) latent frames (\(seconds)s, negatives \(negativeAudioEmbeddings != nil ? "on" : "off")).")
   738	    }
   739	
   740	    // Step 6: Denoising loop with I2V state
   741	    logger.info("Denoising with I2V conditioning...")
   742	    let latentsAll = denoisingLoop(
   743	      latents: state.latent,
   744	      positions: positions,
   745	      precomputedPE: precomputedPE,
   746	      textEmbeddings: textOutput.videoEmbeddings,
   747	      negativeEmbeddings: negativeEmbeddings,
   748	      sigmas: sigmas,
   749	      cfgScale: cfgScale,
   750	      state: state,
  1100	      // Positive pass through transformer.
  1101	      // Pass PER-TOKEN timesteps so the model sees conditioned frames (mask=0)
  1102	      // at timestep 0 (clean) and generated frames at `sigma`. Without this the
  1103	      // transformer treats every token as equally noisy and cannot tell which
  1104	      // frame is the I2V conditioning frame — it discounts the image and
  1105	      // produces T2V-like output. Matches reference guided_denoise_loop which
  1106	      // passes `video_timesteps = denoise_mask * sigma` when the mask is
  1107	      // non-uniform. For T2V, `timesteps` is a scalar (1,1) → broadcast, so this
  1108	      // is a no-op there.
  1109	      // NAG rides ONLY the positive pass: it steers the conditional prediction
  1110	      // away from the negative concept inside cross-attention. Applying it to
  1111	      // the unconditional pass too would guide the baseline that CFG++ steps
  1112	      // along, double-counting the guidance.
  1113	      let velocityPos: MLXArray
  1114	      var avVelocityPos: MLXArray? = nil
  1115	      var avVelocityNeg: MLXArray? = nil
  1116	      if let av = avState {
  1117	        // Joint A/V pass (task #21): both velocities in one forward. NAG is
  1118	        // not available on this path yet (spec open item) — the joint model's
  1119	        // a2v cross-modal coupling replaces it as the video conditioning
  1120	        // difference. Audio updates HERE (positive pass only, plain Euler,
  1121	        // deterministic): CFG/STG passes below remain video-only and steer
  1122	        // only the video x0.
  1123	        let (vv, va) = transformer.callAV(
  1124	          latent: latentsFlat,
  1125	          audioLatents: av.audioLatents.asType(dtype),
  1126	          timestep: timesteps,
  1127	          videoSigmaMax: sigma,
  1128	          audioSigma: sigma,
  1129	          context: textEmbeddings.asType(dtype),
  1130	          audioContext: av.audioContext.asType(dtype),
  1131	          sigma: sigmaArray,
  1132	          pe: av.pe)
  1133	        velocityPos = vv
  1134	        avVelocityPos = va
  1135	      } else {
  1136	        velocityPos = transformer(
  1137	          latent: latentsFlat,
  1138	          timestep: timesteps,
  1139	          context: textEmbeddings.asType(dtype),
  1140	          positions: positions,
  1141	          sigma: sigmaArray,
  1142	          precomputedPE: precomputedPE,
  1143	          nagContext: nagEmbeddings?.asType(dtype),
  1144	          nag: nag
  1145	        )
  1146	      }
  1147	
  1148	      // Compute x0 (denoised) from velocity using per-token timesteps
  1149	      let latentsFlatF32 = currentLatents
  1150	        .reshaped(b, c, -1)
  1151	        .transposed(0, 2, 1)
  1152	      let timestepsF32 = timesteps.asType(.float32).expandedDimensions(axis: -1)
  1153	      var x0GuidedF32 = latentsFlatF32 - timestepsF32 * velocityPos.asType(.float32)
  1154	      let x0CondF32 = x0GuidedF32  // pure conditional x0, saved for the STG delta
  1155	
  1156	      // Negative pass: needed for classic CFG (scale>1) and for CFG++ (every
  1157	      // step, even at cfg=1 — the CFG++ update steps along the UNCONDITIONAL
  1158	      // noise direction; see ComfyUI sample_euler_ancestral_cfg_pp which sets
  1159	      // disable_cfg1_optimization=True for exactly this reason).
  1160	      var x0UncondF32: MLXArray? = nil
  1161	      if useCFG || useCfgPP, let negEmb = negativeEmbeddings {
  1162	        let velocityNeg: MLXArray
  1163	        if let av = avState, let negACtx = av.negativeAudioContext {
  1164	          // Audio joins the negative pass (task #26): the reference recipe's
  1165	          // negative ("...distorted sound, saturated sound, loud") flows
  1166	          // through BOTH streams; ours never steered audio until now. The
  1167	          // dual negative also makes the video-side negative prediction
  1168	          // reference-faithful (it carries a2v cross-modal contributions).
  1169	          let (vv, va) = transformer.callAV(
  1170	            latent: latentsFlat,
  1171	            audioLatents: av.audioLatents.asType(dtype),
  1172	            timestep: timesteps,
  1173	            videoSigmaMax: sigma,
  1174	            audioSigma: sigma,
  1175	            context: negEmb.asType(dtype),
  1176	            audioContext: negACtx.asType(dtype),
  1177	            sigma: sigmaArray,
  1178	            pe: av.pe)
  1179	          velocityNeg = vv
  1180	          avVelocityNeg = va
  1181	        } else {
  1182	          velocityNeg = transformer(
  1183	            latent: latentsFlat,
  1184	            timestep: timesteps,
  1185	            context: negEmb.asType(dtype),
  1186	            positions: positions,
  1187	            sigma: sigmaArray,
  1188	            precomputedPE: precomputedPE
  1189	          )
  1190	        }
  1295	        } else if useSDE {
  1296	          let sigmaF32 = MLXArray(sigma)
  1297	          let eps = (currentLatents - denoised) / sigmaF32
  1298	          let (alphaRatio, sigmaDown, sigmaUp) = getSdeCoeff(sigmaNext: sigmaNext)
  1299	          let noise = MLXRandom.normal(currentLatents.shape, dtype: .float32)
  1300	          currentLatents = MLXArray(alphaRatio) * (denoised + MLXArray(sigmaDown) * eps)
  1301	            + MLXArray(sigmaUp) * noise
  1302	        } else {
  1303	          let sigmaF32 = MLXArray(sigma)
  1304	          let sigmaNextF32 = MLXArray(sigmaNext)
  1305	          currentLatents = denoised + sigmaNextF32 * (currentLatents - denoised) / sigmaF32
  1306	        }
  1307	      } else {
  1308	        currentLatents = denoised
  1309	      }
  1310	      // Audio step (task #26): guided when the negative pass ran dual-stream,
  1311	      // plain Euler otherwise. Ancestral noise comes from the state's keyed
  1312	      // chain so the global RNG (video noise sequence) is untouched.
  1313	      if let av = avState, let velA = avVelocityPos {
  1314	        var aNoise: MLXArray? = nil
  1315	        if useSDE, avVelocityNeg != nil, let key = av.audioNoiseKey {

  1317	          av.audioNoiseKey = keys.0
  1318	          aNoise = MLXRandom.normal(av.audioLatents.shape, key: keys.1).asType(.float32)
  1319	        }

  1321	        // video cfg 3.5 over-guided the audio stream — dragged, slow-motion
  1322	        // sound on otherwise-correct 5s clips; the author's recipes never
  1323	        // run audio above cfg 1. Negatives still steer via CFG++ stepping.
  1324	        // LTX2_AUDIO_CFG overrides (default 1.0).
  1325	        let audioCfg = Float(ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_AUDIO_CFG"] ?? "") ?? 1.0
  1326	        av.audioLatents = ltx2AudioStep(
  1327	          audio: av.audioLatents, velocityPos: velA, velocityNeg: avVelocityNeg,
  1328	          sigma: sigma, sigmaNext: sigmaNext, cfgScale: min(cfgAt(i), audioCfg),
  1329	          useCfgPP: useCfgPP, useSDE: useSDE && !forceDeterministic,
  1330	          ancestralNoise: aNoise)
  1331	        eval(av.audioLatents)
  1332	      }

  1334	      // mask pins at timestep 0 (I2V conditioning / refine re-inject); re-snap
  1335	      // them to the clean latent after every step (ComfyUI does the equivalent
  1336	      // in its outer inpaint wrapper).
  1337	      if useCfgPP, let s = state {
  1338	        currentLatents = LTX2Conditioning.applyDenoiseMask(
  1339	          denoised: currentLatents,
  1340	          clean: s.cleanLatent.asType(.float32),

   135	    let condVar = condF32.variance()
   136	    let guidedVar = guidedF32.variance()
   137	    let condStd = MLX.sqrt(condVar)
   138	    let guidedStd = MLX.sqrt(guidedVar) + 1e-8
   139	    let vFactor = MLXArray(rescale) * (condStd / guidedStd) + MLXArray(1.0 - rescale)
   140	    return guided * vFactor
   141	  }
   142	}
   143	
   144	/// Audio-stream sampler step (task #26 — audio joins the negative pass).
   145	///
   146	/// Mirrors the video Euler/CFG++/ancestral update in `denoisingLoop` for the
   147	/// audio latents `(B, 8, T, F)`, in flow-matching CONST-model terms:
   148	///   x0_cond = ax − σ·v_pos;  x0_uncond = ax − σ·v_neg
   149	///   CFG (scale>1): x0_guided = applyCFG(x0_cond, x0_uncond, scale)
   150	///   CFG++: d = (ax − alphaS·x0_uncond)/σ; ax' = alphaT·x0_guided + σ_down·d
   151	///          (+ alphaT·σ_up·noise, ancestral)
   152	///   plain: ax' = x0 + σ_next·(ax − x0)/σ
   153	/// With no negative velocity the step degrades to plain Euler on v_pos —
   154	/// byte-identical to the pre-negatives behavior.
   155	public func ltx2AudioStep(
   156	  audio ax: MLXArray,
   157	  velocityPos: MLXArray,
   158	  velocityNeg: MLXArray?,
   159	  sigma: Float,
   160	  sigmaNext: Float,
   161	  cfgScale: Float,
   162	  useCfgPP: Bool,
   163	  useSDE: Bool,
   164	  ancestralNoise: MLXArray?
   165	) -> MLXArray {
   166	  let sig = MLXArray(sigma)
   167	  let x0Cond = ax - sig * velocityPos.asType(.float32)
   168	  guard sigmaNext > 0 else {
   169	    if let vn = velocityNeg, cfgScale > 1.0 {
   170	      let x0Neg = ax - sig * vn.asType(.float32)
   171	      return LTX2Guidance.applyCFG(conditioned: x0Cond, unconditioned: x0Neg, scale: cfgScale)
   172	    }
   173	    return x0Cond
   174	  }
   175	
   176	  guard let vn = velocityNeg else {
   177	    // No negative context: plain Euler on the positive velocity (v1 behavior).
   178	    return ax + MLXArray(sigmaNext - sigma) * velocityPos.asType(.float32)
   179	  }
   180	  let x0Neg = ax - sig * vn.asType(.float32)
   181	  var x0Guided = x0Cond
   182	  if cfgScale > 1.0 {
   183	    x0Guided = LTX2Guidance.applyCFG(conditioned: x0Cond, unconditioned: x0Neg, scale: cfgScale)
   184	  }
   185	
   186	  if useCfgPP {
   187	    let alphaS = max(1.0 - sigma, Float(1e-4))
   188	    let alphaT = 1.0 - sigmaNext
   189	    let d = (ax - MLXArray(alphaS) * x0Neg) / sig
   190	    var sigmaDown = sigmaNext
   191	    var sigmaUp: Float = 0
   192	    if useSDE {
   193	      let sf = sigma / alphaS
   194	      let st = sigmaNext / alphaT
   195	      let inner = st * st * (sf * sf - st * st) / (sf * sf)
   196	      let up = min(st, (inner > 0 ? inner : 0).squareRoot())
   197	      sigmaDown = alphaT * (max(st * st - up * up, 0)).squareRoot()
   198	      sigmaUp = up
   199	    }
   200	    var next = MLXArray(alphaT) * x0Guided + MLXArray(sigmaDown) * d
   201	    if useSDE, sigmaUp > 0, let noise = ancestralNoise {
   202	      next = next + MLXArray(alphaT) * noise * MLXArray(sigmaUp)
   203	    }
   204	    return next
   205	  }
   206	  // Classic CFG without cfg_pp: Euler toward the guided x0.
   207	  return x0Guided + MLXArray(sigmaNext) * (ax - x0Guided) / sig
   208	}
    85	
    86	  /// Extract video features from hidden states.
    87	  ///
    88	  /// - Parameters:
    89	  ///   - hiddenStates: List of hidden state arrays, each `[B, T, D]`.
    90	  ///   - attentionMask: Binary attention mask `[B, T]` (1 = valid, 0 = pad).
    91	  /// - Returns: Projected video features `[B, T, videoOutputDim]`.

    99	    )

   101	    let rescaled = LTX2FeatureExtractor.rescaleNorm(
   102	      normed, targetDim: targetDim, sourceDim: embeddingDim)

   104	  }

   124	  }

   127	// MARK: - Static Normalization Functions
   128	
   129	/// Static helper functions for feature extraction normalization.
   130	public enum LTX2FeatureExtractor {
   205	
   206	  /// V2 normalization: Per-token RMSNorm across hidden dimension.
   207	  ///
   208	  /// Matches Python `norm_and_concat_per_token_rms` function.
   209	  ///
   210	  /// - Parameters:
   211	  ///   - hiddenStates: List of hidden state arrays from Gemma layers.
   212	  ///   - attentionMask: Binary attention mask `[B, T]`.
   213	  /// - Returns: Normalized and concatenated tensor `[B, T, D*L]`.
   214	  public static func normAndConcatPerTokenRMS(
   215	    hiddenStates: [MLXArray],
   216	    attentionMask: MLXArray
   217	  ) -> MLXArray {
   218	    // Stack: (B, T, D, L)
   219	    let encoded = MLX.stacked(hiddenStates, axis: -1)
   220	    let dtype = encoded.dtype
   221	    let b = encoded.dim(0)
   222	    let t = encoded.dim(1)
   223	    let d = encoded.dim(2)
   224	    let numLayers = encoded.dim(3)
   225	
   226	    // Per-token RMSNorm across hidden dimension: variance = mean(x^2) over dim D
   227	    let encodedF32 = encoded.asType(.float32)
   228	    let variance = MLX.mean(encodedF32 * encodedF32, axis: 2, keepDims: true)  // (B, T, 1, L)
   229	    let normed = (encodedF32 * MLX.rsqrt(variance + MLXArray(Float(1e-6)))).asType(dtype)
   230	
   231	    // Flatten layers: (B, T, D*L)
   232	    var result = normed.reshaped(b, t, d * numLayers)
   233	
   234	    // Zero out padded positions
   235	    let mask3D = attentionMask.expandedDimensions(axis: 2)  // (B, T, 1)
   236	    let maskBool = mask3D .> MLXArray(Float(0))
   237	    result = MLX.where(
   238	      MLX.broadcast(maskBool, to: result.shape),
   239	      result,
   240	      MLX.zeros(like: result)
   241	    )
   242	
   243	    return result
   244	  }
   245	
   246	  /// Rescale normalization: x * sqrt(target_dim / source_dim).
   247	  ///
   248	  /// Used in V2 feature extraction to compensate for dimension changes.
   249	  public static func rescaleNorm(
   250	    _ x: MLXArray, targetDim: Int, sourceDim: Int
    35	  }
    36	}

   219	  ) -> (video: MLXArray, audio: MLXArray) {
   220	    precondition(hasAudio, "callDualStream requires hasAudio")
   221	    let b = video.dim(0)
   222	
   223	    // ---- Video stream: self-attn, text cross-attn (reuse video sub-logic) ----
   224	    var v = video
   225	    let vMSA = getAdaValues(table: scaleShiftTable, batchSize: b, timestep: timestep, range: 0..<3)
   226	    let vNorm = weightFreeRMSNorm(v) * (1 + vMSA[1]) + vMSA[0]
   227	    v = v + attn1(vNorm, pe: pe) * vMSA[2]
   228	
   229	    let vCross = getAdaValues(table: scaleShiftTable, batchSize: b, timestep: timestep, range: 6..<9)
   230	    var vCtx = context
   231	    if let pTS = promptTimestep, let pTable = promptScaleShiftTable {
   232	      let pp = getAdaValues(table: pTable, batchSize: b, timestep: pTS, range: 0..<2)
   233	      vCtx = context * (1 + pp[1]) + pp[0]
   234	    }
   235	    v = v + attn2(weightFreeRMSNorm(v) * (1 + vCross[1]) + vCross[0],
   236	                  context: vCtx, mask: contextMask) * vCross[2]
   237	
   238	    // ---- Audio stream: self-attn, text cross-attn ----
   239	    var a = audio
   240	    let aMSA = getAdaValues(table: audioScaleShiftTable!, batchSize: b, timestep: audioTimestep, range: 0..<3)
   241	    a = a + audioAttn1!(audioRMSNorm(a) * (1 + aMSA[1]) + aMSA[0], pe: audioPE) * aMSA[2]
   242	
   243	    let aCross = getAdaValues(table: audioScaleShiftTable!, batchSize: b, timestep: audioTimestep, range: 6..<9)
   244	    var aCtx = audioContext
   245	    if let pTS = audioPromptTimestep, let pTable = audioPromptScaleShiftTable {
   246	      let pp = getAdaValues(table: pTable, batchSize: b, timestep: pTS, range: 0..<2)
   247	      aCtx = audioContext * (1 + pp[1]) + pp[0]
   248	    }
   249	    a = a + audioAttn2!(audioRMSNorm(a) * (1 + aCross[1]) + aCross[0],
   250	                        context: aCtx, mask: audioContextMask) * aCross[2]
   251	
   252	    // ---- Cross-modal: A2V (update video from audio) + V2A (update audio) ----
   253	    // Norms taken once, BEFORE either update (reference vx_norm3/ax_norm3).
   254	    let vxNorm3 = weightFreeRMSNorm(v)
   255	    let axNorm3 = audioRMSNorm(a)
   256	    let (vCA, vGateCA) = avCrossAdaValues(
   257	      table: scaleShiftTableA2VCaVideo!, batchSize: b,
   258	      scaleShiftTimestep: crossScaleShiftTimestep, gateTimestep: crossGateTimestep)
   259	    let (aCA, aGateCA) = avCrossAdaValues(

   262	
   263	    // a2v: Q = modulated video, KV = modulated audio (scale row 0, shift row 1).
   264	    let vxScaledA2V = vxNorm3 * (1 + vCA[0]) + vCA[1]
   265	    let axScaledA2V = axNorm3 * (1 + aCA[0]) + aCA[1]

   267	                              pe: crossPE, kPE: audioCrossPE) * vGateCA
   268

   270	    let axScaledV2A = axNorm3 * (1 + aCA[2]) + aCA[3]
   271	    let vxScaledV2A = vxNorm3 * (1 + vCA[2]) + vCA[3]

   273	                              pe: audioCrossPE, kPE: crossPE) * aGateCA
   274

   277	    v = v + ff(weightFreeRMSNorm(v) * (1 + vMLP[1]) + vMLP[0]) * vMLP[2]

   139	  /// Joint dual-stream forward: video + audio velocities in one pass.
   140	  ///
   141	  /// Mirrors LTXAVModel.forward: per-modality timesteps, the av_ca cross
   142	  /// conditioning from `prepareAVConditioning`, all 48 blocks via
   143	  /// `callDualStream`, then both output projections.
   144	  ///
   145	  /// v1 scope: audio timestep is a SCALAR sigma (t2v audio; per-token audio
   146	  /// timesteps arrive with i2v mask integration); no NAG/STG on this path
   147	  /// yet. `videoSigmaMax` drives the av_ca video scale-shift and the v2a
   148	  /// gate — pass the max video sigma (== sigma for uniform timesteps).
   149	  public func callAV(
   150	    latent: MLXArray,
   151	    audioLatents: MLXArray,
   152	    timestep: MLXArray,
   153	    videoSigmaMax: Float,
   154	    audioSigma: Float,
   155	    context: MLXArray,
   156	    audioContext: MLXArray,
   157	    contextMask: MLXArray? = nil,
   158	    sigma: MLXArray? = nil,
   159	    pe: (videoPE: (cos: MLXArray, sin: MLXArray),
   160	         audioPE: (cos: MLXArray, sin: MLXArray),
   161	         crossVideoPE: (cos: MLXArray, sin: MLXArray),
   162	         crossAudioPE: (cos: MLXArray, sin: MLXArray))
   163	  ) -> (video: MLXArray, audio: MLXArray) {
   164	    precondition(hasAudio, "callAV requires hasAudio")
   165	    let batchSize = latent.dim(0)
   166	
   167	    // ---- Video side: identical prep to the video-only forward ----
   168	    var vx = patchifyProj(latent)
   169	    let scaledTimestep = timestep * timestepScaleMultiplier
   170	    let (timestepEmb, embeddedTimestep) = adaLNSingle(
   171	      scaledTimestep.reshaped(-1), hiddenDtype: vx.dtype)
   172	    let vTsEmb = timestepEmb.reshaped(batchSize, -1, timestepEmb.dim(-1))
   173	    let vEmbTS = embeddedTimestep.reshaped(batchSize, -1, embeddedTimestep.dim(-1))
   174	
   175	    var ctx = context
   176	    if let captionProj = captionProjection { ctx = captionProj(ctx) }
   177	    ctx = ctx.reshaped(batchSize, -1, vx.dim(-1))
   178	
   179	    var attnMask = contextMask
   180	    if let mask = attnMask {
   181	      if mask.dtype != .float32 && mask.dtype != .float16 && mask.dtype != .bfloat16 {
   182	        let floatMask = (mask.asType(latent.dtype) - 1) * 1e9
   183	        attnMask = floatMask.reshaped(mask.dim(0), 1, -1, mask.dim(-1))
   184	      }
   185	    }
   186	
   187	    var promptTS: MLXArray? = nil
   188	    if let promptAda = promptAdaLNSingle, let s = sigma {
   189	      let scaledSigma = s * timestepScaleMultiplier
   190	      let (pTS, _) = promptAda(scaledSigma.reshaped(-1), hiddenDtype: vx.dtype)
   191	      promptTS = pTS.reshaped(batchSize, -1, pTS.dim(-1))
   192	    }
   193	
   194	    // ---- Audio side ----
   195	    var (ax, _) = projectAudioTokens(audioLatents)
   196	    ax = ax.asType(vx.dtype)
   197	    let av = prepareAVConditioning(
   198	      videoTimesteps: timestep, videoSigmaMax: videoSigmaMax,
   199	      audioSigma: audioSigma, batchSize: batchSize,
   200	      hiddenDtype: vx.dtype)
   201	    let aCtx = audioContext.reshaped(batchSize, -1, audioInnerDim).asType(vx.dtype)
   202	
   203	    // ---- Joint block loop ----
   204	    for block in transformerBlocks {
   205	      (vx, ax) = block.callDualStream(
   206	        video: vx, audio: ax,
   207	        context: ctx, audioContext: aCtx,
   208	        contextMask: attnMask, audioContextMask: attnMask,
   209	        timestep: vTsEmb, audioTimestep: av.audioTimestep,
   210	        pe: pe.videoPE, audioPE: pe.audioPE,
   211	        crossPE: pe.crossVideoPE, audioCrossPE: pe.crossAudioPE,
   212	        crossScaleShiftTimestep: av.crossScaleShiftTimestep,
   213	        audioCrossScaleShiftTimestep: av.audioCrossScaleShiftTimestep,
   214	        crossGateTimestep: av.crossGateTimestep,
   215	        audioCrossGateTimestep: av.audioCrossGateTimestep,
   216	        promptTimestep: promptTS,
   217	        audioPromptTimestep: av.audioPromptTimestep)
   218	    }
   219	
   220	    // ---- Outputs ----
   221	    let videoOut = processOutput(x: vx, embeddedTimestep: vEmbTS)
   222	    let audioOut = processAudioOutput(ax, embeddedTimestep: av.audioEmbeddedTimestep)
   223	    return (videoOut, audioOut)
   224	  }
   225	
   226	  /// Audio output processing: LayerNorm (no affine, eps 1e-6) ->
   227	  /// table+embedded modulation (row 0 shift, row 1 scale) -> proj_out ->
   228	  /// unpatchify to `(B, 8, T, 16)`. Requires `hasAudio`.
   325	    // Local: standard base=10000 (rope_local_base_freq)
   326	    // Global: base=10000 with linear scaling factor 8 (rope_scaling.factor)
   327	    self.rotaryEmbLocal = Gemma3RoPE(
   328	      dim: config.headDim,
   329	      base: 10_000.0,
   330	      scaleFactor: 1.0
   331	    )
   332	    self.rotaryEmbGlobal = Gemma3RoPE(
   333	      dim: config.headDim,
   334	      base: 10_000.0,
   335	      scaleFactor: 8.0  // rope_scaling.factor from config
   336	    )
   337	  }
   338	
   339	  /// Forward pass through the full Gemma 3 model.
   340	  ///
   341	  /// - Parameters:
   342	  ///   - inputIds: Token IDs `[B, S]`.
   343	  ///   - attentionMask: Padding mask `[B, S]` (1 = attend, 0 = pad).
   344	  ///   - outputHiddenStates: When true, returns all 49 hidden states.
   345	  /// - Returns: Array of hidden states `[embedding, layer0, ..., layer47]` where
   346	  ///   the final entry has the final RMSNorm applied. Each tensor is `[B, S, 3840]`.
   347	  public func callAsFunction(
   348	    inputIds: MLXArray,
   349	    attentionMask: MLXArray,
   350	    outputHiddenStates: Bool = true
   351	  ) -> [MLXArray] {
   352	    let seqLen = inputIds.dim(1)
   353	
   354	    // Token embedding with Gemma scaling (sqrt(hidden_size))
   355	    var h = embedTokens(inputIds)
   356	    let scaleFactor = MLXArray(Float(config.hiddenSize).squareRoot()).asType(h.dtype)
   357	    h = h * scaleFactor
   358	    eval(h)
   359	
   360	    // Build causal + padding attention mask
   361	    let fullCausalMask = LTX2GemmaModel.buildCausalMask(
   362	      seqLen: seqLen,
   363	      attentionMask: attentionMask,
   364	      dtype: h.dtype
   365	    )
   366	
   367	    // Pre-compute both RoPE variants
   368	    let globalCosSin = rotaryEmbGlobal(seqLen)
   369	    let localCosSin = rotaryEmbLocal(seqLen)
   370	
   371	    // Collect hidden states
   372	    var hiddenStatesList: [MLXArray] = outputHiddenStates ? [h] : []
   373	
   374	    let numLayers = layers.count
   375	    for (i, layer) in layers.enumerated() {
   376	      // Gemma 3 sliding window pattern:
   377	      // Every 6th layer (indices 5, 11, 17, ...) uses full attention + global RoPE
   378	      // All other layers use sliding window attention + local RoPE
   379	      let isGlobal = (i % config.slidingWindowPattern == config.slidingWindowPattern - 1)
   380	      let cosSin = isGlobal ? globalCosSin : localCosSin
   381	
   382	      h = layer(
   383	        hiddenStates: h,
   384	        attentionMask: fullCausalMask,
   385	        cosSin: cosSin
   386	      )
   387	      eval(h)
   388	
   389	      if outputHiddenStates && i < numLayers - 1 {
   390	        hiddenStatesList.append(h)
   391	      }
   392	    }
   393	
   394	    // Apply final RMSNorm
   395	    h = norm(h)
   396	    eval(h)
   397	
   398	    if outputHiddenStates {
   399	      hiddenStatesList.append(h)
   400	      return hiddenStatesList
   401	    }
   402	
   403	    return [h]
   404	  }
   405	
   406	  // MARK: - Attention Mask
   407	
   408	  /// Build a 4D causal + padding attention mask.
   409	  public static func buildCausalMask(
   410	    seqLen: Int,
   411	    attentionMask: MLXArray,
   412	    dtype: DType
   413	  ) -> MLXArray {
   414	    let batchSize = attentionMask.dim(0)
   415	    let minVal: Float = (dtype == .float16 || dtype == .bfloat16) ? -65504.0 : -1e9
   416	    let maskDtype: DType = .float32
   417	
   418	    // Padding mask
   419	    let keepMask = attentionMask .== MLXArray(1).asType(attentionMask.dtype)
   420	    let padOnes = MLX.zeros(attentionMask.shape, dtype: maskDtype)
   421	    let padNegInf = MLX.full(attentionMask.shape, values: MLXArray(minVal), dtype: maskDtype)
   422	    var paddingMask = MLX.where(keepMask, padOnes, padNegInf)
   423	    paddingMask = paddingMask.expandedDimensions(axis: 1).expandedDimensions(axis: 1)
   424	
   425	    // Causal mask
   426	    let idx = MLXArray(0..<seqLen).asType(.int32)
   427	    let j = idx.expandedDimensions(axis: 0)
   428	    let i = idx.expandedDimensions(axis: 1)
   429	    let triBool = j .> i
   430	    let zeros2D = MLX.zeros([seqLen, seqLen], dtype: maskDtype)
   431	    let negInf2D = MLX.full([seqLen, seqLen], values: MLXArray(minVal), dtype: maskDtype)
   432	    var causalMask = MLX.where(triBool, negInf2D, zeros2D)
   433	    causalMask = causalMask.expandedDimensions(axis: 0).expandedDimensions(axis: 0)
   434	    causalMask = MLX.broadcast(causalMask, to: [batchSize, 1, seqLen, seqLen])
   435	
   436	    return (causalMask + paddingMask).asType(dtype)
   437	  }
   438	
   439	  // MARK: - Weight Loading
   440	
   441	  /// Sanitize weight keys from safetensors format.

   443	    var sanitized: [String: MLXArray] = [:]
   444	    for (key, value) in weights {
   445	      var newKey: String? = nil

   154	  /// Assemble the effective description for a content mode.

   158	  public func resolvedDescription(for mode: ContentModeManager.Mode = .neutral) -> String {

   162	    if mode == .avocado, let avocado, !avocado.isEmpty { parts.append(avocado) }

   164	  }

   390	
   391	/**
   392	 * Read the SCENE description embedded in a rendered PNG — the image's own
   393	 * generation prompt, stored in the EXIF ImageDescription tag (0x010e) that
   394	 * ComfyBox writes (also visible in Finder as the image Description).
   395	 *
   396	 * Todd 2026-07-14: i2v starts on the real frame but a GENERIC motion prompt
   397	 * makes LTX re-imagine a stock bedroom mid-clip. Feeding the source frame's
   398	 * actual scene back into the motion prompt keeps the video in the real scene.
   399	 *
   400	 * Zero-dep: walks PNG chunks to the `eXIf` chunk, parses the TIFF/IFD, returns
   401	 * the ImageDescription string. Returns undefined for remote/non-PNG/unreadable
   402	 * sources or if the tag is absent — callers fall back to the raw motion prompt.
   403	 */
   404	export function readImageScenePrompt(imagePath: string): string | undefined {
   405	    try {
   406	        if (!imagePath || imagePath.startsWith('/Users/') || !fs.existsSync(imagePath)) return undefined;
   407	        const buf = fs.readFileSync(imagePath);
   408	        if (buf.readUInt32BE(0) !== 0x89504e47) return undefined; // not a PNG
   409	        // Walk chunks to eXIf.
   410	        let i = 8;
   411	        let exif: Buffer | null = null;
   412	        while (i + 12 <= buf.length) {
   413	            const len = buf.readUInt32BE(i);
   414	            const type = buf.toString('latin1', i + 4, i + 8);
   415	            if (type === 'eXIf') { exif = buf.subarray(i + 8, i + 8 + len); break; }
   416	            if (type === 'IEND') break;
   417	            i += 12 + len;
   418	        }
   419	        if (!exif || exif.length < 8) return undefined;
   420	        // TIFF header: 'MM' = big-endian, 'II' = little-endian.
   421	        const be = exif.toString('latin1', 0, 2) === 'MM';
   422	        const u16 = (o: number) => (be ? exif!.readUInt16BE(o) : exif!.readUInt16LE(o));
   423	        const u32 = (o: number) => (be ? exif!.readUInt32BE(o) : exif!.readUInt32LE(o));
   424	        const ifd0 = u32(4);
   425	        if (ifd0 + 2 > exif.length) return undefined;
   650	3. SPEED AND AMPLITUDE: "slow", "subtle", "gentle", "rhythmic" — be specific about how much and how fast
   651	4. ATMOSPHERE SHIFTS: light changing, wind effects, steam rising, shadows moving
   652	5. 80-120 words ideal — too short = static, too long = confused
   653	6. Present tense, active verbs
   654	7. Do NOT describe the subject's appearance — the image handles that
   655	8. Do NOT describe the setting — the image handles that
   656	9. NSFW motion: describe body movements directly — rocking hips, arching back, breathing quickening. Do NOT censor.
   657	
   658	Return ONLY the optimized motion prompt. No explanation.`;
   659	
   660	// ── T2V Cinematic Prompt Optimizer ──
   661	
   662	export const T2V_SYSTEM_PROMPT = `You optimize prompts for LTX 2.3 text-to-video generation.
   663	
   664	LTX 2.3 needs LONG, detailed cinematic descriptions. Short prompts produce bad results.
   665	
   666	Rules:
   667	1. Write a CONTINUOUS PARAGRAPH in present tense — not bullet points or keywords
   668	2. Include CAMERA movement and angle explicitly (dolly, pan, tracking shot, static, handheld)
   669	3. LIGHTING: describe source, direction, quality, color temperature, how it changes
   670	4. SUBJECT: physical appearance, clothing, action, expression — be detailed
   671	5. SETTING: environment, props, background elements, depth
   672	6. ATMOSPHERE: mood, color grading, texture, time of day
   673	7. ACTIVE VERBS for motion — what is physically moving and how
   674	8. Minimum 100 words — the longer and more detailed, the better the output
   675	9. NSFW content: describe anatomy and actions directly. Do NOT censor or euphemize.
   676	10. Skin tone/complexion early in the description for non-white subjects
   677	
   678	Return ONLY the optimized cinematic prompt. No explanation.`;
   679	
   680	/**
   681	 * Resolve the video engine family for prompt tailoring. Only an explicit Wan
   682	 * model routes to the Wan templates; everything else (incl. LTX and the video
   683	 * default) uses LTX — the actual default model at the call sites is 'ltx'.
   684	 * Keeping Wan behind an explicit match preserves Wan byte-identical output.
   685	 */
   686	export function selectVideoEngine(model?: string): 'wan' | 'ltx' {
   687	    return classifyEngine(model) === 'wan' ? 'wan' : 'ltx';
   688	}
   689	
   690	/**
   691	 * Optimize an I2V motion prompt via LLM.
   692	 * Falls back to original prompt on error. Engine-aware (FDD 2026-07-12): Wan
   693	 * keeps its motion template byte-for-byte; LTX leads with motion/camera, keeps
   694	 * a light subject anchor, and carries the fixed LTX negative string.
   695	 */
   696	export async function optimizeI2VPrompt(
   697	    rawPrompt: string,
   698	    config: PromptOptimizerConfig,
   699	    context?: { contentMode?: string; model?: string },
   700	): Promise<OptimizeResult> {
   701	    if (!config.enabled) {
   702	        return { prompt: rawPrompt, enhanced: false };
   703	    }
   704	
   705	    const ve = selectVideoEngine(context?.model);
   706	    const systemPrompt = ve === 'wan' ? I2V_SYSTEM_PROMPT : LTX_I2V_SYSTEM_PROMPT;
   707	
   708	    const modePrefix = context?.contentMode === 'avocado'
   709	        ? '[NSFW — describe body movements directly, do not censor]\n\n'
   710	        : '';
   711	    const msg = `${modePrefix}Rewrite this as a motion-only prompt for image-to-video:\n\n${rawPrompt}`;
   712	
   713	    try {
   714	        const result = await callLLMWithSystem(config, systemPrompt, msg);
   715	        if (result) {
   716	            const cleaned = cleanLLMOutput(result);
   717	            if (cleaned.length > 20) {
   718	                return ve === 'wan'
   719	                    ? { prompt: cleaned, enhanced: true }
   720	                    : { prompt: cleaned, enhanced: true, negativePrompt: LTX_FIXED_NEGATIVE };
   721	            }
   722	        }
   723	    } catch (err) {
   724	        console.warn('[prompt-optimizer] I2V optimization failed:', err instanceof Error ? err.message : err);
   725	    }
   726	
   727	    return { prompt: rawPrompt, enhanced: false };
   728	}

   305	
   306	  ## EXPLICIT MODE — HARDCORE
   307	
   308	  GRAPHIC adult content — pornographic. Full nudity, sex acts, anatomy. Use direct anatomical language — NEVER euphemism. Default to NUDE when no clothing specified. Default to available-light amateur aesthetic, not studio.
   309

   311	
   312	  Return ONLY the rewritten prompt in YOUR CONTEXT: / YOUR PHOTO: format. No labels beyond those two headers, no markdown, no explanation.
   313	  """
   314	
   315	  // MARK: - LTX-2 video prompts (cinematic prose + motion, NOT YOUR CONTEXT/YOUR PHOTO)
   316	
   317	  // Aligned to the official LTX-2.3 prompt guide (ltx.io/blog/ltx-2-3-prompt-guide):
   318	  // long detailed cinematic shot descriptions, physical performance cues, camera
   319	  // vocabulary, length scaled to clip duration.
   320	  static let ltxRules = """
   321	  ## LTX-2.3 VIDEO RULES (per the official + community LTX-2 prompt guides)
   322	
   323	  You rewrite prompts for LTX-2.3, a text-to-VIDEO model. Write a detailed cinematic
   324	  SHOT DESCRIPTION as flowing natural prose, one paragraph, PRESENT TENSE, no labels.
   325	
   326	  MOTION MUST BE NATURAL AND COHERENT. Describe the action as ONE smooth SEQUENCE that
   327	  flows from beginning to end — a real, physically-plausible movement, NOT a frantic
   328	  pile of verbs. The subject moves deliberately and continuously; the camera makes ONE
   329	  clean move. Avoid BOTH failure modes: a static/posed subject (frozen clip) AND a
   330	  chaotic spray of rapid actions (spastic, jittery clip).
   331	
   332	  1. Write the core action as a natural sequence — beginning, middle, end — and say how it
   333	     RESOLVES (where the subject and camera end up) so the model knows how to finish the
   334	     motion. e.g. "she walks forward, slows, and turns to face the camera," NOT "she spins
   335	     jumps twirls leaps whips."
   336	  2. Motion is smooth and grounded in real physics — weight, momentum, follow-through. Hair
   337	     and fabric move WITH the body. Prefer deliberate and flowing over fast and frantic.
   338	  3. ONE clean, readable camera move, stated plainly: "slow dolly in," "handheld tracking,"
   339	     "the camera pans right to reveal…," "orbits slowly." NOT rapid whip pans, crash zooms,
   340	     or several conflicting moves at once.
   341	  4. Direct performance with PHYSICAL cues, not emotional labels — posture, gesture, facial
   342	     nuance; keep it subtle. LTX excels at nuanced single-subject motion.
   343	  5. Then add ENVIRONMENT, LIGHTING, and lens/film feel briefly (golden hour, 85mm, shallow
   344	     depth of field, low angle). LIGHTING RULE: the subject must be WELL-LIT with the face
   345	     clearly visible — state it explicitly ("warm light illuminating her face and body").
   346	     NEVER stage the subject as a backlit silhouette against a bright sky unless the user
   347	     explicitly asks for a silhouette.
   348	  6. If a character description is given, treat it as canonical — never contradict it. Name
   349	     the character AT MOST ONCE, pronouns after.
   350	  7. Longer prompts help on 2.3 — scale length to the clip. Natural prose only. NO
   351	     "YOUR CONTEXT"/"YOUR PHOTO", no markdown, no lists, no preamble.
   352	  8. ANATOMY GROUNDING (anti-extra-limb — from the reference author's staging style): give
   353	     every VISIBLE hand and limb an explicit owner and placement, like stage directions —
   354	     "her left hand slides down her stomach, her right hand grips the sheet" — never an
   355	     unattributed "hands caress" or "a hand reaches." Ungrounded limbs get hallucinated as
   356	     extra ones. When two people are in frame, anchor each person's touching hands/limbs
   357	     to named positions on the other ("his hands on her hips").
   358	  9. FIGURE COUNT — state it explicitly. Solo scene: say she is ALONE in the frame, no one
   359	     else visible, and never imply an off-frame person (no POV hands, no "someone"). Multi-
   360	     person: state the exact count and each person's position relative to the others.
   361	  """

   399	  // Image-to-video: the subject, appearance, wardrobe, setting, and lighting ALREADY

   404	  subject, their appearance, clothing, the setting, and the lighting. You add ONLY the
   405	  MOTION that brings that image to life.
   406	
   407	  {{LTX_RULES}}
   408	
   409	  ## IMAGE-TO-VIDEO — MOTION ONLY
   410	  CRITICAL: Do NOT re-describe the subject's looks, body, clothing, or the environment —
   411	  those come from the image and any contradiction corrupts the result. Describe ONLY:
   412	  how the subject moves (one natural, physically-grounded sequence), one clean camera
   413	  move if any, and motion-relevant detail (hair/fabric sway, ambient movement). Keep it
   414	  concise — motion direction, not a scene. If a content mode is set, the motion may be
   415	  explicit, but still describe only movement, never appearance.
   416	
   417	  ## OUTPUT
   418	  Return ONLY the motion description as a single short flowing paragraph.
   419	  """
   420	
   421	  /// An optimizer that refuses must NEVER have its refusal rendered as the
   422	  /// prompt — the author's own workflow shipped a frame where the enhancer's
   423	  /// "I am programmed to be a safe and ethical AI assistant…" text was the
   424	  /// conditioning. Heuristic markers; false positives just fall back to the
   425	  /// raw prompt, which is always safe.
   426	  static func looksLikeRefusal(_ text: String) -> Bool {
   427	    let lowered = text.lowercased()
   428	    let markers = [
   429	      "i cannot fulfill", "i can't fulfill", "i cannot create", "i can't create",
   430	      "i cannot assist", "i can't assist", "i'm sorry, but", "i am sorry, but",
   431	      "i am programmed to be", "safety guidelines", "ethical principles",
   432	      "i'm not able to participate", "as an ai", "i must decline",
   433	    ]
   434	    return markers.contains { lowered.contains($0) }
   435	  }

   437	  static func selectSystemPrompt(contentMode: String, mediaKind: String = "image") -> String {
   438	    // Video constants carry a {{LTX_RULES}} placeholder (task #15 — so a
   439	    // file-level rules override reaches file templates); the legacy path
   440	    // expands it with the builtin rules block.
   441	    func expand(_ t: String) -> String {
   442	      t.replacingOccurrences(of: "{{LTX_RULES}}", with: ltxRules)
   443	    }
   444	    let kind = mediaKind.lowercased()
   445	    if kind == "video-i2v" { return expand(systemPromptVideoI2V) }
   446	    if kind == "video" || kind == "video-t2v" {
   447	      switch contentMode.lowercased() {
   448	      case "avocado": return expand(systemPromptVideoAvocado)
   449	      case "banana": return expand(systemPromptVideoBanana)
   450	      default: return expand(systemPromptVideoNeutral)
   451	      }
   452	    }
   453	    switch contentMode.lowercased() {
   454	    case "avocado": return systemPromptAvocado
   455	    case "banana": return systemPromptBanana
   456	    default: return systemPromptNeutral
   457	    }
   458	  }
   459	
   460	  // MARK: - User Message Builder
    60	    let prefix = (kind == "video" || kind == "video-t2v") ? "video" : "image"
    61	    switch contentMode.lowercased() {
    62	    case "avocado": return "\(prefix)-avocado"
    63	    case "banana": return "\(prefix)-banana"
    64	    default: return "\(prefix)-neutral"
    65	    }
    66	  }
    67	
    68	  public func template(_ id: String) -> Resolved {
    69	    let builtin = Self.builtins[id] ?? ""
    70	
    71	    let fileURL = directory.appendingPathComponent("\(id).md")
    72	    if let raw = try? String(contentsOf: fileURL, encoding: .utf8) {
    73	      let trimmed = raw.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
    74	      if trimmed.isEmpty {
    75	        logger.error("prompt template '\(id)' at \(fileURL.path) is empty — using builtin. Delete the file or fill it in.")
    76	      } else {
    77	        let text = substituteRules(in: raw)
    78	        return Resolved(id: id, text: text, source: .file, hash: Self.hash(text))
    79	      }
    80	    }
    81	
    82	    // Builtin video templates carry the same {{LTX_RULES}} placeholder as
    83	    // file templates, so a rules-file override reaches them too.
    84	    let text = substituteRules(in: builtin)
    85	    return Resolved(id: id, text: text, source: .builtin, hash: Self.hash(text))
    86	  }
    87	
    88	  private func substituteRules(in text: String) -> String {
    89	    guard text.contains(Self.rulesPlaceholder) else { return text }
    90	    let rules: String
    91	    let rulesURL = directory.appendingPathComponent("ltx-rules.md")
    92	    if let raw = try? String(contentsOf: rulesURL, encoding: .utf8),
    93	       !raw.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty {
    94	      rules = raw
    95	    } else {
    96	      rules = PromptOptimizer.ltxRules
    97	    }
    98	    return text.replacingOccurrences(of: Self.rulesPlaceholder, with: rules)
    99	  }
   100	
   101	  private static func hash(_ text: String) -> String {
   102	    let digest = SHA256.hash(data: Data(text.utf8))
   103	    return digest.map { String(format: "%02x", $0) }.joined().prefix(12).lowercased()
   104	  }

  3212	  private func decode<T: Decodable>(_ type: T.Type, from data: Data) throws -> T {
  3213	    let decoder = JSONDecoder()
